#include "car_gate.h"

bool car_gate_motion(bool go, bool emergency_stopped, uint32_t faults)
{
    return go && !emergency_stopped && faults == 0U;
}

bool car_gate_follow(bool go, bool emergency_stopped, uint32_t faults,
                     bool obstacle)
{
    return car_gate_motion(go, emergency_stopped, faults) && !obstacle;
}

bool car_gate_rearm(bool go_rising, bool emergency_stopped, uint32_t faults,
                    bool obstacle)
{
    return go_rising && car_gate_follow(true, emergency_stopped, faults, obstacle);
}

#ifndef CAR_GATE_H
#define CAR_GATE_H

#include <stdbool.h>
#include <stdint.h>

/* These predicates do not check data freshness or clear a motor E-stop.
 * The controller must validate its inputs and handle stops separately. */
bool car_gate_motion(bool go, bool emergency_stopped, uint32_t faults);
bool car_gate_follow(bool go, bool emergency_stopped, uint32_t faults,
                     bool obstacle);
/* Rearms line following only after the motor E-stop is already clear. */
bool car_gate_rearm(bool go_rising, bool emergency_stopped, uint32_t faults,
                    bool obstacle);

#endif

#include "../app.h"
#include "car_gate_test.h"

#if APP_TEST
#include "../test_report.h"
#include "car_gate.h"
#include "car_safety.h"
#include "controller_state.h"
#include "../obstacle/obs_profile_config.h"

static const car_safety_config_t safety_config = {100U, 100U, 100U, 200U, 500U, 300U};

static line_observation_message_t safety_line(uint32_t sequence, uint32_t timestamp,
					      uint8_t detected)
{
	line_observation_message_t message = {0};
	message.header.type = VEHICLE_MESSAGE_LINE_OBSERVATION;
	message.header.version = VEHICLE_MESSAGE_VERSION;
	message.header.sequence = sequence;
	message.header.timestamp_ms = timestamp;
	message.line_detected = detected;
	message.confidence = detected ? 1000U : 0U;
	return message;
}

static imu_estimate_message_t safety_imu(uint32_t sequence, uint32_t timestamp)
{
	imu_estimate_message_t message = {0};
	message.header.type = VEHICLE_MESSAGE_IMU_ESTIMATE;
	message.header.version = VEHICLE_MESSAGE_VERSION;
	message.header.sequence = sequence;
	message.header.timestamp_ms = timestamp;
	message.validity = VEHICLE_IMU_VALID_ROLL_PITCH;
	return message;
}

static obstacle_event_message_t safety_obstacle(uint32_t sequence, uint32_t timestamp,
						 uint8_t type, uint16_t distance)
{
	obstacle_event_message_t message = {0};
	message.header.type = VEHICLE_MESSAGE_OBSTACLE_EVENT;
	message.header.version = VEHICLE_MESSAGE_VERSION;
	message.header.sequence = sequence;
	message.header.timestamp_ms = timestamp;
	message.obstacle_class = type;
	message.distance_mm = distance;
	return message;
}

static BOOL safety_inputs(car_safety_t *safety, uint32_t sequence, uint32_t now)
{
	line_observation_message_t line = safety_line(sequence, now, 1U);
	imu_estimate_message_t imu = safety_imu(sequence, now);
	obstacle_event_message_t obstacle =
		safety_obstacle(sequence, now, VEHICLE_OBSTACLE_AHEAD_READING, OBS_DIST_NONE);
	return car_safety_line(safety, &line, now) && car_safety_imu(safety, &imu, now) &&
	       car_safety_obstacle(safety, &obstacle, now);
}

static BOOL safety_rearm_test(void)
{
	car_safety_t safety = {0};
	line_observation_message_t line;
	imu_estimate_message_t imu;
	obstacle_event_message_t obstacle;
	BOOL ok;

	ok = car_safety_init(&safety, &safety_config, 100U) &&
	     safety_inputs(&safety, 1U, 100U) &&
	     !car_safety_follow(&safety, 100U, true, false, false) &&
	     car_safety_follow(&safety, 100U, true, true, false);
	line = safety_line(2U, 101U, 0U);
	ok = car_safety_line(&safety, &line, 101U) &&
	     !car_safety_follow(&safety, 101U, true, false, false) && ok;
	line = safety_line(3U, 102U, 1U);
	ok = car_safety_line(&safety, &line, 102U) &&
	     !car_safety_follow(&safety, 102U, true, false, false) &&
	     car_safety_follow(&safety, 102U, true, true, false) && ok;
	obstacle = safety_obstacle(2U, 103U, VEHICLE_OBSTACLE_AHEAD_READING, 300U);
	ok = car_safety_obstacle(&safety, &obstacle, 103U) &&
	     !car_safety_follow(&safety, 103U, true, false, false) && ok;
	obstacle = safety_obstacle(3U, 104U, VEHICLE_OBSTACLE_AHEAD_READING, OBS_DIST_NONE);
	ok = car_safety_obstacle(&safety, &obstacle, 104U) &&
	     !car_safety_follow(&safety, 104U, true, false, false) &&
	     car_safety_follow(&safety, 104U, true, true, false) && ok;
	imu = safety_imu(2U, 105U);
	imu.tilt_detected = 1U;
	ok = car_safety_imu(&safety, &imu, 105U) &&
	     !car_safety_follow(&safety, 105U, true, true, false) && ok;
	imu = safety_imu(3U, 106U);
	ok = car_safety_imu(&safety, &imu, 106U) &&
	     !car_safety_follow(&safety, 106U, true, false, false) &&
	     car_safety_follow(&safety, 106U, true, true, false) && ok;
	return ok;
}

static BOOL safety_scan_test(void)
{
	car_safety_t safety = {0};
	line_observation_message_t line;
	imu_estimate_message_t imu;
	obstacle_event_message_t obstacle;
	BOOL ok;

	ok = car_safety_init(&safety, &safety_config, 100U) &&
	     safety_inputs(&safety, 1U, 100U) &&
	     car_safety_follow(&safety, 100U, true, true, false) &&
	     car_safety_begin_scan(&safety, 110U) &&
	     !car_safety_follow(&safety, 110U, true, true, false);
	line = safety_line(2U, 180U, 1U);
	imu = safety_imu(2U, 180U);
	ok = car_safety_line(&safety, &line, 180U) && car_safety_imu(&safety, &imu, 180U) && ok;
	line = safety_line(3U, 230U, 1U);
	imu = safety_imu(3U, 230U);
	ok = car_safety_line(&safety, &line, 230U) && car_safety_imu(&safety, &imu, 230U) &&
	     !car_safety_follow(&safety, 230U, true, true, false) && safety.faults == 0U && ok;
	obstacle = safety_obstacle(2U, 240U, VEHICLE_OBSTACLE_SCAN_CLEAR, OBS_DIST_NONE);
	ok = car_safety_obstacle(&safety, &obstacle, 240U) &&
	     !car_safety_follow(&safety, 240U, true, true, false) && ok;
	obstacle = safety_obstacle(3U, 245U, VEHICLE_OBSTACLE_AHEAD_READING, OBS_DIST_NONE);
	ok = car_safety_obstacle(&safety, &obstacle, 245U) &&
	     !car_safety_follow(&safety, 245U, true, false, false) &&
	     car_safety_follow(&safety, 245U, true, true, false) && ok;
	return ok;
}

static BOOL safety_fault_test(void)
{
	car_safety_t safety = {0};
	car_safety_config_t config = safety_config;
	line_observation_message_t line;
	obstacle_event_message_t obstacle;
	BOOL ok;

	ok = car_safety_init(&safety, &config, 100U) &&
	     safety_inputs(&safety, 1U, 100U);
	line = safety_line(3U, 101U, 1U);
	ok = !car_safety_line(&safety, &line, 101U) &&
	     (safety.faults & CAR_SAFETY_FAULT_LINE) != 0U &&
	     !car_safety_follow(&safety, 101U, true, true, false) && ok;
	ok = car_safety_init(&safety, &config, 100U) &&
	     safety_inputs(&safety, 1U, 100U) && ok;
	line = safety_line(2U, 201U, 1U);
	ok = !car_safety_line(&safety, &line, 201U) &&
	     (safety.faults & CAR_SAFETY_FAULT_LINE) != 0U && ok;
	ok = car_safety_init(&safety, &config, 100U) &&
	     safety_inputs(&safety, 1U, 100U) && ok;
	obstacle = safety_obstacle(2U, 101U, VEHICLE_OBSTACLE_AHEAD_READING, 100U);
	obstacle.emergency = 1U;
	ok = !car_safety_obstacle(&safety, &obstacle, 101U) &&
	     (safety.faults & CAR_SAFETY_FAULT_EMERGENCY) != 0U &&
	     !car_safety_follow(&safety, 101U, true, true, false) && ok;
	config.line_max_age_ms = 1000U;
	config.imu_max_age_ms = 1000U;
	ok = car_safety_init(&safety, &config, 100U) &&
	     safety_inputs(&safety, 1U, 100U) &&
	     car_safety_begin_scan(&safety, 100U) && ok;
	obstacle = safety_obstacle(2U, 301U, VEHICLE_OBSTACLE_SCAN_CLEAR, OBS_DIST_NONE);
	ok = !car_safety_obstacle(&safety, &obstacle, 301U) &&
	     (safety.faults & CAR_SAFETY_FAULT_OBSTACLE) != 0U && ok;
	ok = car_safety_init(&safety, &config, 100U) &&
	     safety_inputs(&safety, 1U, 100U) && car_safety_begin_scan(&safety, 110U) && ok;
	obstacle = safety_obstacle(2U, 109U, VEHICLE_OBSTACLE_SCAN_CLEAR, OBS_DIST_NONE);
	ok = !car_safety_obstacle(&safety, &obstacle, 110U) &&
	     (safety.faults & CAR_SAFETY_FAULT_OBSTACLE) != 0U && ok;
	ok = car_safety_init(&safety, &config, 100U) &&
	     safety_inputs(&safety, 1U, 100U) && car_safety_begin_scan(&safety, 110U) && ok;
	obstacle = safety_obstacle(2U, 111U, VEHICLE_OBSTACLE_SCAN_CLEAR, OBS_DIST_NONE);
	ok = car_safety_obstacle(&safety, &obstacle, 120U) && ok;
	obstacle = safety_obstacle(3U, 119U, VEHICLE_OBSTACLE_AHEAD_READING, OBS_DIST_NONE);
	ok = !car_safety_obstacle(&safety, &obstacle, 120U) &&
	     (safety.faults & CAR_SAFETY_FAULT_OBSTACLE) != 0U && ok;
	config.ahead_max_age_ms = 0U;
	ok = !car_safety_init(&safety, &config, 400U) &&
	     !car_safety_follow(&safety, 400U, true, true, false) && ok;
	return ok;
}

static BOOL safety_wrap_test(void)
{
	car_safety_t safety = {0};
	line_observation_message_t line;
	BOOL ok;

	ok = car_safety_init(&safety, &safety_config, UINT32_MAX - 10U) &&
	     safety_inputs(&safety, UINT32_MAX, UINT32_MAX - 10U) &&
	     car_safety_follow(&safety, UINT32_MAX - 10U, true, true, false);
	line = safety_line(0U, 5U, 1U);
	ok = car_safety_line(&safety, &line, 5U) &&
	     car_safety_follow(&safety, 5U, true, false, false) && ok;
	ok = !car_safety_follow(&safety, 4U, true, false, false) &&
	     (safety.faults & CAR_SAFETY_FAULT_CLOCK) != 0U && ok;
	return ok;
}

static BOOL controller_state_flow_test(void)
{
	controller_state_t controller = {0};
	controller_state_t startup = {0};
	line_observation_message_t line;
	obstacle_event_message_t obstacle;
	BOOL ok;

	ok = controller_state_init(&controller, &safety_config, 100U) &&
	     !controller_state_init(&controller, &safety_config, 100U) &&
	     safety_inputs(&controller.safety, 1U, 100U) &&
	     controller_state_step(&controller, 100U, true, false) == CONTROLLER_BLOCKED &&
	     controller_state_step(&controller, 100U, false, false) == CONTROLLER_WAITING &&
	     controller_state_step(&controller, 100U, true, false) == CONTROLLER_FOLLOWING;
	line = safety_line(2U, 101U, 0U);
	ok = car_safety_line(&controller.safety, &line, 101U) &&
	     controller_state_step(&controller, 101U, true, false) == CONTROLLER_BLOCKED && ok;
	line = safety_line(3U, 102U, 1U);
	ok = car_safety_line(&controller.safety, &line, 102U) &&
	     controller_state_step(&controller, 102U, true, false) == CONTROLLER_BLOCKED &&
	     controller_state_step(&controller, 102U, false, false) == CONTROLLER_WAITING &&
	     controller_state_step(&controller, 102U, true, false) == CONTROLLER_FOLLOWING && ok;
	obstacle = safety_obstacle(2U, 103U, VEHICLE_OBSTACLE_AHEAD_READING, 300U);
	ok = car_safety_obstacle(&controller.safety, &obstacle, 103U) &&
	     controller_state_step(&controller, 103U, true, false) == CONTROLLER_BLOCKED && ok;
	obstacle = safety_obstacle(3U, 104U, VEHICLE_OBSTACLE_AHEAD_READING, OBS_DIST_NONE);
	ok = car_safety_obstacle(&controller.safety, &obstacle, 104U) &&
	     controller_state_step(&controller, 104U, true, false) == CONTROLLER_BLOCKED &&
	     controller_state_step(&controller, 104U, false, false) == CONTROLLER_WAITING &&
	     controller_state_step(&controller, 104U, true, false) == CONTROLLER_FOLLOWING && ok;
	ok = controller_state_init(&startup, &safety_config, 200U) &&
	     controller_state_step(&startup, 200U, false, false) == CONTROLLER_WAITING &&
	     controller_state_step(&startup, 200U, true, false) == CONTROLLER_BLOCKED &&
	     safety_inputs(&startup.safety, 1U, 201U) &&
	     controller_state_step(&startup, 201U, true, false) == CONTROLLER_BLOCKED &&
	     controller_state_step(&startup, 201U, false, false) == CONTROLLER_WAITING &&
	     controller_state_step(&startup, 201U, true, false) == CONTROLLER_FOLLOWING && ok;
	return ok;
}

static BOOL controller_state_fault_test(void)
{
	controller_state_t controller = {0};
	controller_state_t stale = {0};
	controller_state_t wrap = {0};
	controller_state_t bad = {0};
	car_safety_config_t invalid = safety_config;
	line_observation_message_t line;
	BOOL ok;

	invalid.line_max_age_ms = 0U;
	ok = controller_state_step(NULL, 0U, false, false) == CONTROLLER_FAULTED &&
	     controller_state_step(&controller, 0U, false, false) == CONTROLLER_FAULTED &&
	     !controller_state_init(&bad, &invalid, 100U) &&
	     controller_state_step(&bad, 100U, false, false) == CONTROLLER_FAULTED &&
	     controller_state_init(&controller, &safety_config, 100U) &&
	     safety_inputs(&controller.safety, 1U, 100U) &&
	     controller_state_step(&controller, 100U, false, false) == CONTROLLER_WAITING &&
	     controller_state_step(&controller, 100U, true, false) == CONTROLLER_FOLLOWING &&
	     controller_state_step(&controller, 101U, true, true) == CONTROLLER_FAULTED &&
	     controller_state_step(&controller, 102U, false, false) == CONTROLLER_FAULTED &&
	     !controller_state_init(&controller, &safety_config, 102U);
	ok = controller_state_init(&stale, &safety_config, 100U) &&
	     safety_inputs(&stale.safety, 1U, 100U) &&
	     controller_state_step(&stale, 201U, false, false) == CONTROLLER_FAULTED && ok;
	line = safety_line(3U, 101U, 1U);
	ok = controller_state_init(&bad, &safety_config, 100U) &&
	     safety_inputs(&bad.safety, 1U, 100U) &&
	     !car_safety_line(&bad.safety, &line, 101U) &&
	     controller_state_step(&bad, 101U, true, false) == CONTROLLER_FAULTED && ok;
	ok = controller_state_init(&wrap, &safety_config, UINT32_MAX - 5U) &&
	     safety_inputs(&wrap.safety, UINT32_MAX, UINT32_MAX - 5U) &&
	     controller_state_step(&wrap, UINT32_MAX - 5U, false, false) == CONTROLLER_WAITING &&
	     controller_state_step(&wrap, 3U, true, false) == CONTROLLER_FOLLOWING &&
	     controller_state_step(&wrap, 2U, true, false) == CONTROLLER_FAULTED && ok;
	return ok;
}

static BOOL controller_state_scan_test(void)
{
	controller_state_t controller = {0};
	obstacle_event_message_t obstacle;
	BOOL ok;

	ok = controller_state_init(&controller, &safety_config, 100U) &&
	     safety_inputs(&controller.safety, 1U, 100U) &&
	     controller_state_step(&controller, 100U, false, false) == CONTROLLER_WAITING &&
	     car_safety_begin_scan(&controller.safety, 101U) &&
	     controller_state_step(&controller, 101U, true, false) == CONTROLLER_SCANNING;
	obstacle = safety_obstacle(2U, 102U, VEHICLE_OBSTACLE_SCAN_CLEAR, OBS_DIST_NONE);
	ok = car_safety_obstacle(&controller.safety, &obstacle, 102U) &&
	     controller_state_step(&controller, 102U, true, false) == CONTROLLER_BLOCKED && ok;
	obstacle = safety_obstacle(3U, 103U, VEHICLE_OBSTACLE_AHEAD_READING, OBS_DIST_NONE);
	ok = car_safety_obstacle(&controller.safety, &obstacle, 103U) &&
	     controller_state_step(&controller, 103U, true, false) == CONTROLLER_BLOCKED &&
	     controller_state_step(&controller, 103U, false, false) == CONTROLLER_WAITING &&
	     controller_state_step(&controller, 103U, true, false) == CONTROLLER_FOLLOWING && ok;
	return ok;
}

BOOL car_gate_test(void)
{
	BOOL motion_ok;
	BOOL follow_ok;
	BOOL rearm_ok;
	BOOL safety_ok;
	BOOL scan_ok;
	BOOL fault_ok;
	BOOL wrap_ok;
	BOOL state_flow_ok;
	BOOL state_fault_ok;
	BOOL state_scan_ok;
	TEST_INIT();

	motion_ok = car_gate_motion(true, false, 0U) &&
		    !car_gate_motion(false, false, 0U) &&
		    !car_gate_motion(true, true, 0U) &&
		    !car_gate_motion(true, false, 1U) &&
		    !car_gate_motion(true, false, 1UL << 31);

	follow_ok = car_gate_follow(true, false, 0U, false) &&
		    !car_gate_follow(true, false, 0U, true) &&
		    !car_gate_follow(false, false, 0U, false) &&
		    !car_gate_follow(true, true, 0U, false) &&
		    !car_gate_follow(true, false, 1U, false) &&
		    car_gate_motion(true, false, 0U);

	rearm_ok = car_gate_rearm(true, false, 0U, false) &&
		   !car_gate_rearm(false, false, 0U, false) &&
		   !car_gate_rearm(true, true, 0U, false) &&
		   !car_gate_rearm(true, false, 1U, false) &&
		   !car_gate_rearm(true, false, 0U, true);
	safety_ok = safety_rearm_test();
	scan_ok = safety_scan_test();
	fault_ok = safety_fault_test();
	wrap_ok = safety_wrap_test();
	state_flow_ok = controller_state_flow_test();
	state_fault_ok = controller_state_fault_test();
	state_scan_ok = controller_state_scan_test();

	TEST_CHECK("VC-U01", motion_ok, "stop, emergency and fault veto motion");
	TEST_CHECK("VC-U02", follow_ok, "obstacle vetoes line following");
	TEST_CHECK("VC-U03", rearm_ok, "line following requires a fresh go edge");
	TEST_CHECK("VC-U04", safety_ok, "sensor vetoes require a fresh go edge");
	TEST_CHECK("VC-U05", scan_ok, "scan requires fresh ahead data before rearming");
	TEST_CHECK("VC-U06", fault_ok, "invalid, late and emergency inputs latch faults");
	TEST_CHECK("VC-U07", wrap_ok, "sensor and clock wrap with backward clock rejection");
	TEST_CHECK("VC-U10", state_flow_ok, "state transitions and fresh go edges");
	TEST_CHECK("VC-U11", state_fault_ok, "state faults, emergency latch and clock wrap");
	TEST_CHECK("VC-U12", state_scan_ok, "scan state and ahead reacquisition");
	TEST_SUMMARY("controller gate");
	return motion_ok && follow_ok && rearm_ok && safety_ok && scan_ok && fault_ok &&
	       wrap_ok && state_flow_ok && state_fault_ok && state_scan_ok;
}

#include "car_safety.h"
#include "car_gate.h"
#include "../obstacle/obs_profile_config.h"

#include <stddef.h>

static bool interval_valid(uint32_t value)
{
	return value != 0U && value <= INT32_MAX;
}

static bool fail(car_safety_t *safety, uint32_t fault)
{
	safety->faults |= fault;
	safety->armed = false;
	return false;
}

static bool clock_valid(car_safety_t *safety, uint32_t now)
{
	if (now - safety->last_clock_ms > INT32_MAX)
		return fail(safety, CAR_SAFETY_FAULT_CLOCK);
	safety->last_clock_ms = now;
	return true;
}

static bool check_deadlines(car_safety_t *safety, uint32_t now)
{
	if (now - (safety->line.have_message ? safety->line.timestamp_ms :
		   safety->line_wait_ms) > safety->config.line_max_age_ms)
		(void)fail(safety, CAR_SAFETY_FAULT_LINE);
	if (now - (safety->imu.have_message ? safety->imu.timestamp_ms :
		   safety->imu_wait_ms) > safety->config.imu_max_age_ms)
		(void)fail(safety, CAR_SAFETY_FAULT_IMU);
	if (safety->scanning) {
		if (now - safety->scan_start_ms > safety->config.scan_timeout_ms)
			(void)fail(safety, CAR_SAFETY_FAULT_OBSTACLE);
	} else if (now - (safety->have_ahead ? safety->obstacle.timestamp_ms :
			  safety->ahead_wait_ms) > safety->config.ahead_max_age_ms) {
		(void)fail(safety, CAR_SAFETY_FAULT_OBSTACLE);
	}
	return safety->faults == 0U;
}

static bool accept(car_safety_t *safety, car_safety_stream_t *stream,
		   const vehicle_message_header_t *header, uint16_t type,
		   uint32_t now, uint32_t max_age, uint32_t not_before, uint32_t fault)
{
	if (header == NULL || header->type != type ||
	    header->version != VEHICLE_MESSAGE_VERSION ||
	    now - header->timestamp_ms > max_age ||
	    (!stream->have_message && header->timestamp_ms - not_before > INT32_MAX) ||
	    (stream->have_message &&
	     (header->sequence - stream->sequence != 1U ||
	      header->timestamp_ms - stream->timestamp_ms > INT32_MAX)))
		return fail(safety, fault);
	stream->sequence = header->sequence;
	stream->timestamp_ms = header->timestamp_ms;
	stream->have_message = true;
	return true;
}

bool car_safety_init(car_safety_t *safety, const car_safety_config_t *config,
		     uint32_t now_ms)
{
	car_safety_t initial = {0};

	if (safety == NULL) return false;
	if (config == NULL ||
	    !interval_valid(config->line_max_age_ms) ||
	    !interval_valid(config->imu_max_age_ms) ||
	    !interval_valid(config->ahead_max_age_ms) ||
	    !interval_valid(config->scan_timeout_ms) ||
	    config->min_line_confidence > 1000U ||
	    config->obstacle_stop_mm < OBS_MIN_RANGE_MM ||
	    config->obstacle_stop_mm > OBS_MAX_RANGE_MM) {
		*safety = (car_safety_t){.faults = CAR_SAFETY_FAULT_EXTERNAL};
		return false;
	}
	initial.config = *config;
	initial.last_clock_ms = now_ms;
	initial.line_wait_ms = now_ms;
	initial.imu_wait_ms = now_ms;
	initial.ahead_wait_ms = now_ms;
	initial.initialized = true;
	*safety = initial;
	return true;
}

bool car_safety_line(car_safety_t *safety, const line_observation_message_t *message,
		     uint32_t now_ms)
{
	if (safety == NULL || !safety->initialized) return false;
	if (!clock_valid(safety, now_ms) || !check_deadlines(safety, now_ms)) return false;
	if (message == NULL || message->line_detected > 1U ||
	    message->intersection_detected > 1U || message->confidence > 1000U)
		return fail(safety, CAR_SAFETY_FAULT_LINE);
	if (!accept(safety, &safety->line, &message->header, VEHICLE_MESSAGE_LINE_OBSERVATION,
		    now_ms, safety->config.line_max_age_ms, safety->line_wait_ms,
		    CAR_SAFETY_FAULT_LINE))
		return false;
	safety->line_clear = message->line_detected == 1U &&
			     message->confidence >= safety->config.min_line_confidence;
	if (!safety->line_clear) safety->armed = false;
	return true;
}

bool car_safety_imu(car_safety_t *safety, const imu_estimate_message_t *message,
		    uint32_t now_ms)
{
	if (safety == NULL || !safety->initialized) return false;
	if (!clock_valid(safety, now_ms) || !check_deadlines(safety, now_ms)) return false;
	if (message == NULL || !(message->validity & VEHICLE_IMU_VALID_ROLL_PITCH) ||
	    message->shock_detected > 1U || message->tilt_detected > 1U)
		return fail(safety, CAR_SAFETY_FAULT_IMU);
	if (!accept(safety, &safety->imu, &message->header, VEHICLE_MESSAGE_IMU_ESTIMATE,
		    now_ms, safety->config.imu_max_age_ms, safety->imu_wait_ms,
		    CAR_SAFETY_FAULT_IMU))
		return false;
	safety->imu_clear = !message->shock_detected && !message->tilt_detected;
	if (!safety->imu_clear) safety->armed = false;
	return true;
}

bool car_safety_obstacle(car_safety_t *safety, const obstacle_event_message_t *message,
			 uint32_t now_ms)
{
	uint32_t max_age;

	if (safety == NULL || !safety->initialized) return false;
	if (!clock_valid(safety, now_ms) || !check_deadlines(safety, now_ms)) return false;
	if (message == NULL || message->emergency > 1U ||
	    (message->distance_mm != OBS_DIST_NONE &&
	     (message->distance_mm < OBS_MIN_RANGE_MM || message->distance_mm > OBS_MAX_RANGE_MM)))
		return fail(safety, CAR_SAFETY_FAULT_OBSTACLE);
	max_age = safety->scanning ? safety->config.scan_timeout_ms :
				    safety->config.ahead_max_age_ms;
	if (!accept(safety, &safety->obstacle, &message->header, VEHICLE_MESSAGE_OBSTACLE_EVENT,
		    now_ms, max_age, safety->ahead_wait_ms, CAR_SAFETY_FAULT_OBSTACLE))
		return false;
	if (message->emergency) return fail(safety, CAR_SAFETY_FAULT_EMERGENCY);
	if (message->obstacle_class == VEHICLE_OBSTACLE_AHEAD_READING && !safety->scanning) {
		if (!safety->have_ahead &&
		    message->header.timestamp_ms - safety->ahead_wait_ms > INT32_MAX)
			return fail(safety, CAR_SAFETY_FAULT_OBSTACLE);
		safety->have_ahead = true;
		safety->ahead_clear = message->distance_mm == OBS_DIST_NONE ||
				      message->distance_mm > safety->config.obstacle_stop_mm;
		if (!safety->ahead_clear) safety->armed = false;
		return true;
	}
	if (safety->scanning &&
	    (message->obstacle_class == VEHICLE_OBSTACLE_SCAN_CLEAR ||
	     message->obstacle_class == VEHICLE_OBSTACLE_SCAN_FOUND)) {
		if (message->header.timestamp_ms - safety->scan_start_ms > INT32_MAX)
			return fail(safety, CAR_SAFETY_FAULT_OBSTACLE);
		safety->scanning = false;
		safety->have_ahead = false;
		safety->ahead_clear = false;
		safety->ahead_wait_ms = now_ms;
		safety->armed = false;
		return true;
	}
	return fail(safety, CAR_SAFETY_FAULT_OBSTACLE);
}

bool car_safety_begin_scan(car_safety_t *safety, uint32_t now_ms)
{
	if (safety == NULL || !safety->initialized) return false;
	if (!clock_valid(safety, now_ms) || !check_deadlines(safety, now_ms)) return false;
	safety->armed = false;
	if (safety->faults != 0U || safety->scanning || !safety->have_ahead ||
	    now_ms - safety->obstacle.timestamp_ms > safety->config.ahead_max_age_ms)
		return false;
	safety->scanning = true;
	safety->scan_start_ms = now_ms;
	safety->have_ahead = false;
	safety->ahead_clear = false;
	return true;
}

void car_safety_latch_fault(car_safety_t *safety)
{
	if (safety == NULL || !safety->initialized) return;
	(void)fail(safety, CAR_SAFETY_FAULT_EXTERNAL);
}

bool car_safety_follow(car_safety_t *safety, uint32_t now_ms,
		       bool go, bool go_rising, bool emergency_stopped)
{
	bool blocked;

	if (safety == NULL || !safety->initialized) return false;
	if (!clock_valid(safety, now_ms)) return false;
	(void)check_deadlines(safety, now_ms);
	if (emergency_stopped) (void)fail(safety, CAR_SAFETY_FAULT_EMERGENCY);
	blocked = safety->scanning || !safety->line.have_message || !safety->line_clear ||
		  !safety->imu.have_message || !safety->imu_clear ||
		  !safety->have_ahead || !safety->ahead_clear;
	if (!car_gate_follow(go, emergency_stopped, safety->faults, blocked)) {
		safety->armed = false;
	} else if (!safety->armed &&
		   car_gate_rearm(go_rising, emergency_stopped, safety->faults, blocked)) {
		safety->armed = true;
	}
	return safety->armed;
}
=== C:\Users\Yong Jun\school\INF2004-Embedded_Systems\project\INF2004_Project\app_program\controller\car_safety.h ===
#ifndef CAR_SAFETY_H
#define CAR_SAFETY_H

#include "../vehicle_messages.h"

#define CAR_SAFETY_FAULT_LINE      0x01U
#define CAR_SAFETY_FAULT_IMU       0x02U
#define CAR_SAFETY_FAULT_OBSTACLE  0x04U
#define CAR_SAFETY_FAULT_CLOCK     0x08U
#define CAR_SAFETY_FAULT_EXTERNAL  0x10U
#define CAR_SAFETY_FAULT_EMERGENCY 0x20U

typedef struct {
	uint32_t line_max_age_ms;
	uint32_t imu_max_age_ms;
	uint32_t ahead_max_age_ms;
	uint32_t scan_timeout_ms;
	uint16_t min_line_confidence;
	uint16_t obstacle_stop_mm;
} car_safety_config_t;

typedef struct {
	uint32_t sequence;
	uint32_t timestamp_ms;
	bool have_message;
} car_safety_stream_t;

typedef struct {
	car_safety_config_t config;
	car_safety_stream_t line;
	car_safety_stream_t imu;
	car_safety_stream_t obstacle;
	uint32_t last_clock_ms;
	uint32_t line_wait_ms;
	uint32_t imu_wait_ms;
	uint32_t ahead_wait_ms;
	uint32_t scan_start_ms;
	uint32_t faults;
	bool initialized;
	bool line_clear;
	bool imu_clear;
	bool ahead_clear;
	bool have_ahead;
	bool scanning;
	bool armed;
} car_safety_t;

/* Single controller owner only. Configuration and thresholds must be verified
 * for the robot. Faults latch until reinitialization after system restart. */
bool car_safety_init(car_safety_t *safety, const car_safety_config_t *config,
		     uint32_t now_ms);
bool car_safety_line(car_safety_t *safety, const line_observation_message_t *message,
		     uint32_t now_ms);
bool car_safety_imu(car_safety_t *safety, const imu_estimate_message_t *message,
		    uint32_t now_ms);
bool car_safety_obstacle(car_safety_t *safety, const obstacle_event_message_t *message,
			 uint32_t now_ms);

/* Call only after independently confirming the physical robot stopped, before
 * requesting a scan. Motion must stay stopped until a fresh ahead reading.
 * The helper cannot itself stop or verify the motors. */
bool car_safety_begin_scan(car_safety_t *safety, uint32_t now_ms);

/* Latch a received subsystem fault or an independently detected stop fault. */
void car_safety_latch_fault(car_safety_t *safety);

/* Call periodically even when no messages arrive. A fresh go edge is needed
 * after any stop or unsafe observation. This is only an advisory predicate:
 * it does not send commands, verify a motor stop, or replace a watchdog. */
bool car_safety_follow(car_safety_t *safety, uint32_t now_ms,
		       bool go, bool go_rising, bool emergency_stopped);

=== C:\Users\Yong Jun\school\INF2004-Embedded_Systems\project\INF2004_Project\app_program\controller\controller_monitor.c ===
#include "../app.h"
#include "../vehicle_buffers.h"
#include "controller_monitor.h"

#define CONTROLLER_MONITOR_PRIORITY 10
#define CONTROLLER_MONITOR_FAULT_DELAY_MS 1000U

static controller_monitor_config_t s_config;
static controller_state_t s_controller;
static bool s_started;

static bool read_clock(car_safety_t *safety, uint32_t *now)
{
	SYSTIM time;
	ER er = tk_get_otm(&time);
	if (er != E_OK) {
		tm_printf((UB*)"controller monitor clock failed: %d\n", (int)er);
		safety->faults |= CAR_SAFETY_FAULT_CLOCK;
		safety->armed = false;
		return false;
	}
	*now = time.lo;
	return true;
}

static bool receive_faults(car_safety_t *safety)
{
	fault_message_t fault;
	INT received = tk_rcv_mbf(g_mbf_faults, &fault, TMO_POL);
	if (received == E_TMOUT) return true;
	if (received != (INT)sizeof(fault) ||
	    fault.header.type != VEHICLE_MESSAGE_FAULT ||
	    fault.header.version != VEHICLE_MESSAGE_VERSION ||
	    fault.source < VEHICLE_FAULT_SENSOR || fault.source > VEHICLE_FAULT_SYSTEM ||
	    fault.recoverable > 1U || fault.code == 0U) {
		tm_printf((UB*)"controller monitor invalid fault message: %d\n", (int)received);
	} else {
		tm_printf((UB*)"controller monitor subsystem fault: source=%u code=%u\n",
			  (unsigned)fault.source, (unsigned)fault.code);
	}
	car_safety_latch_fault(safety);
	return false;
}

static bool receive_line(car_safety_t *safety)
{
	unsigned i;
	for (i = 0U; i < VEHICLE_MB_LINE_CAPACITY; i++) {
		line_observation_message_t message;
		uint32_t now;
		INT received = tk_rcv_mbf(g_mbf_line_observations, &message, TMO_POL);
		if (received == E_TMOUT) return true;
		if (received != (INT)sizeof(message)) {
			tm_printf((UB*)"controller monitor line receive failed: %d\n", (int)received);
			car_safety_latch_fault(safety);
			return false;
		}
		if (!read_clock(safety, &now) || !car_safety_line(safety, &message, now)) return false;
	}
	return true;
}

static bool receive_imu(car_safety_t *safety)
{
	unsigned i;
	for (i = 0U; i < VEHICLE_MB_IMU_CAPACITY; i++) {
		imu_estimate_message_t message;
		uint32_t now;
		INT received = tk_rcv_mbf(g_mbf_imu_estimates, &message, TMO_POL);
		if (received == E_TMOUT) return true;
		if (received != (INT)sizeof(message)) {
			tm_printf((UB*)"controller monitor IMU receive failed: %d\n", (int)received);
			car_safety_latch_fault(safety);
			return false;
		}
		if (!read_clock(safety, &now) || !car_safety_imu(safety, &message, now)) return false;
	}
	return true;
}

static bool receive_obstacle(car_safety_t *safety)
{
	unsigned i;
	for (i = 0U; i < VEHICLE_MB_OBSTACLE_CAPACITY; i++) {
		obstacle_event_message_t message;
		uint32_t now;
		INT received = tk_rcv_mbf(g_mbf_obstacle_events, &message, TMO_POL);
		if (received == E_TMOUT) return true;
		if (received != (INT)sizeof(message)) {
			tm_printf((UB*)"controller monitor obstacle receive failed: %d\n", (int)received);
			car_safety_latch_fault(safety);
			return false;
		}
		if (!read_clock(safety, &now) || !car_safety_obstacle(safety, &message, now))
			return false;
	}
	return true;
}

static bool monitor_cycle(controller_state_t *controller)
{
	car_safety_t *safety = &controller->safety;
	uint32_t now = safety->last_clock_ms;

	if (!receive_faults(safety) || !receive_line(safety) || !receive_imu(safety) ||
	    !receive_obstacle(safety) || !read_clock(safety, &now)) {
		(void)controller_state_step(controller, safety->last_clock_ms, false, false);
		return false;
	}
	return controller_state_step(controller, now, false, false) != CONTROLLER_FAULTED;
}

static void monitor_stopped(void)
{
	tm_printf((UB*)"controller monitor stopped; safety faults=0x%x\n",
		  (unsigned)s_controller.safety.faults);
	for (;;) {
		ER er = tk_dly_tsk(CONTROLLER_MONITOR_FAULT_DELAY_MS);
		if (er != E_OK) {
			tm_printf((UB*)"controller monitor delay failed: %d\n", (int)er);
			er = tk_slp_tsk(TMO_FEVR);
			tm_printf((UB*)"controller monitor sleep returned: %d\n", (int)er);
		}
	}
}

static void controller_monitor_task(INT stacd, void *exinf)
{
	ER er;

	(void)stacd;
	(void)exinf;
	for (;;) {
		if (!monitor_cycle(&s_controller)) monitor_stopped();
		er = tk_dly_tsk(s_config.period_ms);
		if (er != E_OK) {
			tm_printf((UB*)"controller monitor schedule failed: %d\n", (int)er);
			car_safety_latch_fault(&s_controller.safety);
			(void)controller_state_step(&s_controller, s_controller.safety.last_clock_ms,
						    false, false);
			monitor_stopped();
		}
	}
}

ER controller_monitor_start(const controller_monitor_config_t *config)
{
	controller_state_t probe = {0};
	controller_state_t validation = {0};
	T_CTSK task = {0};
	SYSTIM now;
	ID id;
	ER er;

	if (s_started) return E_OBJ;
	if (config == NULL || config->period_ms == 0U ||
	    config->period_ms > config->safety.line_max_age_ms ||
	    config->period_ms > config->safety.imu_max_age_ms ||
	    config->period_ms > config->safety.ahead_max_age_ms ||
	    !controller_state_init(&probe, &config->safety, 0U))
		return E_PAR;
	if (g_mbf_line_observations <= 0 || g_mbf_imu_estimates <= 0 ||
	    g_mbf_obstacle_events <= 0 || g_mbf_faults <= 0)
		return E_OBJ;
	er = tk_get_otm(&now);
	if (er != E_OK) return er < E_OK ? er : E_SYS;
	if (!controller_state_init(&validation, &config->safety, now.lo)) return E_PAR;
	s_config = *config;
	s_controller = validation;
	task.task = (FP)controller_monitor_task;
	task.tskatr = TA_HLNG | TA_RNG3;
	task.itskpri = CONTROLLER_MONITOR_PRIORITY;
	task.stksz = TASK_STACK_SIZE;
	id = tk_cre_tsk(&task);
	if (id < E_OK) return id;
	s_started = true;
	er = tk_sta_tsk(id, 0);
	if (er != E_OK) {
		ER cleanup = tk_del_tsk(id);
		if (cleanup != E_OK)
			tm_printf((UB*)"controller monitor task cleanup failed: %d\n", (int)cleanup);
		else s_started = false;
		return er < E_OK ? er : E_SYS;
	}
	return E_OK;
}

#if APP_TEST
#include "../test_report.h"
#include "../obstacle/obs_profile_config.h"

static BOOL drain_test_buffer(ID id)
{
	union {
		line_observation_message_t line;
		imu_estimate_message_t imu;
		obstacle_event_message_t obstacle;
		fault_message_t fault;
	} message;
	INT received;
	do {
		received = tk_rcv_mbf(id, &message, TMO_POL);
	} while (received > 0);
	return received == E_TMOUT;
}

static BOOL drain_test_buffers(void)
{
	BOOL ok = drain_test_buffer(g_mbf_line_observations);
	ok = drain_test_buffer(g_mbf_imu_estimates) && ok;
	ok = drain_test_buffer(g_mbf_obstacle_events) && ok;
	ok = drain_test_buffer(g_mbf_faults) && ok;
	return ok;
}

static const car_safety_config_t test_config = {10000U, 10000U, 10000U, 10000U, 500U, 300U};

static BOOL monitor_flow_test(void)
{
	controller_state_t controller = {0};
	car_safety_t *safety = &controller.safety;
	line_observation_message_t line = {0};
	imu_estimate_message_t imu = {0};
	obstacle_event_message_t obstacle = {0};
	uint32_t now;
	BOOL ok;

	if (!read_clock(safety, &now) || !controller_state_init(&controller, &test_config, now))
		return FALSE;
	line.header = (vehicle_message_header_t){VEHICLE_MESSAGE_LINE_OBSERVATION,
						  VEHICLE_MESSAGE_VERSION, 1U, now};
	line.line_detected = 1U;
	line.confidence = 1000U;
	imu.header = (vehicle_message_header_t){VEHICLE_MESSAGE_IMU_ESTIMATE,
						 VEHICLE_MESSAGE_VERSION, 1U, now};
	imu.validity = VEHICLE_IMU_VALID_ROLL_PITCH;
	obstacle.header = (vehicle_message_header_t){VEHICLE_MESSAGE_OBSTACLE_EVENT,
						      VEHICLE_MESSAGE_VERSION, 1U, now};
	obstacle.obstacle_class = VEHICLE_OBSTACLE_AHEAD_READING;
	obstacle.distance_mm = OBS_DIST_NONE;
	ok = tk_snd_mbf(g_mbf_line_observations, &line, (INT)sizeof(line), TMO_POL) == E_OK;
	ok = tk_snd_mbf(g_mbf_imu_estimates, &imu, (INT)sizeof(imu), TMO_POL) == E_OK && ok;
	ok = tk_snd_mbf(g_mbf_obstacle_events, &obstacle, (INT)sizeof(obstacle), TMO_POL) ==
	     E_OK && ok;
	ok = monitor_cycle(&controller) && safety->line.have_message && safety->imu.have_message &&
	     safety->have_ahead && safety->faults == 0U && !safety->armed &&
	     controller.state == CONTROLLER_WAITING && ok;
	ok = monitor_cycle(&controller) && !safety->armed &&
	     controller.state == CONTROLLER_WAITING && ok;
	return drain_test_buffers() && ok;
}

static BOOL monitor_fault_test(void)
{
	controller_state_t controller = {0};
	car_safety_t *safety = &controller.safety;
	car_safety_config_t config = test_config;
	line_observation_message_t line = {0};
	fault_message_t fault = {0};
	uint32_t now;
	BOOL ok;

	if (!read_clock(safety, &now) || !controller_state_init(&controller, &config, now))
		return FALSE;
	line.header = (vehicle_message_header_t){VEHICLE_MESSAGE_LINE_OBSERVATION,
						  VEHICLE_MESSAGE_VERSION, 1U, now};
	line.line_detected = 1U;
	line.confidence = 1000U;
	ok = tk_snd_mbf(g_mbf_line_observations, &line, (INT)sizeof(line) - 1, TMO_POL) == E_OK;
	ok = !monitor_cycle(&controller) && (safety->faults & CAR_SAFETY_FAULT_EXTERNAL) != 0U &&
	     controller.state == CONTROLLER_FAULTED && ok;
	controller = (controller_state_t){0};
	ok = controller_state_init(&controller, &config, now) && ok;
	ok = tk_snd_mbf(g_mbf_line_observations, &line, (INT)sizeof(line), TMO_POL) == E_OK && ok;
	line.header.sequence = 3U;
	ok = tk_snd_mbf(g_mbf_line_observations, &line, (INT)sizeof(line), TMO_POL) == E_OK && ok;
	ok = !monitor_cycle(&controller) && (safety->faults & CAR_SAFETY_FAULT_LINE) != 0U &&
	     controller.state == CONTROLLER_FAULTED && ok;
	controller = (controller_state_t){0};
	ok = controller_state_init(&controller, &config, now) && ok;
	fault.header.type = VEHICLE_MESSAGE_FAULT;
	fault.header.version = VEHICLE_MESSAGE_VERSION;
	fault.source = VEHICLE_FAULT_SENSOR;
	fault.code = VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_IMU_SENSOR;
	ok = tk_snd_mbf(g_mbf_faults, &fault, (INT)sizeof(fault), TMO_POL) == E_OK && ok;
	ok = !monitor_cycle(&controller) && (safety->faults & CAR_SAFETY_FAULT_EXTERNAL) != 0U &&
	     controller.state == CONTROLLER_FAULTED && ok;
	config.line_max_age_ms = 10U;
	config.imu_max_age_ms = 10U;
	config.ahead_max_age_ms = 10U;
	controller = (controller_state_t){0};
	ok = controller_state_init(&controller, &config, now - 11U) && ok;
	ok = !monitor_cycle(&controller) && (safety->faults & CAR_SAFETY_FAULT_LINE) != 0U &&
	     controller.state == CONTROLLER_FAULTED && ok;
	return drain_test_buffers() && ok;
}

BOOL controller_monitor_test(void)
{
	BOOL flow_ok;
	BOOL fault_ok;
	TEST_INIT();

	flow_ok = monitor_flow_test();
	fault_ok = monitor_fault_test();
	TEST_CHECK("VC-U08", flow_ok, "controller consumes copied sensor messages while waiting");
	TEST_CHECK("VC-U09", fault_ok, "invalid, missing and fault messages enter faulted state");
	TEST_SUMMARY("controller monitor");
	return flow_ok && fault_ok;
}

#ifndef CONTROLLER_MONITOR_H
#define CONTROLLER_MONITOR_H

#include <tk/tkernel.h>
#include "controller_state.h"

typedef struct {
	car_safety_config_t safety;
	RELTIM period_ms;
} controller_monitor_config_t;

/* Opt-in diagnostic only; exclusively consumes the controller's line, IMU,
 * obstacle and fault buffers. Start before their producers, not alongside
 * another consumer. It never issues motor, scan, or network commands and
 * cannot stop or verify the physical robot. It steps the advisory controller
 * state with go held low, so it cannot arm. Keep motor power safely disabled.
 * Only forward obstacle observations are supported; do not request scans.
 * Faults latch until restart and are reported on the console. */
ER controller_monitor_start(const controller_monitor_config_t *config);

#if APP_TEST
BOOL controller_monitor_test(void);

=== C:\Users\Yong Jun\school\INF2004-Embedded_Systems\project\INF2004_Project\app_program\controller\controller_state.c ===
#include "controller_state.h"

#include <stddef.h>

bool controller_state_init(controller_state_t *controller,
			   const car_safety_config_t *config, uint32_t now_ms)
{
	controller_state_t initial = {0};

	if (controller == NULL || controller->initialized ||
	    !car_safety_init(&initial.safety, config, now_ms))
		return false;
	initial.previous_go = true;
	initial.initialized = true;
	*controller = initial;
	return true;
}

controller_state_value_t controller_state_step(controller_state_t *controller,
						uint32_t now_ms, bool go,
						bool emergency_stopped)
{
	bool rising, following;

	if (controller == NULL || !controller->initialized) return CONTROLLER_FAULTED;
	rising = go && !controller->previous_go;
	controller->previous_go = go;
	following = car_safety_follow(&controller->safety, now_ms, go, rising,
				      emergency_stopped);
	if (controller->safety.faults != 0U)
		controller->state = CONTROLLER_FAULTED;
	else if (controller->safety.scanning)
		controller->state = CONTROLLER_SCANNING;
	else if (following)
		controller->state = CONTROLLER_FOLLOWING;
	else if (go)
		controller->state = CONTROLLER_BLOCKED;
	else
		controller->state = CONTROLLER_WAITING;
	return controller->state;
}

#ifndef CONTROLLER_STATE_H
#define CONTROLLER_STATE_H

#include "car_safety.h"

typedef enum {
	CONTROLLER_WAITING = 0,
	CONTROLLER_FOLLOWING,
	CONTROLLER_BLOCKED,
	CONTROLLER_SCANNING,
	CONTROLLER_FAULTED
} controller_state_value_t;

typedef struct {
	car_safety_t safety;
	controller_state_value_t state;
	bool previous_go;
	bool initialized;
} controller_state_t;

/* Single controller owner only. Initialize a zeroed object once; do not reset
 * it during operation. The owner feeds observations into safety and calls
 * controller_state_step every cycle, including when no messages arrive. */
bool controller_state_init(controller_state_t *controller,
			   const car_safety_config_t *config, uint32_t now_ms);

/* Advisory simulation only: go must be a current trusted local input, not a
 * decoded network command. A go level already high at startup cannot arm.
 * The returned state does not send commands, stop motors, request scans or
 * confirm a physical stop; do not use it as an independent safety system. */
controller_state_value_t controller_state_step(controller_state_t *controller,
						uint32_t now_ms, bool go,
						bool emergency_stopped);

#endif

#include "line_steering.h"
#include "../line_barcode/line_observation.h"

#include <stddef.h>

#define FORWARD_PWM 25
#define CORRECTION_PWM 10
#define LOST_CONTINUE_MS 300U

void line_steering_reset(line_steering_t *steering)
{
	if (steering == NULL) return;
	*steering = (line_steering_t){0};
}

line_steering_command_t line_steering_update(
	line_steering_t *steering, uint8_t position, uint32_t now_ms)
{
	line_steering_command_t stop = {0, 0, true};
	line_steering_command_t command = {0, 0, false};

	if (steering == NULL || steering->stop_latched) return stop;

	if (position == POS_LOST) {
		if (!steering->line_lost) {
			steering->line_lost = true;
			steering->lost_since_ms = now_ms;
		}
		if ((uint32_t)(now_ms - steering->lost_since_ms) >= LOST_CONTINUE_MS) {
			steering->stop_latched = true;
			return stop;
		}
		command.motor_1_pwm = steering->last_motor_1_pwm;
		command.motor_2_pwm = steering->last_motor_2_pwm;
		return command;
	}

	steering->line_lost = false;
	switch (position) {
	case POS_JUNCTION:
		command.motor_1_pwm = FORWARD_PWM;
		command.motor_2_pwm = -FORWARD_PWM;
		break;
	case POS_LEFT:
		command.motor_1_pwm = FORWARD_PWM - CORRECTION_PWM;
		command.motor_2_pwm = -FORWARD_PWM;
		break;
	case POS_RIGHT:
		command.motor_1_pwm = FORWARD_PWM;
		command.motor_2_pwm = -(FORWARD_PWM - CORRECTION_PWM);
		break;
	default:
		steering->stop_latched = true;
		return stop;
	}

	steering->last_motor_1_pwm = command.motor_1_pwm;
	steering->last_motor_2_pwm = command.motor_2_pwm;
	return command;
}

#include "../app.h"
#include "line_steering_test.h"

#if APP_TEST
#include "../test_report.h"
#include "../line_barcode/line_observation.h"
#include "line_steering.h"

BOOL line_steering_test(void)
{
	line_steering_t steering = {0};
	line_steering_command_t command;
	BOOL positions_ok;
	BOOL lost_line_ok;
	BOOL latch_ok;
	BOOL invalid_position_ok;
	TEST_INIT();

	line_steering_reset(&steering);
	command = line_steering_update(&steering, POS_JUNCTION, 1000U);
	positions_ok = command.motor_1_pwm == 25 &&
		       command.motor_2_pwm == -25 && !command.stop_latched;
	command = line_steering_update(&steering, POS_LEFT, 1010U);
	positions_ok = command.motor_1_pwm == 15 &&
		       command.motor_2_pwm == -25 && !command.stop_latched &&
		       positions_ok;
	command = line_steering_update(&steering, POS_RIGHT, 1020U);
	positions_ok = command.motor_1_pwm == 25 &&
		       command.motor_2_pwm == -15 && !command.stop_latched &&
		       positions_ok;
	TEST_CHECK("VC-U18", positions_ok,
		   "junction, left and right positions preserve source PWM decisions");

	command = line_steering_update(&steering, POS_LOST, 1030U);
	lost_line_ok = command.motor_1_pwm == 25 &&
		       command.motor_2_pwm == -15 && !command.stop_latched;
	command = line_steering_update(&steering, POS_LOST, 1329U);
	lost_line_ok = !command.stop_latched && lost_line_ok;
	command = line_steering_update(&steering, POS_LOST, 1330U);
	lost_line_ok = command.stop_latched && command.motor_1_pwm == 0 &&
		       command.motor_2_pwm == 0 && lost_line_ok;
	TEST_CHECK("VC-U19", lost_line_ok,
		   "lost-line hold expires at 300 ms with a zero-output latch");

	command = line_steering_update(&steering, POS_JUNCTION, 1400U);
	latch_ok = command.stop_latched && command.motor_1_pwm == 0 &&
		   command.motor_2_pwm == 0;
	line_steering_reset(&steering);
	command = line_steering_update(&steering, POS_JUNCTION, 1410U);
	latch_ok = !command.stop_latched && command.motor_1_pwm == 25 &&
		   command.motor_2_pwm == -25 && latch_ok;
	TEST_CHECK("VC-U20", latch_ok,
		   "stop latch persists until explicit reset");

	line_steering_reset(&steering);
	command = line_steering_update(&steering, UINT8_MAX, 1420U);
	invalid_position_ok = command.stop_latched &&
			      command.motor_1_pwm == 0 &&
			      command.motor_2_pwm == 0;
	command = line_steering_update(&steering, POS_JUNCTION, 1430U);
	invalid_position_ok = command.stop_latched &&
			      command.motor_1_pwm == 0 &&
			      command.motor_2_pwm == 0 && invalid_position_ok;
	TEST_CHECK("VC-U21", invalid_position_ok,
		   "invalid line positions fail closed until reset");

	TEST_SUMMARY("line steering");
	return positions_ok && lost_line_ok && latch_ok && invalid_position_ok;
}

=== C:\Users\Yong Jun\school\INF2004-Embedded_Systems\project\INF2004_Project\app_program\controller\vehicle_controller_task.c ===
#include "../app.h"
#include "../vehicle_buffers.h"
#include "vehicle_controller_task.h"

#define VEHICLE_CONTROLLER_PRIORITY 10

typedef struct {
	controller_state_t controller;
	uint32_t motion_sequence;
	uint32_t status_wait_ms;
	uint32_t status_sequence;
	uint32_t status_timestamp_ms;
	motion_status_message_t motion_status;
	bool arm_requested;
	bool emergency_sent;
	bool have_motion_status;
} vehicle_controller_context_t;

static vehicle_controller_task_config_t s_config;
static vehicle_controller_context_t s_context;
static bool s_started;

static bool publish_motion_command(vehicle_controller_context_t *context,
				   uint8_t mode, uint8_t stop_reason,
				   uint32_t now)
{
	motion_command_message_t message = {0};
	INT result;

	message.header.type = VEHICLE_MESSAGE_MOTION_COMMAND;
	message.header.version = VEHICLE_MESSAGE_VERSION;
	message.header.sequence = context->motion_sequence + 1U;
	message.header.timestamp_ms = now;
	message.mode = mode;
	message.stop_reason = stop_reason;
	result = tk_snd_mbf(g_mbf_motion_commands, &message,
			    (INT)sizeof(message), TMO_POL);
	if (result != E_OK) {
		tm_printf((UB*)"vehicle controller command delivery failed: %d\n",
			  (int)result);
		return false;
	}
	context->motion_sequence = message.header.sequence;
	return true;
}

static bool receive_line(vehicle_controller_context_t *context, uint32_t now)
{
	unsigned i;

	for (i = 0U; i < VEHICLE_MB_LINE_CAPACITY; i++) {
		line_observation_message_t message;
		INT received = tk_rcv_mbf(g_mbf_line_observations, &message, TMO_POL);

		if (received == E_TMOUT) return true;
		if (received != (INT)sizeof(message)) {
			tm_printf((UB*)"vehicle controller line receive failed: %d\n",
				  (int)received);
			car_safety_latch_fault(&context->controller.safety);
			return false;
		}
		if (!car_safety_line(&context->controller.safety, &message, now))
			return false;
	}
	return true;
}

static bool receive_imu(vehicle_controller_context_t *context, uint32_t now)
{
	unsigned i;

	for (i = 0U; i < VEHICLE_MB_IMU_CAPACITY; i++) {
		imu_estimate_message_t message;
		INT received = tk_rcv_mbf(g_mbf_imu_estimates, &message, TMO_POL);

		if (received == E_TMOUT) return true;
		if (received != (INT)sizeof(message)) {
			tm_printf((UB*)"vehicle controller IMU receive failed: %d\n",
				  (int)received);
			car_safety_latch_fault(&context->controller.safety);
			return false;
		}
		if (!car_safety_imu(&context->controller.safety, &message, now))
			return false;
	}
	return true;
}

static bool receive_obstacle(vehicle_controller_context_t *context, uint32_t now)
{
	unsigned i;

	for (i = 0U; i < VEHICLE_MB_OBSTACLE_CAPACITY; i++) {
		obstacle_event_message_t message;
		INT received = tk_rcv_mbf(g_mbf_obstacle_events, &message, TMO_POL);

		if (received == E_TMOUT) return true;
		if (received != (INT)sizeof(message)) {
			tm_printf((UB*)"vehicle controller obstacle receive failed: %d\n",
				  (int)received);
			car_safety_latch_fault(&context->controller.safety);
			return false;
		}
		if (!car_safety_obstacle(&context->controller.safety, &message, now))
			return false;
	}
	return true;
}

static bool receive_faults(vehicle_controller_context_t *context)
{
	for (;;) {
		fault_message_t message;
		INT received = tk_rcv_mbf(g_mbf_faults, &message, TMO_POL);

		if (received == E_TMOUT) return true;
		if (received != (INT)sizeof(message) ||
		    message.header.type != VEHICLE_MESSAGE_FAULT ||
		    message.header.version != VEHICLE_MESSAGE_VERSION ||
		    message.source < VEHICLE_FAULT_SENSOR ||
		    message.source > VEHICLE_FAULT_SYSTEM ||
		    message.recoverable > 1U || message.code == 0U) {
			tm_printf((UB*)"vehicle controller invalid fault message: %d\n",
				  (int)received);
		} else {
			tm_printf((UB*)"vehicle controller subsystem fault: source=%u code=%u\n",
				  (unsigned)message.source, (unsigned)message.code);
		}
		car_safety_latch_fault(&context->controller.safety);
		return false;
	}
}

static bool receive_motion_status(vehicle_controller_context_t *context,
				  uint32_t now)
{
	unsigned i;

	for (i = 0U; i < VEHICLE_MB_STATUS_CAPACITY; i++) {
		motion_status_message_t message;
		uint32_t age;
		uint32_t future;
		INT received = tk_rcv_mbf(g_mbf_motion_status, &message, TMO_POL);

		if (received == E_TMOUT) return true;
		if (received != (INT)sizeof(message) ||
		    message.header.type != VEHICLE_MESSAGE_MOTION_STATUS ||
		    message.header.version != VEHICLE_MESSAGE_VERSION ||
		    message.active > 1U || message.faulted > 1U ||
		    (message.faulted != 0U && message.fault_code == 0U) ||
		    (message.faulted == 0U && message.fault_code != 0U)) {
			tm_printf((UB*)"vehicle controller invalid motion status: %d\n",
				  (int)received);
			car_safety_latch_fault(&context->controller.safety);
			return false;
		}
		age = now - message.header.timestamp_ms;
		future = message.header.timestamp_ms - now;
		if (age > s_config.motion_status_max_age_ms ||
		    (future != 0U && future <= INT32_MAX) ||
		    (context->have_motion_status &&
		     (message.header.sequence - context->status_sequence != 1U ||
		      message.header.timestamp_ms - context->status_timestamp_ms >
			      INT32_MAX))) {
			tm_printf((UB*)"vehicle controller stale or out-of-order motion status\n");
			car_safety_latch_fault(&context->controller.safety);
			return false;
		}
		context->motion_status = message;
		context->status_sequence = message.header.sequence;
		context->status_timestamp_ms = message.header.timestamp_ms;
		context->have_motion_status = true;
		if (message.faulted != 0U || message.active != 0U ||
		    message.motor_1_pwm != 0 || message.motor_2_pwm != 0) {
			tm_printf((UB*)"vehicle controller motion status is not safely idle\n");
			car_safety_latch_fault(&context->controller.safety);
			return false;
		}
	}
	return true;
}

static void latch_controller_fault(vehicle_controller_context_t *context,
				   uint32_t now)
{
	car_safety_latch_fault(&context->controller.safety);
	(void)controller_state_step(&context->controller, now, false, false);
}

static bool controller_cycle(vehicle_controller_context_t *context,
			     uint32_t now,
			     const vehicle_controller_inputs_t *inputs)
{
	controller_state_value_t state;
	bool messages_valid = true;

	if (!receive_faults(context)) messages_valid = false;
	if (!receive_motion_status(context, now)) messages_valid = false;
	if (!receive_line(context, now)) messages_valid = false;
	if (!receive_imu(context, now)) messages_valid = false;
	if (!receive_obstacle(context, now)) messages_valid = false;
	if ((!context->have_motion_status &&
	     now - context->status_wait_ms > s_config.motion_status_max_age_ms) ||
	    (context->have_motion_status &&
	     now - context->status_timestamp_ms > s_config.motion_status_max_age_ms)) {
		tm_printf((UB*)"vehicle controller motion status deadline expired\n");
		messages_valid = false;
	}
	if (!messages_valid) car_safety_latch_fault(&context->controller.safety);

	state = controller_state_step(&context->controller, now,
				      inputs->go, inputs->emergency_stopped);
	if (state == CONTROLLER_FAULTED) {
		if (!context->emergency_sent &&
		    publish_motion_command(context, VEHICLE_MOTION_EMERGENCY_STOP,
					   (uint8_t)context->controller.safety.faults,
					   now))
			context->emergency_sent = true;
		return false;
	}

	if (state == CONTROLLER_FOLLOWING) {
		if (!context->have_motion_status) return true;
		if (!context->arm_requested) {
			if (publish_motion_command(context, VEHICLE_MOTION_ARM, 0U, now)) {
				context->arm_requested = true;
			} else {
				latch_controller_fault(context, now);
				if (publish_motion_command(context,
							   VEHICLE_MOTION_EMERGENCY_STOP,
							   (uint8_t)context->controller.safety.faults,
							   now))
					context->emergency_sent = true;
				return false;
			}
		}
		return true;
	}

	if (context->arm_requested) {
		if (publish_motion_command(context, VEHICLE_MOTION_STOP, 0U, now)) {
			context->arm_requested = false;
		} else {
			latch_controller_fault(context, now);
			if (publish_motion_command(context, VEHICLE_MOTION_EMERGENCY_STOP,
						   (uint8_t)context->controller.safety.faults,
						   now))
				context->emergency_sent = true;
			return false;
		}
	}
	return true;
}

static void vehicle_controller_task(INT stacd, void *exinf)
{
	(void)stacd;
	(void)exinf;
	for (;;) {
		vehicle_controller_inputs_t inputs = {0};
		SYSTIM now;
		ER er = tk_get_otm(&now);

		if (er != E_OK) {
			tm_printf((UB*)"vehicle controller clock failed: %d\n", (int)er);
			latch_controller_fault(&s_context,
					       s_context.controller.safety.last_clock_ms);
			if (!s_context.emergency_sent &&
			    publish_motion_command(&s_context,
						   VEHICLE_MOTION_EMERGENCY_STOP,
						   (uint8_t)s_context.controller.safety.faults,
						   s_context.controller.safety.last_clock_ms))
				s_context.emergency_sent = true;
		} else {
			bool inputs_valid = s_config.read_inputs(s_config.input_context, &inputs);
			if (!inputs_valid) {
				tm_printf((UB*)"vehicle controller local input read failed\n");
				latch_controller_fault(&s_context, now.lo);
			}
			(void)controller_cycle(&s_context, now.lo, &inputs);
		}

		er = tk_dly_tsk(s_config.period_ms);
		if (er != E_OK) {
			tm_printf((UB*)"vehicle controller schedule failed: %d\n", (int)er);
			latch_controller_fault(&s_context,
					       s_context.controller.safety.last_clock_ms);
			if (!s_context.emergency_sent &&
			    publish_motion_command(&s_context,
						   VEHICLE_MOTION_EMERGENCY_STOP,
						   (uint8_t)s_context.controller.safety.faults,
						   s_context.controller.safety.last_clock_ms))
				s_context.emergency_sent = true;
			for (;;) {
				er = tk_slp_tsk(TMO_FEVR);
				if (er != E_OK)
					tm_printf((UB*)"vehicle controller sleep failed: %d\n",
						  (int)er);
			}
		}
	}
}

ER vehicle_controller_task_start(const vehicle_controller_task_config_t *config)
{
	controller_state_t validation = {0};
	controller_state_t initial = {0};
	T_CTSK task = {0};
	SYSTIM now;
	ID id;
	ER er;

	if (s_started) return E_OBJ;
	if (config == NULL || config->read_inputs == NULL ||
	    config->period_ms == 0U ||
	    config->period_ms > VEHICLE_MOTION_STATUS_PERIOD_MS / 2U ||
	    config->motion_status_max_age_ms < 100U ||
	    config->motion_status_max_age_ms > INT32_MAX ||
	    config->period_ms > config->motion_status_max_age_ms ||
	    config->period_ms > config->safety.line_max_age_ms ||
	    config->period_ms > config->safety.imu_max_age_ms ||
	    config->period_ms > config->safety.ahead_max_age_ms ||
	    !controller_state_init(&validation, &config->safety, 0U))
		return E_PAR;
	if (g_mbf_line_observations <= 0 || g_mbf_imu_estimates <= 0 ||
	    g_mbf_obstacle_events <= 0 || g_mbf_faults <= 0 ||
	    g_mbf_motion_commands <= 0 || g_mbf_motion_status <= 0)
		return E_OBJ;
	er = tk_get_otm(&now);
	if (er != E_OK) return er < E_OK ? er : E_SYS;
	if (!controller_state_init(&initial, &config->safety, now.lo))
		return E_PAR;

	s_config = *config;
	s_context = (vehicle_controller_context_t){0};
	s_context.controller = initial;
	s_context.status_wait_ms = now.lo;
	task.task = (FP)vehicle_controller_task;
	task.tskatr = TA_HLNG | TA_RNG3;
	task.itskpri = VEHICLE_CONTROLLER_PRIORITY;
	task.stksz = TASK_STACK_SIZE;
	id = tk_cre_tsk(&task);
	if (id < E_OK) return id;
	s_started = true;
	er = tk_sta_tsk(id, 0);
	if (er != E_OK) {
		ER cleanup = tk_del_tsk(id);
		if (cleanup != E_OK)
			tm_printf((UB*)"vehicle controller cleanup failed: %d\n", (int)cleanup);
		else
			s_started = false;
		return er < E_OK ? er : E_SYS;
	}
	return E_OK;
}

#if APP_TEST
#include "../test_report.h"
#include "../obstacle/obs_profile_config.h"

static BOOL drain_controller_test_buffer(ID id)
{
	union {
		line_observation_message_t line;
		imu_estimate_message_t imu;
		obstacle_event_message_t obstacle;
		fault_message_t fault;
		motion_status_message_t status;
		motion_command_message_t command;
	} message;
	INT received;

	do {
		received = tk_rcv_mbf(id, &message, TMO_POL);
	} while (received > 0);
	return received == E_TMOUT;
}

static BOOL drain_controller_test_buffers(void)
{
	BOOL ok = drain_controller_test_buffer(g_mbf_line_observations);
	ok = drain_controller_test_buffer(g_mbf_imu_estimates) && ok;
	ok = drain_controller_test_buffer(g_mbf_obstacle_events) && ok;
	ok = drain_controller_test_buffer(g_mbf_faults) && ok;
	ok = drain_controller_test_buffer(g_mbf_motion_status) && ok;
	ok = drain_controller_test_buffer(g_mbf_motion_commands) && ok;
	return ok;
}

static BOOL queue_motion_status(uint32_t now, uint32_t sequence,
				uint8_t active, int16_t motor_1, int16_t motor_2)
{
	motion_status_message_t status = {0};

	status.header.type = VEHICLE_MESSAGE_MOTION_STATUS;
	status.header.version = VEHICLE_MESSAGE_VERSION;
	status.header.sequence = sequence;
	status.header.timestamp_ms = now;
	status.active = active;
	status.motor_1_pwm = motor_1;
	status.motor_2_pwm = motor_2;
	return tk_snd_mbf(g_mbf_motion_status, &status, (INT)sizeof(status),
			  TMO_POL) == E_OK;
}

static BOOL queue_safe_sensor_messages(uint32_t now, uint32_t sequence)
{
	line_observation_message_t line = {0};
	imu_estimate_message_t imu = {0};
	obstacle_event_message_t obstacle = {0};
	BOOL ok;

	line.header = (vehicle_message_header_t){VEHICLE_MESSAGE_LINE_OBSERVATION,
						  VEHICLE_MESSAGE_VERSION, sequence, now};
	line.line_detected = 1U;
	line.confidence = 1000U;
	imu.header = (vehicle_message_header_t){VEHICLE_MESSAGE_IMU_ESTIMATE,
						 VEHICLE_MESSAGE_VERSION, sequence, now};
	imu.validity = VEHICLE_IMU_VALID_ROLL_PITCH;
	obstacle.header = (vehicle_message_header_t){VEHICLE_MESSAGE_OBSTACLE_EVENT,
						      VEHICLE_MESSAGE_VERSION, sequence, now};
	obstacle.obstacle_class = VEHICLE_OBSTACLE_AHEAD_READING;
	obstacle.distance_mm = OBS_DIST_NONE;
	ok = tk_snd_mbf(g_mbf_line_observations, &line, (INT)sizeof(line), TMO_POL) == E_OK;
	ok = tk_snd_mbf(g_mbf_imu_estimates, &imu, (INT)sizeof(imu), TMO_POL) == E_OK && ok;
	ok = tk_snd_mbf(g_mbf_obstacle_events, &obstacle, (INT)sizeof(obstacle),
			TMO_POL) == E_OK && ok;
	return ok;
}

static BOOL receive_test_command(uint8_t expected_mode, uint32_t expected_sequence)
{
	motion_command_message_t command;
	INT received = tk_rcv_mbf(g_mbf_motion_commands, &command, TMO_POL);

	return received == (INT)sizeof(command) &&
	       command.header.type == VEHICLE_MESSAGE_MOTION_COMMAND &&
	       command.header.version == VEHICLE_MESSAGE_VERSION &&
	       command.header.sequence == expected_sequence &&
	       command.mode == expected_mode &&
	       command.linear_velocity == 0 && command.angular_velocity == 0 &&
	       command.duration_ms == 0U;
}

static const vehicle_controller_task_config_t test_config = {
	{200U, 200U, 200U, 200U, 500U, 300U},
	10U, 100U, NULL, NULL
};

static BOOL controller_arm_test(void)
{
	vehicle_controller_context_t context = {0};
	vehicle_controller_inputs_t inputs = {false, false};
	motion_command_message_t unexpected;
	BOOL ok = controller_state_init(&context.controller, &test_config.safety, 100U);

	context.status_wait_ms = 100U;
	ok = queue_safe_sensor_messages(100U, 1U) && ok;
	ok = controller_cycle(&context, 100U, &inputs) &&
	     context.controller.state == CONTROLLER_WAITING &&
	     tk_rcv_mbf(g_mbf_motion_commands, &unexpected, TMO_POL) == E_TMOUT && ok;
	inputs.go = true;
	ok = controller_cycle(&context, 101U, &inputs) &&
	     context.controller.state == CONTROLLER_FOLLOWING &&
	     !context.arm_requested &&
	     tk_rcv_mbf(g_mbf_motion_commands, &unexpected, TMO_POL) == E_TMOUT && ok;
	ok = queue_motion_status(101U, 1U, 0U, 0, 0) && ok;
	ok = controller_cycle(&context, 102U, &inputs) &&
	     context.controller.state == CONTROLLER_FOLLOWING &&
	     context.arm_requested &&
	     receive_test_command(VEHICLE_MOTION_ARM, 1U) && ok;
	inputs.go = false;
	ok = controller_cycle(&context, 103U, &inputs) &&
	     context.controller.state == CONTROLLER_WAITING &&
	     !context.arm_requested &&
	     receive_test_command(VEHICLE_MOTION_STOP, 2U) && ok;
	return drain_controller_test_buffers() && ok;
}

static BOOL controller_fault_test(void)
{
	vehicle_controller_context_t context = {0};
	vehicle_controller_inputs_t inputs = {false, false};
	BOOL ok = controller_state_init(&context.controller, &test_config.safety, 100U);

	context.status_wait_ms = 100U;
	ok = queue_motion_status(301U, 1U, 0U, 0, 0) && ok;
	ok = !controller_cycle(&context, 301U, &inputs) &&
	     context.controller.state == CONTROLLER_FAULTED &&
	     context.emergency_sent &&
	     receive_test_command(VEHICLE_MOTION_EMERGENCY_STOP, 1U) && ok;
	return drain_controller_test_buffers() && ok;
}

static BOOL controller_local_emergency_test(void)

	vehicle_controller_context_t context = {0};

	context.status_wait_ms = 100U;

	ok = queue_motion_status(100U, 1U, 0U, 0, 0) && ok;

	     context.controller.state == CONTROLLER_FAULTED &&
	     context.emergency_sent &&
	     receive_test_command(VEHICLE_MOTION_EMERGENCY_STOP, 1U) && ok;
	return drain_controller_test_buffers() && ok;
}

	context.status_wait_ms = 100U;
	ok = queue_safe_sensor_messages(100U, 1U) && ok;
	ok = queue_motion_status(100U, 1U, 0U, 20, -20) && ok;

	     context.controller.state == CONTROLLER_FAULTED &&
	     context.emergency_sent &&
	     receive_test_command(VEHICLE_MOTION_EMERGENCY_STOP, 1U) && ok;

	vehicle_controller_context_t context = {0};

	ok = queue_safe_sensor_messages(201U, 2U) && ok;

	     context.controller.state == CONTROLLER_FAULTED &&

BOOL vehicle_controller_task_test(void)
{
	BOOL arm_ok;
	BOOL fault_ok;
	BOOL emergency_ok;
	BOOL status_ok;
	BOOL stale_status_ok;
	TEST_INIT();

	arm_ok = controller_arm_test();
	fault_ok = controller_fault_test();
	emergency_ok = controller_local_emergency_test();
	status_ok = controller_nonidle_status_test();
	stale_status_ok = controller_stale_status_test();
	TEST_CHECK("VC-U13", arm_ok, "fresh safety inputs and local GO gate ARM/STOP commands");
	TEST_CHECK("VC-U14", emergency_ok, "local emergency input sends a latched emergency stop");
	TEST_CHECK("VC-U15", fault_ok, "missing sensor deadlines latch fault and request emergency stop");
	TEST_CHECK("VC-U16", status_ok, "nonzero commanded output status blocks arming and requests emergency stop");
	TEST_CHECK("VC-U17", stale_status_ok, "stale motion status faults despite fresh sensor observations");
	TEST_SUMMARY("vehicle controller task");
	return arm_ok && fault_ok && emergency_ok && status_ok && stale_status_ok;
}

#ifndef VEHICLE_CONTROLLER_TASK_H
#define VEHICLE_CONTROLLER_TASK_H

#include <tk/tkernel.h>
#include "controller_state.h"

typedef struct {
	bool go;
	bool emergency_stopped;
} vehicle_controller_inputs_t;

typedef bool (*vehicle_controller_read_inputs_t)(
	void *context, vehicle_controller_inputs_t *inputs);

typedef struct {
	car_safety_config_t safety;
	RELTIM period_ms;
	uint32_t motion_status_max_age_ms;
	vehicle_controller_read_inputs_t read_inputs;
	void *input_context;
} vehicle_controller_task_config_t;

/* Opt-in safety/arming task. The callback supplies trusted local GO and
 * emergency-stop inputs and must return promptly without network access.
 * The callback context must remain valid while the task runs. The task
 * exclusively consumes line, IMU, obstacle, fault and motion-status buffers.
 * It sends ARM only after the safety gate passes and a fresh zero-output
 * motion status is received; it does not yet issue motion commands. Motion
 * status is commanded state, not evidence that the vehicle physically stopped.
 * The task period must be no more than half the motion-status publish interval.
 * Do not start it alongside controller_monitor_start(). */
ER vehicle_controller_task_start(const vehicle_controller_task_config_t *config);

#include "../app.h"
#include "gpio_dispatcher.h"

#include <sys/sysdef.h>
#include <tk/syslib.h>

#define GPIO_BANK_IRQ 13U
#define GPIO_INTR_BASE (IO_BANK0_BASE + 0x0f0U)
#define GPIO_PROC0_INTE_BASE (IO_BANK0_BASE + 0x100U)
#define GPIO_PROC0_INTF_BASE (IO_BANK0_BASE + 0x110U)
#define GPIO_PROC0_INTS_BASE (IO_BANK0_BASE + 0x120U)
#define GPIO_PROC1_INTE_BASE (IO_BANK0_BASE + 0x130U)
#define GPIO_PROC1_INTF_BASE (IO_BANK0_BASE + 0x140U)

static gpio_irq_router_t s_router;
static bool s_started;

bool gpio_dispatcher_is_started(void)
{
	return s_started;
}

static void gpio_dispatcher_irq(UINT intno)
{
	uint32_t events[GPIO_IRQ_GROUPS];
	uint32_t pins;
	unsigned i;

	(void)intno;
	for (i = 0U; i < GPIO_IRQ_GROUPS; i++) {
		events[i] = in_w(GPIO_PROC0_INTS_BASE + 4U * i) &
			    s_router.claimed[i];
		if (events[i] != 0U)
			out_w(GPIO_INTR_BASE + 4U * i, events[i]);
	}
	pins = in_w(GPIO_IN);
	(void)gpio_irq_router_dispatch(&s_router, events, pins);
}

ER gpio_dispatcher_register(const uint32_t mask[GPIO_IRQ_GROUPS],
                            gpio_irq_callback_t callback, void *context)
{
#if TK_SUPPORT_SMP
	if (tk_get_prc() != 1) return E_CTX;
#endif
	if (s_started) return E_OBJ;
	return gpio_irq_router_add(&s_router, mask, callback, context) ? E_OK : E_PAR;
}

ER gpio_dispatcher_start(void)
{
	T_DINT interrupt = {0};
	unsigned i;
	ER er;

#if TK_SUPPORT_SMP
	if (tk_get_prc() != 1) return E_CTX;
#endif
	if (s_started || s_router.count == 0U) return E_OBJ;
	if ((in_w(NVIC_ISER(GPIO_BANK_IRQ)) & (1U << GPIO_BANK_IRQ)) != 0U)
		return E_OBJ;
	for (i = 0U; i < GPIO_IRQ_GROUPS; i++) {
		if (in_w(GPIO_PROC0_INTE_BASE + 4U * i) != 0U ||
		    in_w(GPIO_PROC0_INTF_BASE + 4U * i) != 0U ||
		    in_w(GPIO_PROC1_INTE_BASE + 4U * i) != 0U ||
		    in_w(GPIO_PROC1_INTF_BASE + 4U * i) != 0U)
			return E_OBJ;
	}
	interrupt.intatr = TA_HLNG;
	interrupt.inthdr = (FP)gpio_dispatcher_irq;
	er = tk_def_int(GPIO_BANK_IRQ, &interrupt);
	if (er < E_OK) return er;

	s_started = true;
	for (i = 0U; i < GPIO_IRQ_GROUPS; i++) {
		out_w(GPIO_INTR_BASE + 4U * i, s_router.claimed[i]);
		out_w(GPIO_PROC0_INTE_BASE + 4U * i, s_router.claimed[i]);
	}
	EnableInt(GPIO_BANK_IRQ, 2);
	return E_OK;
}

#ifndef GPIO_DISPATCHER_H
#define GPIO_DISPATCHER_H

#include <tk/tkernel.h>
#include "gpio_irq_router.h"

/* One startup task pinned to core 0 registers every client before start.
 * No other code may install IRQ 13 or enable GPIO interrupts on either core.
 * Registrations stay fixed and callback contexts must remain valid while the
 * IRQ is enabled. Callbacks run in interrupt context and must not block.
 * Pending bits do not preserve edge order or timestamps; clients must treat
 * simultaneous opposite edges on one pin as ambiguous. */
ER gpio_dispatcher_register(const uint32_t mask[GPIO_IRQ_GROUPS],
                            gpio_irq_callback_t callback, void *context);
ER gpio_dispatcher_start(void);

/* Read only from the same core-0 startup owner or a core-0-pinned task. */
bool gpio_dispatcher_is_started(void);

#endif
=== C:\Users\Yong Jun\school\INF2004-Embedded_Systems\project\INF2004_Project\app_program\gpio\gpio_irq_router.c ===
#include "gpio_irq_router.h"

#include <stddef.h>

bool gpio_irq_router_add(gpio_irq_router_t *router,
                         const uint32_t mask[GPIO_IRQ_GROUPS],
                         gpio_irq_callback_t callback, void *context)
{
	unsigned group;
	bool any = false;
	gpio_irq_client_t *client;

	if (router == NULL || mask == NULL || callback == NULL ||
	    router->count >= GPIO_IRQ_CLIENTS) return false;
	for (group = 0U; group < GPIO_IRQ_GROUPS; group++) {
		unsigned shift;
		if ((mask[group] & 0x33333333U) != 0U ||
		    (group == 3U && (mask[group] & 0xff000000U) != 0U))
			return false;
		for (shift = 0U; shift < 32U; shift += 4U) {
			uint32_t pin_mask = 0xfU << shift;
			if ((mask[group] & pin_mask) != 0U &&
			    (router->claimed[group] & pin_mask) != 0U)
				return false;
		}
		any = any || mask[group] != 0U;
	}
	if (!any) return false;

	client = &router->clients[router->count];
	for (group = 0U; group < GPIO_IRQ_GROUPS; group++) {
		client->mask[group] = mask[group];
		router->claimed[group] |= mask[group];
	}
	client->callback = callback;
	client->context = context;
	router->count++;
	return true;
}

bool gpio_irq_router_dispatch(const gpio_irq_router_t *router,
                              const uint32_t events[GPIO_IRQ_GROUPS],
                              uint32_t pin_levels)
{
	unsigned client_index;

	if (router == NULL || events == NULL || router->count > GPIO_IRQ_CLIENTS)
		return false;
	for (client_index = 0U; client_index < router->count; client_index++) {
		const gpio_irq_client_t *client = &router->clients[client_index];
		uint32_t selected[GPIO_IRQ_GROUPS];
		unsigned group;
		bool any = false;
		for (group = 0U; group < GPIO_IRQ_GROUPS; group++) {
			selected[group] = events[group] & client->mask[group];
			any = any || selected[group] != 0U;
		}
		if (any) client->callback(selected, pin_levels, client->context);
	}
	return true;
}
=== C:\Users\Yong Jun\school\INF2004-Embedded_Systems\project\INF2004_Project\app_program\gpio\gpio_irq_router.h ===
#ifndef GPIO_IRQ_ROUTER_H
#define GPIO_IRQ_ROUTER_H

#include <stdbool.h>
#include <stdint.h>

#define GPIO_IRQ_GROUPS 4U
#define GPIO_IRQ_CLIENTS 4U

/* GPIO n is in group n/8, with falling/rising bits 2/3 of nibble n%8.
 * The event array is valid only for the duration of the callback. */
typedef void (*gpio_irq_callback_t)(const uint32_t events[GPIO_IRQ_GROUPS],
                                    uint32_t pin_levels, void *context);

typedef struct {
	uint32_t mask[GPIO_IRQ_GROUPS];
	gpio_irq_callback_t callback;
	void *context;
} gpio_irq_client_t;

typedef struct {
	gpio_irq_client_t clients[GPIO_IRQ_CLIENTS];
	uint32_t claimed[GPIO_IRQ_GROUPS];
	uint8_t count;
} gpio_irq_router_t;

/* Zero-initialise and configure before delivery starts. Each GPIO has one owner. */
bool gpio_irq_router_add(gpio_irq_router_t *router,
                         const uint32_t mask[GPIO_IRQ_GROUPS],
                         gpio_irq_callback_t callback, void *context);
bool gpio_irq_router_dispatch(const gpio_irq_router_t *router,
                              const uint32_t events[GPIO_IRQ_GROUPS],
                              uint32_t pin_levels);

#endif

#include "../app.h"
#include "gpio_irq_test.h"

#if APP_TEST
#include "../test_report.h"
#include "gpio_irq_router.h"

typedef struct {
	uint32_t events[GPIO_IRQ_GROUPS];
	uint32_t pins;
	unsigned calls;
} captured_t;

static void capture(const uint32_t events[GPIO_IRQ_GROUPS],
                    uint32_t pins, void *context)
{
	captured_t *result = context;
	unsigned i;
	for (i = 0U; i < GPIO_IRQ_GROUPS; i++) result->events[i] = events[i];
	result->pins = pins;
	result->calls++;
}

static BOOL routing_test(void)
{
	gpio_irq_router_t router = {0};
	captured_t encoder = {0}, echo = {0};
	const uint32_t encoder_mask[GPIO_IRQ_GROUPS] = {0U, 0U, 0x0000ccccU, 0U};
	const uint32_t echo_mask[GPIO_IRQ_GROUPS] = {0x000c0000U, 0U, 0U, 0U};
	const uint32_t events[GPIO_IRQ_GROUPS] = {0x000c0000U, 0U, 0x000000ccU, 0U};

	return gpio_irq_router_add(&router, encoder_mask, capture, &encoder) &&
	       gpio_irq_router_add(&router, echo_mask, capture, &echo) &&
	       gpio_irq_router_dispatch(&router, events, 0x1234U) &&
	       encoder.calls == 1U && encoder.events[2] == 0xccU &&
	       encoder.events[0] == 0U && encoder.pins == 0x1234U &&
	       echo.calls == 1U && echo.events[0] == 0x000c0000U &&
	       echo.events[2] == 0U && echo.pins == 0x1234U;
}

static BOOL ownership_test(void)
{
	gpio_irq_router_t router = {0};
	captured_t captured = {0};
	const uint32_t original[GPIO_IRQ_GROUPS] = {0U, 0U, 0x00000004U, 0U};
	const uint32_t same_pin[GPIO_IRQ_GROUPS] = {0U, 0U, 0x00000008U, 0U};
	const uint32_t invalid_level[GPIO_IRQ_GROUPS] = {1U, 0U, 0U, 0U};
	const uint32_t invalid_pin[GPIO_IRQ_GROUPS] = {0U, 0U, 0U, 0x04000000U};
	const uint32_t empty[GPIO_IRQ_GROUPS] = {0U, 0U, 0U, 0U};
	const uint32_t unrelated[GPIO_IRQ_GROUPS] = {0U, 0U, 0x00000080U, 0U};
	const uint32_t second[GPIO_IRQ_GROUPS] = {4U, 0U, 0U, 0U};
	const uint32_t third[GPIO_IRQ_GROUPS] = {0U, 4U, 0U, 0U};
	const uint32_t fourth[GPIO_IRQ_GROUPS] = {0U, 0U, 0U, 4U};
	const uint32_t fifth[GPIO_IRQ_GROUPS] = {0x40U, 0U, 0U, 0U};

	return gpio_irq_router_add(&router, original, capture, &captured) &&
	       !gpio_irq_router_add(&router, same_pin, capture, &captured) &&
	       !gpio_irq_router_add(&router, invalid_level, capture, &captured) &&
	       !gpio_irq_router_add(&router, invalid_pin, capture, &captured) &&
	       !gpio_irq_router_add(&router, empty, capture, &captured) &&
	       !gpio_irq_router_add(&router, original, NULL, &captured) &&
	       gpio_irq_router_dispatch(&router, unrelated, 0U) &&
	       captured.calls == 0U &&
	       gpio_irq_router_add(&router, second, capture, &captured) &&
	       gpio_irq_router_add(&router, third, capture, &captured) &&
	       gpio_irq_router_add(&router, fourth, capture, &captured) &&
	       !gpio_irq_router_add(&router, fifth, capture, &captured);
}

BOOL gpio_irq_test(void)
{
	BOOL routing_ok;
	BOOL ownership_ok;
	TEST_INIT();

	routing_ok = routing_test();
	ownership_ok = ownership_test();
	TEST_CHECK("GPIO-U01", routing_ok, "simultaneous events routed to owners");
	TEST_CHECK("GPIO-U02", ownership_ok, "pin conflicts and invalid masks rejected");
	TEST_SUMMARY("gpio routing");
	return routing_ok && ownership_ok;
}
