#include "../app.h"
#include "line_adc_reader.h"

#include <sys/sysdef.h>

#define LINE_ADC_OVERSAMPLES 8U
#define LINE_ADC_MAX_VALUE 4095U
#define LINE_ADC_FIRST_PIN 26U
#define LINE_ADC_BLACK_THRESHOLD 1000U

static line_adc_reader_t s_task_reader;
static line_adc_config_t s_task_reader_config;
static bool s_task_start_attempted;

static ER initialize_task_reader(void *context)
{
	return line_adc_reader_init(context, &s_task_reader_config);
}

static bool valid_channel(const line_adc_channel_t *channel)
{
	return channel->channel <= 2U && channel->threshold <= LINE_ADC_MAX_VALUE;
}

static bool valid_config(const line_adc_config_t *config)
{
	return config != NULL && valid_channel(&config->barcode) &&
	       valid_channel(&config->center) && valid_channel(&config->right) &&
	       config->barcode.channel != config->center.channel &&
	       config->barcode.channel != config->right.channel &&
	       config->center.channel != config->right.channel &&
	       config->max_sample_span_ms > 0U &&
	       config->max_sample_span_ms <= INT32_MAX;
}

static bool is_black(const line_adc_channel_t *config, uint16_t raw)
{
	return config->black_above ? raw > config->threshold : raw < config->threshold;
}

static uint64_t clock_ms(const SYSTIM *time)
{
	return ((uint64_t)(uint32_t)time->hi << 32) | time->lo;
}

ER line_adc_reader_default_config(line_adc_config_t *config,
				  uint32_t max_sample_span_ms)
{
	line_adc_config_t imported = {
		{2U, LINE_ADC_BLACK_THRESHOLD, true},
		{1U, LINE_ADC_BLACK_THRESHOLD, true},
		{0U, LINE_ADC_BLACK_THRESHOLD, true},
		max_sample_span_ms
	};

	if (config == NULL || !valid_config(&imported)) return E_PAR;
	*config = imported;
	return E_OK;
}

ER line_adc_reader_init(line_adc_reader_t *reader, const line_adc_config_t *config)
{
	const line_adc_channel_t *channels[3];
	ID device;
	unsigned i;

	if (reader == NULL || !valid_config(config)) return E_PAR;
	if (reader->initialized) return E_OBJ;
	device = tk_opn_dev((const UB *)"adca", TD_READ | TD_EXCL);
	if (device < E_OK) return device;
	if (device == E_OK) return E_SYS;

	channels[0] = &config->barcode;
	channels[1] = &config->center;
	channels[2] = &config->right;
	for (i = 0U; i < 3U; i++) {
		UINT pin = LINE_ADC_FIRST_PIN + channels[i]->channel;
		out_w(GPIO_CTRL(pin), GPIO_CTRL_FUNCSEL_NULL);
		clr_w(GPIO(pin), GPIO_IE | GPIO_PUE | GPIO_PDE);
	}
	reader->config = *config;
	reader->device = device;
	reader->faulted = false;
	reader->initialized = true;
	return E_OK;
}

ER line_adc_reader_start_task(const line_adc_config_t *reader_config,
			      const line_barcode_task_config_t *task_config)
{
	line_barcode_task_config_t task;
	ER er;

	if (!valid_config(reader_config) || task_config == NULL) return E_PAR;
	if (s_task_start_attempted) return E_OBJ;
	s_task_reader_config = *reader_config;
	task = *task_config;
	task.initialize = initialize_task_reader;
	task.read = line_adc_reader_read;
	task.read_context = &s_task_reader;
	er = line_barcode_task_start(&task);
	if (er == E_OK) s_task_start_attempted = true;
	return er;
}

ER line_adc_reader_start_default_task(uint32_t max_sample_span_ms,
				      const line_barcode_task_config_t *task_config)
{
	line_adc_config_t reader_config;
	ER er = line_adc_reader_default_config(&reader_config, max_sample_span_ms);

	if (er != E_OK) return er;
	return line_adc_reader_start_task(&reader_config, task_config);
}

static ER read_channel(line_adc_reader_t *reader, const line_adc_channel_t *channel,
		       ir_reading_t *reading)
{
	UW sum = 0U;
	unsigned i;

	for (i = 0U; i < LINE_ADC_OVERSAMPLES; i++) {
		SZ actual = 0;
		ER er;
		reader->pending_value = 0U;
		er = tk_srea_dev(reader->device, channel->channel,
				 &reader->pending_value, 1, &actual);
		if (er != E_OK) return er < E_OK ? er : E_IO;
		if (actual != 1 || reader->pending_value > LINE_ADC_MAX_VALUE) return E_IO;
		sum += reader->pending_value;
	}
	reading->raw = (uint16_t)(sum / LINE_ADC_OVERSAMPLES);
	reading->black = is_black(channel, reading->raw);
	return E_OK;
}

ER line_adc_reader_read(void *context, ir_readings_t *readings,
			uint32_t *timestamp_ms)
{
	line_adc_reader_t *reader = context;
	ir_readings_t result;
	SYSTIM start, barcode_start, end;
	uint64_t start_ms, barcode_ms, end_ms;
	ER er;

	if (reader == NULL || readings == NULL || timestamp_ms == NULL) return E_PAR;
	if (!reader->initialized || reader->faulted) return E_OBJ;
	er = tk_get_otm(&start);
	if (er != E_OK) goto fault;
	er = read_channel(reader, &reader->config.center, &result.center);
	if (er != E_OK) goto fault;
	er = read_channel(reader, &reader->config.right, &result.right);
	if (er != E_OK) goto fault;
	er = tk_get_otm(&barcode_start);
	if (er != E_OK) goto fault;
	er = read_channel(reader, &reader->config.barcode, &result.barcode);
	if (er != E_OK) goto fault;
	er = tk_get_otm(&end);
	if (er != E_OK) goto fault;
	start_ms = clock_ms(&start);
	barcode_ms = clock_ms(&barcode_start);
	end_ms = clock_ms(&end);
	if (barcode_ms < start_ms || end_ms < barcode_ms ||
	    end_ms - start_ms > reader->config.max_sample_span_ms) {
		er = E_TMOUT;
		goto fault;
	}
	*readings = result;
	*timestamp_ms = barcode_start.lo;
	return E_OK;

fault:
	reader->faulted = true;
	return er < E_OK ? er : E_IO;
}

ER line_adc_reader_close(line_adc_reader_t *reader)
{
	ER er;

	if (reader == NULL) return E_PAR;
	if (!reader->initialized || reader->faulted) return E_OBJ;
	er = tk_cls_dev(reader->device, 0U);
	if (er != E_OK) return er < E_OK ? er : E_IO;
	reader->initialized = false;
	reader->device = 0;
	return E_OK;
}

BOOL line_adc_reader_test(void)

	TEST_SUMMARY("line ADC reader");

#ifndef LINE_ADC_READER_H
#define LINE_ADC_READER_H

#include <tk/tkernel.h>
#include "line_barcode_task.h"
#include "line_observation.h"

typedef struct {
	uint8_t channel;
	uint16_t threshold;
	bool black_above;
} line_adc_channel_t;

typedef struct {
	line_adc_channel_t barcode;
	line_adc_channel_t center;
	line_adc_channel_t right;
	uint32_t max_sample_span_ms;
} line_adc_config_t;

typedef struct {
	line_adc_config_t config;
	ID device;
	UW pending_value;
	bool initialized;
	bool faulted;
} line_adc_reader_t;

/* Import the original line/barcode ADC mapping and thresholds. The acquisition
 * span remains caller-selected because it depends on the chosen task period. */
ER line_adc_reader_default_config(line_adc_config_t *config,
				  uint32_t max_sample_span_ms);

/* Zero-initialize the reader and keep it alive until close or reboot. A
 * single owner must reserve the ADC and pins before calling init. Channels
 * 0..2 map to GP26..28; GP29 is prohibited. */
ER line_adc_reader_init(line_adc_reader_t *reader, const line_adc_config_t *config);

/* Starts the line/barcode task with a static-lifetime ADC reader. ADC
 * ownership is acquired inside that task; this function is not called by
 * default. */
ER line_adc_reader_start_task(const line_adc_config_t *reader_config,
			      const line_barcode_task_config_t *task_config);

/* Uses the imported sensor mapping/thresholds with a caller-selected
 * acquisition span, then starts the line/barcode task. */
ER line_adc_reader_start_default_task(uint32_t max_sample_span_ms,
				      const line_barcode_task_config_t *task_config);

/* Compatible with line_barcode_read_t. On error, outputs are unchanged and
 * the reader is latched faulted; its ADC ownership is retained until reboot. */
ER line_adc_reader_read(void *context, ir_readings_t *readings,
			uint32_t *timestamp_ms);

/* Close only an unfaulted reader that no task is using. Pins remain analog. */
ER line_adc_reader_close(line_adc_reader_t *reader);

#if APP_TEST
BOOL line_adc_reader_test(void);
#endif

=== C:\Users\Yong Jun\school\INF2004-Embedded_Systems\project\INF2004_Project\app_program\line_barcode\line_barcode_task.c ===
#include "../app.h"
#include "../vehicle_buffers.h"
#include "line_barcode_task.h"

#define LINE_BARCODE_PRIORITY 10
#define LINE_FAULT_RETRY_MS 1000U

typedef struct {
	barcode_scanner_t scanner;
	barcode_scanner_config_t barcode_config;
	wheel_odometry_message_t pending;
	uint32_t start_ms;
	uint32_t max_age_ms;
	uint32_t last_odom_ms;
	uint32_t last_odom_sequence;
	uint32_t last_left;
	uint32_t last_right;
	uint32_t mean_count;
	int32_t half_count;
	uint32_t line_sequence;
	uint32_t barcode_sequence;
	uint32_t last_sample_ms;
	ER delivery_error;
	bool have_pending;
	bool have_odom;
	bool scanner_ready;
	bool have_sample;
} line_state_t;

static line_barcode_task_config_t s_config;
static line_state_t s_state;
static bool s_started;

static int32_t signed_bits(uint32_t bits)
{
	return bits <= INT32_MAX ? (int32_t)bits :
	       INT32_MIN + (int32_t)(bits - 0x80000000U);
}

static bool add_odometry(line_state_t *state, const wheel_odometry_message_t *message)
{
	uint32_t left = (uint32_t)message->left_count;
	uint32_t right = (uint32_t)message->right_count;

	if (message->header.type != VEHICLE_MESSAGE_WHEEL_ODOMETRY ||
	    message->header.version != VEHICLE_MESSAGE_VERSION || message->valid != 1U)
		return false;
	if (state->have_odom) {
		uint32_t ld = left - state->last_left;
		uint32_t rd = right - state->last_right;
		uint32_t elapsed = message->header.timestamp_ms - state->last_odom_ms;
		int64_t sum;
		int64_t whole;
		int32_t remainder;
		if (message->header.sequence - state->last_odom_sequence != 1U ||
		    elapsed == 0U || elapsed > INT32_MAX ||
		    ld == 0x80000000U || rd == 0x80000000U)
			return false;
		sum = (int64_t)signed_bits(ld) + signed_bits(rd) + state->half_count;
		whole = sum / 2;
		remainder = (int32_t)(sum % 2);
		if (remainder < 0) {
			whole--;
			remainder += 2;
		}
		state->mean_count += (uint32_t)whole;
		state->half_count = remainder;
	}
	state->last_left = left;
	state->last_right = right;
	state->last_odom_ms = message->header.timestamp_ms;
	state->last_odom_sequence = message->header.sequence;
	state->have_odom = true;
	return true;
}

/* A future-dated message is retained until a later sensor sample. */
static uint16_t receive_odometry(line_state_t *state, uint32_t timestamp_ms,
				 bool *updated)
{
	wheel_odometry_message_t message;
	INT received;

	*updated = false;
	for (;;) {
		uint32_t ahead;
		if (state->have_pending) {
			message = state->pending;
		} else {
			received = tk_rcv_mbf(g_mbf_line_odometry, &message, TMO_POL);
			if (received == E_TMOUT) break;
			if (received != (INT)sizeof(message))
				return VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_LINE_ODOMETRY;
		}
		if (message.header.type != VEHICLE_MESSAGE_WHEEL_ODOMETRY ||
		    message.header.version != VEHICLE_MESSAGE_VERSION || message.valid != 1U)
			return VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_LINE_ODOMETRY;
		ahead = message.header.timestamp_ms - timestamp_ms;
		if (ahead != 0U && ahead <= INT32_MAX) {
			state->pending = message;
			state->have_pending = true;
			break;
		}
		state->have_pending = false;
		if (timestamp_ms - message.header.timestamp_ms > state->max_age_ms ||
		    !add_odometry(state, &message))
			return VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_LINE_ODOMETRY;
		*updated = true;
	}
	if (state->have_odom) {
		if (timestamp_ms - state->last_odom_ms > state->max_age_ms)
			return VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_LINE_ODOMETRY;
	} else if (timestamp_ms - state->start_ms > state->max_age_ms) {
		return VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_LINE_ODOMETRY;
	}
	return 0U;
}

static uint16_t process_readings(line_state_t *state, const ir_readings_t *readings,
				 uint32_t timestamp_ms)
{
	line_observation_message_t line = {0};
	barcode_event_message_t barcode = {0};
	uint8_t position;
	bool odom_updated;
	uint16_t fault;
	ER er;

	if (state->have_sample) {
		uint32_t elapsed = timestamp_ms - state->last_sample_ms;
		if (elapsed == 0U || elapsed > INT32_MAX)
			return VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_LINE_CLOCK;
	}
	fault = receive_odometry(state, timestamp_ms, &odom_updated);
	if (fault != 0U) return fault;
	if (state->have_odom) {
		int32_t count = signed_bits(state->mean_count);
		if (!state->scanner_ready) {
			if (!barcode_scanner_init(&state->scanner, &state->barcode_config,
						  readings->barcode.black, count))
				return VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_LINE_BARCODE;
			state->scanner_ready = true;
		} else {
			if (readings->barcode.black != state->scanner.last_black) {
				uint32_t since_sample = state->last_odom_ms - state->last_sample_ms;
				if (!odom_updated || since_sample == 0U || since_sample > INT32_MAX)
					return VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_LINE_ODOMETRY;
			}
			if (!barcode_scanner_update(&state->scanner, readings->barcode.black, count))
				return VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_LINE_BARCODE;
		}
	}
	position = line_following_position(readings);
	line.header.type = VEHICLE_MESSAGE_LINE_OBSERVATION;
	line.header.version = VEHICLE_MESSAGE_VERSION;
	line.header.sequence = ++state->line_sequence;
	line.header.timestamp_ms = timestamp_ms;
	line.lateral_error = position == POS_LEFT ? -1 : position == POS_RIGHT ? 1 : 0;
	line.confidence = position == POS_LOST ? 0U : 1000U;
	line.line_detected = position != POS_LOST;
	line.intersection_detected = position == POS_JUNCTION;
	er = tk_snd_mbf(g_mbf_line_observations, &line, (INT)sizeof(line), TMO_POL);
	if (er != E_OK) {
		state->delivery_error = er;
		return VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_LINE_DELIVERY;
	}
	if (state->scanner_ready && state->scanner.result_ready) {
		uint8_t payload[BARCODE_MAX_PAYLOAD];
		uint8_t length;
		unsigned i;
		if (!barcode_scanner_take_result(&state->scanner, payload, sizeof(payload), &length) ||
		    length > sizeof(barcode.payload))
			return VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_LINE_BARCODE;
		barcode.header.type = VEHICLE_MESSAGE_BARCODE_EVENT;
		barcode.header.version = VEHICLE_MESSAGE_VERSION;
		barcode.header.sequence = ++state->barcode_sequence;
		barcode.header.timestamp_ms = timestamp_ms;
		for (i = 0U; i < length; i++) barcode.payload[i] = (char)payload[i];
		barcode.length = length;
		barcode.valid = 1U;
		er = tk_snd_mbf(g_mbf_barcode_events, &barcode, (INT)sizeof(barcode), TMO_POL);
		if (er != E_OK) {
			state->delivery_error = er;
			return VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_LINE_DELIVERY;
		}
	}
	state->last_sample_ms = timestamp_ms;
	state->have_sample = true;
	return 0U;
}

static void report_line_fault(uint16_t code, uint32_t timestamp_ms)
{
	fault_message_t fault = {0};
	bool sent = false;

	fault.header.type = VEHICLE_MESSAGE_FAULT;
	fault.header.version = VEHICLE_MESSAGE_VERSION;
	fault.header.sequence = 1U;
	fault.header.timestamp_ms = timestamp_ms;
	fault.source = code == VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_LINE_DELIVERY ||
		       code == VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_LINE_ODOMETRY ?
		       VEHICLE_FAULT_COMMUNICATION :
		       (code == VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_LINE_CLOCK ||
			code == VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_LINE_SCHEDULE ?
			VEHICLE_FAULT_SYSTEM : VEHICLE_FAULT_SENSOR);
	fault.code = code;
	fault.recoverable = 0U;
	tm_printf((UB*)"line/barcode stopped; fault code %u\n", (unsigned)code);
	for (;;) {
		ER er;
		if (!sent) {
			er = tk_snd_mbf(g_mbf_faults, &fault, (INT)sizeof(fault), TMO_POL);
			if (er == E_OK) sent = true;
			else tm_printf((UB*)"line/barcode fault delivery failed: %d\n", (int)er);
		}
		er = tk_dly_tsk(LINE_FAULT_RETRY_MS);
		if (er < E_OK) {
			tm_printf((UB*)"line fault retry delay failed: %d\n", (int)er);
			er = tk_slp_tsk(TMO_FEVR);
			tm_printf((UB*)"line fault sleep returned: %d\n", (int)er);
		}
	}
}

static void line_barcode_task(INT stacd, void *exinf)
{
	SYSTIM now;
	uint32_t timestamp_ms = 0U;
	ER er;

	(void)stacd;
	(void)exinf;
	er = tk_get_otm(&now);
	if (er < E_OK) report_line_fault(VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_LINE_CLOCK, timestamp_ms);
	timestamp_ms = now.lo;
	s_state.start_ms = timestamp_ms;
	if (s_config.initialize != NULL) {
		er = s_config.initialize(s_config.read_context);
		if (er != E_OK) {
			tm_printf((UB*)"line sensor initialization failed: %d\n", (int)er);
			report_line_fault(VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_LINE_SENSOR, timestamp_ms);
		}
	}
	for (;;) {
		ir_readings_t readings;
		uint32_t sample_ms;
		uint16_t fault;
		er = s_config.read(s_config.read_context, &readings, &sample_ms);
		if (er != E_OK) {
			tm_printf((UB*)"line sensor read failed: %d\n", (int)er);
			report_line_fault(VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_LINE_SENSOR, timestamp_ms);
		}
		er = tk_get_otm(&now);
		if (er < E_OK) report_line_fault(VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_LINE_CLOCK, timestamp_ms);
		timestamp_ms = now.lo;
		if (timestamp_ms - sample_ms > s_config.odometry_max_age_ms)
			report_line_fault(VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_LINE_SENSOR, timestamp_ms);
		fault = process_readings(&s_state, &readings, sample_ms);
		if (fault != 0U) {
			if (fault == VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_LINE_DELIVERY)
				tm_printf((UB*)"line/barcode delivery failed: %d\n",
					  (int)s_state.delivery_error);
			report_line_fault(fault, timestamp_ms);
		}
		er = tk_dly_tsk(s_config.period_ms);
		if (er < E_OK) report_line_fault(VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_LINE_SCHEDULE, timestamp_ms);
	}
}

ER line_barcode_task_start(const line_barcode_task_config_t *config)
{
	T_CTSK task = {0};
	ID id;
	ER er;

	if (s_started) return E_OBJ;
	if (config == NULL || config->read == NULL || config->period_ms == 0U ||
	    config->period_ms > INT32_MAX || config->odometry_max_age_ms == 0U ||
	    config->odometry_max_age_ms > INT32_MAX ||
	    !barcode_scanner_init(&s_state.scanner, &config->barcode, false, 0))
		return E_PAR;
	if (g_mbf_line_odometry <= 0 || g_mbf_line_observations <= 0 ||
	    g_mbf_barcode_events <= 0 || g_mbf_faults <= 0)
		return E_OBJ;
	s_config = *config;
	s_state.barcode_config = config->barcode;
	s_state.max_age_ms = config->odometry_max_age_ms;
	task.task = (FP)line_barcode_task;
	task.tskatr = TA_HLNG | TA_RNG3;
	task.itskpri = LINE_BARCODE_PRIORITY;
	task.stksz = TASK_STACK_SIZE;
	id = tk_cre_tsk(&task);
	if (id < E_OK) return id;
	s_started = true;
	er = tk_sta_tsk(id, 0);
	if (er < E_OK) {
		ER cleanup = tk_del_tsk(id);
		if (cleanup < E_OK) tm_printf((UB*)"line task cleanup failed: %d\n", (int)cleanup);
		else s_started = false;
		return er;
	}
	return E_OK;
}

#if APP_TEST
#include "../test_report.h"

static line_state_t s_test_state;

static BOOL test_drain(ID buffer)
{
	union {
		wheel_odometry_message_t odometry;
		line_observation_message_t line;
		barcode_event_message_t barcode;
	} message;
	INT result;

	do {
		result = tk_rcv_mbf(buffer, &message, TMO_POL);
	} while (result > 0);
	return result == E_TMOUT;
}

static BOOL test_sample(line_state_t *state, ir_readings_t *readings,
			uint32_t count, uint32_t sequence, uint32_t timestamp)
{
	wheel_odometry_message_t odometry = {0};
	line_observation_message_t line;

	odometry.header.type = VEHICLE_MESSAGE_WHEEL_ODOMETRY;
	odometry.header.version = VEHICLE_MESSAGE_VERSION;
	odometry.header.sequence = sequence;
	odometry.header.timestamp_ms = timestamp;
	odometry.left_count = signed_bits(count);
	odometry.right_count = signed_bits(count);
	odometry.valid = 1U;
	if (tk_snd_mbf(g_mbf_line_odometry, &odometry, (INT)sizeof(odometry), TMO_POL) != E_OK ||
	    process_readings(state, readings, timestamp) != 0U ||
	    tk_rcv_mbf(g_mbf_line_observations, &line, TMO_POL) != (INT)sizeof(line))
		return FALSE;
	return line.header.type == VEHICLE_MESSAGE_LINE_OBSERVATION &&
	       line.line_detected == 1U && line.lateral_error == -1;
}

static BOOL test_character(line_state_t *state, ir_readings_t *readings,
			   const char *pattern, uint32_t *count,
			   uint32_t *sequence, uint32_t *timestamp, int direction)
{
	unsigned i;
	*count += (uint32_t)(direction * 10);
	*timestamp += 10U;
	readings->barcode.black = true;
	if (!test_sample(state, readings, *count, ++*sequence, *timestamp)) return FALSE;
	for (i = 0U; i < BARCODE_ELEMENTS; i++) {
		unsigned index = direction > 0 ? i : BARCODE_ELEMENTS - 1U - i;
		int width = pattern[index] == 'w' ? 20 : 10;
		*count += (uint32_t)(direction * width);
		*timestamp += 10U;
		readings->barcode.black = !readings->barcode.black;
		if (!test_sample(state, readings, *count, ++*sequence, *timestamp)) return FALSE;
	}
	return TRUE;
}

static BOOL task_frame_test(int direction)
{
	ir_readings_t readings = {0};
	barcode_event_message_t result;
	uint32_t count = direction > 0 ? (uint32_t)INT32_MAX - 40U :
			 (uint32_t)INT32_MIN + 40U;
	uint32_t sequence = UINT32_MAX - 10U;
	uint32_t timestamp = 100U;

	s_test_state = (line_state_t){0};
	s_test_state.barcode_config = (barcode_scanner_config_t){10U, 0U, 0U, 0U, 0U};
	s_test_state.max_age_ms = 50U;
	readings.center.black = true;
	if (!test_sample(&s_test_state, &readings, count, sequence, timestamp) ||
	    !test_character(&s_test_state, &readings, "nwnnwnwnn", &count, &sequence, &timestamp, direction) ||
	    !test_character(&s_test_state, &readings, "wnnnnwnnw", &count, &sequence, &timestamp, direction) ||
	    !test_character(&s_test_state, &readings, "nwnnwnwnn", &count, &sequence, &timestamp, direction))
		return FALSE;
	return tk_rcv_mbf(g_mbf_barcode_events, &result, TMO_POL) == (INT)sizeof(result) &&
	       result.header.type == VEHICLE_MESSAGE_BARCODE_EVENT && result.valid == 1U &&
	       result.length == 1U && result.payload[0] == 'A';
}

static BOOL task_mean_test(void)
{
	wheel_odometry_message_t message = {0};

	s_test_state = (line_state_t){0};
	message.header.type = VEHICLE_MESSAGE_WHEEL_ODOMETRY;
	message.header.version = VEHICLE_MESSAGE_VERSION;
	message.header.sequence = UINT32_MAX;
	message.header.timestamp_ms = UINT32_MAX - 5U;
	message.left_count = INT32_MAX - 1;
	message.right_count = INT32_MIN + 1;
	message.valid = 1U;
	if (!add_odometry(&s_test_state, &message)) return FALSE;
	message.header.sequence = 0U;
	message.header.timestamp_ms = 4U;
	message.left_count = INT32_MIN;
	message.right_count = INT32_MIN + 4;
	if (!add_odometry(&s_test_state, &message) ||
	    s_test_state.mean_count != 2U || s_test_state.half_count != 1)
		return FALSE;
	message.header.sequence = 1U;
	message.header.timestamp_ms = 5U;
	message.left_count = INT32_MIN + 1;
	if (!add_odometry(&s_test_state, &message) ||
	    s_test_state.mean_count != 3U || s_test_state.half_count != 0)
		return FALSE;
	message.header.sequence = 3U;
	message.header.timestamp_ms = 6U;
	if (add_odometry(&s_test_state, &message) || s_test_state.mean_count != 3U)
		return FALSE;
	message.header.sequence = 2U;
	message.left_count = INT32_MIN;
	if (!add_odometry(&s_test_state, &message) ||
	    s_test_state.mean_count != 2U || s_test_state.half_count != 1)
		return FALSE;
	message.header.sequence = 3U;
	message.header.timestamp_ms = 7U;
	message.left_count = INT32_MIN + 1;
	return add_odometry(&s_test_state, &message) &&
	       s_test_state.mean_count == 3U && s_test_state.half_count == 0;
}

static BOOL task_fault_test(void)
{
	ir_readings_t readings = {0};
	wheel_odometry_message_t odometry = {0};
	line_observation_message_t line;
	BOOL ok;
	unsigned i;

	s_test_state = (line_state_t){0};
	s_test_state.barcode_config = (barcode_scanner_config_t){10U, 0U, 0U, 0U, 0U};
	s_test_state.max_age_ms = 50U;
	s_test_state.start_ms = 100U;
	readings.center.black = true;
	ok = process_readings(&s_test_state, &readings, 100U) == 0U;
	ok = tk_rcv_mbf(g_mbf_line_observations, &line, TMO_POL) == (INT)sizeof(line) && ok;
	odometry.header.type = VEHICLE_MESSAGE_WHEEL_ODOMETRY;
	odometry.header.version = VEHICLE_MESSAGE_VERSION;
	odometry.header.sequence = 1U;
	odometry.header.timestamp_ms = 110U;
	odometry.valid = 1U;
	ok = tk_snd_mbf(g_mbf_line_odometry, &odometry, (INT)sizeof(odometry), TMO_POL) == E_OK && ok;
	ok = process_readings(&s_test_state, &readings, 105U) == 0U &&
	     s_test_state.have_pending && !s_test_state.have_odom && ok;
	ok = tk_rcv_mbf(g_mbf_line_observations, &line, TMO_POL) == (INT)sizeof(line) && ok;
	ok = process_readings(&s_test_state, &readings, 110U) == 0U &&
	     s_test_state.scanner_ready && !s_test_state.have_pending && ok;
	ok = tk_rcv_mbf(g_mbf_line_observations, &line, TMO_POL) == (INT)sizeof(line) && ok;
	readings.barcode.black = true;
	ok = process_readings(&s_test_state, &readings, 111U) ==
	     VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_LINE_ODOMETRY && ok;

	s_test_state = (line_state_t){0};
	s_test_state.barcode_config = (barcode_scanner_config_t){10U, 0U, 0U, 0U, 0U};
	s_test_state.max_age_ms = 50U;
	readings.barcode.black = false;
	if (!test_sample(&s_test_state, &readings, 0U, 1U, 100U)) ok = FALSE;
	for (i = 0U; i < VEHICLE_MB_LINE_CAPACITY; i++) {
		if (tk_snd_mbf(g_mbf_line_observations, &line, (INT)sizeof(line), TMO_POL) != E_OK)
			ok = FALSE;
	}
	if (process_readings(&s_test_state, &readings, 110U) != VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_LINE_DELIVERY ||
	    s_test_state.delivery_error != E_TMOUT)
		ok = FALSE;
	ok = test_drain(g_mbf_line_odometry) && ok;
	ok = test_drain(g_mbf_line_observations) && ok;
	ok = test_drain(g_mbf_barcode_events) && ok;

	s_test_state = (line_state_t){0};
	s_test_state.barcode_config = (barcode_scanner_config_t){10U, 0U, 0U, 0U, 0U};
	s_test_state.max_age_ms = 50U;
	if (!test_sample(&s_test_state, &readings, 0U, 1U, 100U)) ok = FALSE;
	odometry.header.sequence = 2U;
	odometry.header.timestamp_ms = 101U;
	if (tk_snd_mbf(g_mbf_line_odometry, &odometry, (INT)sizeof(odometry), TMO_POL) != E_OK)
		ok = FALSE;
	if (process_readings(&s_test_state, &readings, 200U) != VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_LINE_ODOMETRY)
		ok = FALSE;
	ok = test_drain(g_mbf_line_odometry) && ok;
	ok = test_drain(g_mbf_line_observations) && ok;
	return ok;
}

BOOL line_barcode_task_test(void)
{
	BOOL frame_ok;
	BOOL mean_ok;
	BOOL fault_ok;
	TEST_INIT();

	frame_ok = task_frame_test(1);
	frame_ok = task_frame_test(-1) && frame_ok;
	mean_ok = task_mean_test();
	fault_ok = task_fault_test();
	frame_ok = test_drain(g_mbf_line_odometry) && frame_ok;
	frame_ok = test_drain(g_mbf_line_observations) && frame_ok;
	frame_ok = test_drain(g_mbf_barcode_events) && frame_ok;
	TEST_CHECK("LB-U01", frame_ok, "simulated line and barcode messages");
	TEST_CHECK("LB-U02", mean_ok, "wheel-count and sequence wrap");
	TEST_CHECK("LB-U03", fault_ok, "odometry synchronization and full queues");
	TEST_SUMMARY("line/barcode task");
	return frame_ok && mean_ok && fault_ok;
}

#include "line_observation.h"

#include <stddef.h>

uint8_t line_following_position(const ir_readings_t *readings)
{
	if (readings == NULL) {
		return POS_LOST;
	}
	if (readings->center.black && readings->right.black) {
		return POS_JUNCTION;
	}
	if (readings->center.black) {
		return POS_LEFT;
	}
	if (readings->right.black) {
		return POS_RIGHT;
	}
	return POS_LOST;
}

#include "motion_control.h"

#include <stddef.h>

#define MOTION_MAX_CONTROL_INTERVAL_MS 250U

bool motion_control_start(motion_control_t *control,
			  const motion_control_config_t *config,
			  int8_t direction, uint32_t now_ms)
{
	motion_control_t initial = {0};

	if (control == NULL) return false;
	if (config == NULL || (direction != 1 && direction != -1) ||
	    config->feedback.max_interval_ms > MOTION_MAX_CONTROL_INTERVAL_MS ||
	    config->feedback.max_age_ms > MOTION_MAX_CONTROL_INTERVAL_MS ||
	    !motion_feedback_init(&initial.feedback, &config->feedback) ||
	    !wheel_speed_control_init(&initial.speed, &config->speed)) {
		*control = (motion_control_t){.faulted = true};
		return false;
	}
	initial.start_ms = now_ms;
	initial.last_check_ms = now_ms;
	initial.direction = direction;
	initial.running = true;
	*control = initial;
	return true;
}

void motion_control_stop(motion_control_t *control)
{
	if (control == NULL) return;
	control->output = (wheel_speed_output_t){0};
	if (control->speed.initialized)
		(void)wheel_speed_control_reset(&control->speed);
	control->have_output = false;
	control->running = false;
}

static motion_control_result_t fault(motion_control_t *control,
				     wheel_speed_output_t *output)
{
	if (output != NULL) *output = (wheel_speed_output_t){0};
	if (control != NULL) {
		motion_control_stop(control);
		control->faulted = true;
	}
	return MOTION_CONTROL_FAULT;
}

motion_control_result_t motion_control_step(motion_control_t *control,
			 const wheel_odometry_message_t *message,
			 uint32_t now_ms, wheel_speed_output_t *output)
{
	motion_feedback_result_t result;
	motion_wheel_speeds_t speeds;
	uint32_t last_sample;
	float left, right;

	if (control == NULL || output == NULL || !control->running || control->faulted)
		return fault(control, output);
	last_sample = control->feedback.have_baseline ?
		      control->feedback.last_timestamp_ms : control->start_ms;
	if (now_ms - control->last_check_ms > INT32_MAX ||
	    now_ms - last_sample > control->feedback.config.max_age_ms)
		return fault(control, output);
	control->last_check_ms = now_ms;
	if (message != NULL) {
		if (message->header.timestamp_ms - control->start_ms > INT32_MAX)
			return fault(control, output);
		result = motion_feedback_update(&control->feedback, message, now_ms, &speeds);
		if (result == MOTION_FEEDBACK_INVALID) return fault(control, output);
		if (result == MOTION_FEEDBACK_READY) {
			left = speeds.left_cm_per_second * control->direction;
			right = speeds.right_cm_per_second * control->direction;
			if (left < 0.0f || right < 0.0f ||
			    !wheel_speed_control_update(&control->speed, left, right,
							speeds.elapsed_seconds, &control->output))
				return fault(control, output);
			control->have_output = true;
		}
	}
	*output = control->output;
	return control->have_output ? MOTION_CONTROL_READY : MOTION_CONTROL_WAITING;
}
=== C:\Users\Yong Jun\school\INF2004-Embedded_Systems\project\INF2004_Project\app_program\motion\motion_control.h ===
#ifndef MOTION_CONTROL_H
#define MOTION_CONTROL_H

#include "motion_feedback.h"
#include "wheel_speed_control.h"

typedef enum {
	MOTION_CONTROL_WAITING = 0,
	MOTION_CONTROL_READY,
	MOTION_CONTROL_FAULT
} motion_control_result_t;

typedef struct {
	motion_feedback_config_t feedback;
	wheel_speed_config_t speed;
} motion_control_config_t;

typedef struct {
	motion_feedback_t feedback;
	wheel_speed_control_t speed;
	wheel_speed_output_t output;
	uint32_t start_ms;
	uint32_t last_check_ms;
	int8_t direction;
	bool have_output;
	bool running;
	bool faulted;
} motion_control_t;

/* Straight motion only: direction is +1 forward or -1 reverse for both
 * polarity-normalized wheels. Start only after the hardware is stopped.
 * The owner must use verified calibration and reset on every restart.
 * Failure clears the control state and latches a fault. */
bool motion_control_start(motion_control_t *control,
			  const motion_control_config_t *config,
			  int8_t direction, uint32_t now_ms);

/* Call periodically even without a message. The first message after start
 * establishes a baseline; output remains zero until a second valid message.
 * On any fault output is zero and the fault is latched. READY output may be
 * retained between samples only while the caller continues checking time.
 * A NULL output or invalid control is a fault; the caller must stop hardware.
 * This calculation cannot stop hardware or replace an independent watchdog. */
motion_control_result_t motion_control_step(motion_control_t *control,
			 const wheel_odometry_message_t *message,
			 uint32_t now_ms, wheel_speed_output_t *output);

/* Clear the output and PID state; caller must also stop the hardware. */
void motion_control_stop(motion_control_t *control);

#endif
=== C:\Users\Yong Jun\school\INF2004-Embedded_Systems\project\INF2004_Project\app_program\motion\motion_feedback.c ===
#include "motion_feedback.h"

#include <stddef.h>

bool motion_feedback_init(motion_feedback_t *feedback,
			  const motion_feedback_config_t *config)
{
	motion_feedback_t initial = {0};
	double distance;

	if (feedback == NULL || config == NULL || config->max_age_ms == 0U ||
	    config->max_age_ms > INT32_MAX || config->max_interval_ms == 0U ||
	    config->max_interval_ms > INT32_MAX ||
	    !wheel_odometry_distance_precise(&config->left, 0, &distance) ||
	    !wheel_odometry_distance_precise(&config->right, 0, &distance))
		return false;
	initial.config = *config;
	initial.initialized = true;
	*feedback = initial;
	return true;
}

motion_feedback_result_t motion_feedback_update(motion_feedback_t *feedback,
				 const wheel_odometry_message_t *message,
				 uint32_t now_ms, motion_wheel_speeds_t *speeds)
{
	motion_wheel_speeds_t next;
	wheel_odometry_result_t left, right;
	uint32_t left_count, right_count, elapsed;
	int32_t left_delta, right_delta;

	if (feedback == NULL || message == NULL || speeds == NULL ||
	    !feedback->initialized ||
	    message->header.type != VEHICLE_MESSAGE_WHEEL_ODOMETRY ||
	    message->header.version != VEHICLE_MESSAGE_VERSION || message->valid != 1U ||
	    now_ms - message->header.timestamp_ms > feedback->config.max_age_ms)
		return MOTION_FEEDBACK_INVALID;
	left_count = (uint32_t)message->left_count;
	right_count = (uint32_t)message->right_count;
	if (feedback->have_baseline) {
		elapsed = message->header.timestamp_ms - feedback->last_timestamp_ms;
		if (message->header.sequence - feedback->last_sequence != 1U ||
		    elapsed == 0U || elapsed > feedback->config.max_interval_ms ||
		    !wheel_odometry_count_delta(left_count, feedback->last_left, &left_delta) ||
		    !wheel_odometry_count_delta(right_count, feedback->last_right, &right_delta))
			return MOTION_FEEDBACK_INVALID;
		next.elapsed_seconds = (float)elapsed / 1000.0f;
		if (!wheel_odometry_calculate(&feedback->config.left, left_delta,
					       next.elapsed_seconds, &left) ||
		    !wheel_odometry_calculate(&feedback->config.right, right_delta,
					       next.elapsed_seconds, &right))
			return MOTION_FEEDBACK_INVALID;
		next.timestamp_ms = message->header.timestamp_ms;
		next.left_cm_per_second = left.speed_cm_per_second;
		next.right_cm_per_second = right.speed_cm_per_second;
	}
	feedback->last_left = left_count;
	feedback->last_right = right_count;
	feedback->last_sequence = message->header.sequence;
	feedback->last_timestamp_ms = message->header.timestamp_ms;
	if (!feedback->have_baseline) {
		feedback->have_baseline = true;
		return MOTION_FEEDBACK_BASELINE;
	}
	*speeds = next;
	return MOTION_FEEDBACK_READY;
}
=== C:\Users\Yong Jun\school\INF2004-Embedded_Systems\project\INF2004_Project\app_program\motion\motion_feedback.h ===
#ifndef MOTION_FEEDBACK_H
#define MOTION_FEEDBACK_H

#include "../odometry/wheel_odometry.h"
#include "../vehicle_messages.h"

typedef struct {
	wheel_odometry_config_t left;
	wheel_odometry_config_t right;
	uint32_t max_age_ms;
	uint32_t max_interval_ms;
} motion_feedback_config_t;

typedef struct {
	motion_feedback_config_t config;
	uint32_t last_left;
	uint32_t last_right;
	uint32_t last_sequence;
	uint32_t last_timestamp_ms;
	bool initialized;
	bool have_baseline;
} motion_feedback_t;

typedef struct {
	uint32_t timestamp_ms;
	float elapsed_seconds;
	/* Signed speeds after odometry's per-wheel polarity normalization. */
	float left_cm_per_second;
	float right_cm_per_second;
} motion_wheel_speeds_t;

typedef enum {
	MOTION_FEEDBACK_INVALID = 0,
	MOTION_FEEDBACK_BASELINE,
	MOTION_FEEDBACK_READY
} motion_feedback_result_t;

/* Calibration must be independently verified; the owner is the motion task. */
bool motion_feedback_init(motion_feedback_t *feedback,
			  const motion_feedback_config_t *config);

/* Messages must be fresh, valid and consecutive; timestamps and wheel counts
 * may wrap, but differences of half a counter range are ambiguous. The first
 * valid message establishes a baseline without changing speeds. On failure,
 * state and speeds are unchanged. The caller must stop on failure, check
 * direction and freshness, and reset the baseline when restarting control. */
motion_feedback_result_t motion_feedback_update(motion_feedback_t *feedback,
				 const wheel_odometry_message_t *message,
				 uint32_t now_ms, motion_wheel_speeds_t *speeds);

#endif

#include "../app.h"
#include "../vehicle_buffers.h"
#include "motor_driver.h"
#include "motion_task.h"

#define MOTION_TASK_PRIORITY 10
#define MOTION_FAULT_RETRY_MS 1000U

typedef struct {
	motion_control_t control;
	wheel_odometry_message_t pending;
	uint32_t last_clock_ms;
	uint32_t last_command_ms;
	uint32_t last_sequence;
	uint32_t issued_ms;
	uint32_t barrier_ms;
	uint32_t status_sequence;
	uint32_t last_status_ms;
	uint16_t duration_ms;
	uint16_t fault_code;
	int16_t velocity;
	int16_t motor_1_pwm;
	int16_t motor_2_pwm;
	bool have_command;
	bool have_pending;
	bool have_barrier;
	bool have_status;
	bool status_dirty;
	bool active;
	bool hardware_armed;
	bool faulted;
} motion_task_state_t;

static motion_task_config_t s_config;
static motion_task_state_t s_state;
static bool s_started;
static bool s_hardware_enabled;

#define MOTION_LEFT_MOTOR MOTOR_1
#define MOTION_RIGHT_MOTOR MOTOR_2
#define MOTION_LEFT_POLARITY 1
#define MOTION_RIGHT_POLARITY (-1)

static int16_t motor_command(uint8_t pwm, int8_t direction, int8_t polarity)
{
	return (int16_t)((int16_t)pwm * direction * polarity);
}

static void set_commanded_pwm(motion_task_state_t *state, int16_t motor_1,
			      int16_t motor_2)
{
	if (state->motor_1_pwm != motor_1 || state->motor_2_pwm != motor_2) {
		state->motor_1_pwm = motor_1;
		state->motor_2_pwm = motor_2;
		state->status_dirty = true;
	}
}

static void make_motion_status_message(const motion_task_state_t *state,
				       uint32_t sequence, uint32_t now,
				       motion_status_message_t *message)
{
	*message = (motion_status_message_t){0};
	message->header.type = VEHICLE_MESSAGE_MOTION_STATUS;
	message->header.version = VEHICLE_MESSAGE_VERSION;
	message->header.sequence = sequence;
	message->header.timestamp_ms = now;
	message->motor_1_pwm = state->motor_1_pwm;
	message->motor_2_pwm = state->motor_2_pwm;
	message->active = state->active ? 1U : 0U;
	message->faulted = state->faulted ? 1U : 0U;
	message->fault_code = state->faulted ? state->fault_code : 0U;
}

static uint16_t publish_motion_status(motion_task_state_t *state, uint32_t now)
{
	motion_status_message_t message;
	INT result;

	if (!s_hardware_enabled) return 0U;
	if (state->have_status && !state->status_dirty &&
	    now - state->last_status_ms < VEHICLE_MOTION_STATUS_PERIOD_MS)
		return 0U;
	make_motion_status_message(state, state->status_sequence + 1U, now, &message);
	result = tk_snd_mbf(g_mbf_motion_status, &message,
			    (INT)sizeof(message), TMO_POL);
	if (result != E_OK) {
		tm_printf((UB*)"motion status delivery failed: %d\n", (int)result);
		return VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_MOTION_STATUS;
	}
	state->status_sequence = message.header.sequence;
	state->last_status_ms = now;
	state->have_status = true;
	state->status_dirty = false;
	return 0U;
}

static void stop_motion(motion_task_state_t *state)
{
	motion_control_stop(&state->control);
	if (state->active) state->status_dirty = true;
	state->active = false;
	if (s_hardware_enabled) {
		ER er = motor_brake_all();
		if (er != E_OK)
			tm_printf((UB*)"motor brake failed: %d\n", (int)er);
		else
			set_commanded_pwm(state, 0, 0);
	} else {
		set_commanded_pwm(state, 0, 0);
	}
}

static ER apply_motor_output(motion_task_state_t *state,
			     const wheel_speed_output_t *output)
{
	ER left_er;
	ER right_er;
	int16_t motor_1;
	int16_t motor_2;

	if (!s_hardware_enabled) return E_OK;
	if (!state->hardware_armed || !state->active ||
	    !state->control.have_output)
	{
		ER er = motor_brake_all();
		if (er == E_OK) set_commanded_pwm(state, 0, 0);
		return er;
	}
	motor_1 = motor_command(output->left_pwm_percent,
				state->control.direction, MOTION_LEFT_POLARITY);
	motor_2 = motor_command(output->right_pwm_percent,
				state->control.direction, MOTION_RIGHT_POLARITY);
	left_er = motor_set_speed(MOTION_LEFT_MOTOR,
				  motor_1);
	right_er = motor_set_speed(MOTION_RIGHT_MOTOR,
				   motor_2);
	if (left_er != E_OK && left_er != E_BUSY) return left_er;
	if (right_er != E_OK && right_er != E_BUSY) return right_er;
	if (left_er == E_BUSY || right_er == E_BUSY) {
		ER er = motor_brake_all();
		if (er == E_OK) set_commanded_pwm(state, 0, 0);
		return er;
	}
	set_commanded_pwm(state, motor_1, motor_2);
	return E_OK;
}

static uint16_t discard_odometry(void)
{
	unsigned i;

	for (i = 0U; i < VEHICLE_MB_ODOMETRY_CAPACITY; i++) {
		wheel_odometry_message_t message;
		INT received = tk_rcv_mbf(g_mbf_motion_odometry, &message, TMO_POL);
		if (received == E_TMOUT) break;
		if (received != (INT)sizeof(message))
			return VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_MOTION_ODOMETRY;
	}
	return 0U;
}

static uint16_t receive_commands(motion_task_state_t *state,
				 const motion_task_config_t *config,
				 uint32_t now, bool suppress_motion)
{
	unsigned i;

	for (i = 0U; i < VEHICLE_MB_MOTION_CAPACITY; i++) {
		motion_command_message_t command;
		uint32_t age;
		int32_t speed;
		INT received = tk_rcv_mbf(g_mbf_motion_commands, &command, TMO_POL);
		if (received == E_TMOUT) break;
		if (received != (INT)sizeof(command) ||
		    command.header.type != VEHICLE_MESSAGE_MOTION_COMMAND ||
		    command.header.version != VEHICLE_MESSAGE_VERSION)
			return VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_MOTION_COMMAND;
		if (command.mode == VEHICLE_MOTION_EMERGENCY_STOP) {
			stop_motion(state);
			return VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_MOTION_EMERGENCY;
		}
		if (command.mode == VEHICLE_MOTION_STOP) {
			stop_motion(state);
			state->barrier_ms = now;
			state->have_barrier = true;
			suppress_motion = true;
		}
		age = now - command.header.timestamp_ms;
		if ((state->have_command &&
		     (command.header.sequence - state->last_sequence != 1U ||
		      command.header.timestamp_ms - state->last_command_ms > INT32_MAX)) ||
		    (command.mode != VEHICLE_MOTION_STOP && age > config->command_max_age_ms))
			return VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_MOTION_COMMAND;
		switch (command.mode) {
		case VEHICLE_MOTION_STOP:
			break;
		case VEHICLE_MOTION_ARM:
			if (!s_hardware_enabled || state->active ||
			    command.linear_velocity != 0 ||
			    command.angular_velocity != 0 ||
			    command.duration_ms != 0U)
				return VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_MOTION_COMMAND;
			if (motor_clear_emergency_stop() != E_OK)
				return VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_MOTION_ACTUATOR;
			state->hardware_armed = true;
			state->barrier_ms = command.header.timestamp_ms;
			state->have_barrier = true;
			break;
		case VEHICLE_MOTION_VELOCITY:
			speed = command.linear_velocity;
			if (speed < 0) speed = -speed;
			if (speed == 0 || speed > config->max_linear_speed_cm_s ||
			    command.angular_velocity != 0 || command.duration_ms == 0U ||
			    command.duration_ms > config->max_duration_ms ||
			    age >= command.duration_ms ||
			    (s_hardware_enabled && !state->hardware_armed))
				return VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_MOTION_COMMAND;
			if (!suppress_motion && (!state->have_barrier ||
			    (command.header.timestamp_ms - state->barrier_ms != 0U &&
			     command.header.timestamp_ms - state->barrier_ms <= INT32_MAX))) {
				if (state->active && state->velocity != command.linear_velocity)
					return VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_MOTION_COMMAND;
				if (!state->active) {
					motion_control_config_t control = config->control;
					control.speed.target_speed_cm_s = (float)speed;
					if (!motion_control_start(&state->control, &control,
					    command.linear_velocity > 0 ? 1 : -1, now))
						return VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_MOTION_CONTROL;
					state->velocity = command.linear_velocity;
					state->active = true;
					state->status_dirty = true;
				}
				state->issued_ms = command.header.timestamp_ms;
				state->duration_ms = command.duration_ms;
			}
			break;
		default:
			return VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_MOTION_COMMAND;
		}
		state->last_command_ms = command.header.timestamp_ms;
		state->last_sequence = command.header.sequence;
		state->have_command = true;
	}
	return 0U;
}

static uint16_t run_cycle(motion_task_state_t *state,
			  const motion_task_config_t *config, uint32_t now)
{
	wheel_speed_output_t output;
	motion_control_result_t control_result;
	uint16_t fault;
	bool expired = false;
	unsigned i;

	if (now - state->last_clock_ms > INT32_MAX)
		return VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_MOTION_CLOCK;
	state->last_clock_ms = now;
	if (state->active && now - state->issued_ms >= state->duration_ms) {
		stop_motion(state);
		state->barrier_ms = now;
		state->have_barrier = true;
		expired = true;
	}
	if (!state->active) {
		state->have_pending = false;
		fault = discard_odometry();
		if (fault != 0U) return fault;
	}
	fault = receive_commands(state, config, now, expired);
	if (fault != 0U) return fault;
	if (!state->active) {
		fault = discard_odometry();
		if (fault != 0U) return fault;
		return publish_motion_status(state, now);
	}
	for (i = 0U; i < VEHICLE_MB_ODOMETRY_CAPACITY + 1U; i++) {
		wheel_odometry_message_t message;
		uint32_t ahead;
		if (state->have_pending) {
			message = state->pending;
		} else {
			INT received = tk_rcv_mbf(g_mbf_motion_odometry, &message, TMO_POL);
			if (received == E_TMOUT) break;
			if (received != (INT)sizeof(message))
				return VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_MOTION_ODOMETRY;
		}
		ahead = message.header.timestamp_ms - now;
		if (ahead != 0U && ahead <= INT32_MAX) {
			state->pending = message;
			state->have_pending = true;
			break;
		}
		state->have_pending = false;
		if (motion_control_step(&state->control, &message, now, &output) ==
		    MOTION_CONTROL_FAULT)
			return VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_MOTION_ODOMETRY;
	}
	control_result = motion_control_step(&state->control, NULL, now, &output);
	if (control_result == MOTION_CONTROL_FAULT)
		return VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_MOTION_ODOMETRY;
	if (apply_motor_output(state, &output) != E_OK)
		return VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_MOTION_ACTUATOR;
	return publish_motion_status(state, now);
}

static void report_fault(uint16_t code, uint32_t now)
{
	fault_message_t fault = {0};
	bool sent = false;

	stop_motion(&s_state);
	if (s_hardware_enabled) {
		ER er = motor_emergency_stop();
		if (er != E_OK) {
			tm_printf((UB*)"motor emergency stop failed: %d\n", (int)er);
		} else {
			set_commanded_pwm(&s_state, 0, 0);
		}
	}
	s_state.faulted = true;
	s_state.fault_code = code;
	s_state.status_dirty = true;
	if (publish_motion_status(&s_state, now) != 0U)
		tm_printf((UB*)"motion fault status could not be queued\n");
	fault.header.type = VEHICLE_MESSAGE_FAULT;
	fault.header.version = VEHICLE_MESSAGE_VERSION;
	fault.header.sequence = 1U;
	fault.header.timestamp_ms = now;
	fault.source = VEHICLE_FAULT_MOTION;
	fault.code = code;
	fault.recoverable = 0U;
	tm_printf((UB*)"motion task fault; code %u\n", (unsigned)code);
	for (;;) {
		ER er;
		if (!sent) {
			er = tk_snd_mbf(g_mbf_faults, &fault, (INT)sizeof(fault), TMO_POL);
			if (er == E_OK) sent = true;
			else tm_printf((UB*)"motion fault delivery failed: %d\n", (int)er);
		}
		er = tk_dly_tsk(MOTION_FAULT_RETRY_MS);
		if (er != E_OK) {
			tm_printf((UB*)"motion fault retry delay failed: %d\n", (int)er);
			er = tk_slp_tsk(TMO_FEVR);
			tm_printf((UB*)"motion fault sleep returned: %d\n", (int)er);
		}
	}
}

static void motion_task(INT stacd, void *exinf)
{
	SYSTIM now;
	uint32_t timestamp = 0U;
	ER er;

	(void)stacd;
	(void)exinf;
	if (s_hardware_enabled) {
		er = motor_driver_init();
		if (er != E_OK) report_fault(VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_MOTION_ACTUATOR, timestamp);
	}
	er = tk_get_otm(&now);
	if (er != E_OK) report_fault(VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_MOTION_CLOCK, timestamp);
	timestamp = now.lo;
	s_state.last_clock_ms = timestamp;
	for (;;) {
		uint16_t fault;
		er = tk_get_otm(&now);
		if (er != E_OK) report_fault(VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_MOTION_CLOCK, timestamp);
		timestamp = now.lo;
		fault = run_cycle(&s_state, &s_config, timestamp);
		if (fault != 0U) report_fault(fault, timestamp);
		er = tk_dly_tsk(s_config.period_ms);
		if (er != E_OK) report_fault(VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_MOTION_SCHEDULE, timestamp);
	}
}

static ER motion_task_start_internal(const motion_task_config_t *config,
				     bool hardware_enabled)
{
	motion_control_t validation = {0};
	T_CTSK task = {0};
	ID id;
	ER er;

	if (s_started) return E_OBJ;
	if (config == NULL || config->period_ms == 0U ||
	    config->period_ms > config->control.feedback.max_age_ms ||
	    config->period_ms > config->control.feedback.max_interval_ms ||
	    config->command_max_age_ms == 0U || config->command_max_age_ms > INT32_MAX ||
	    config->max_duration_ms == 0U || config->max_linear_speed_cm_s == 0U ||
	    !motion_control_start(&validation, &config->control, 1, 0U))
		return E_PAR;
	if (g_mbf_motion_commands <= 0 || g_mbf_motion_odometry <= 0 ||
	    g_mbf_faults <= 0 ||
	    (hardware_enabled && g_mbf_motion_status <= 0))
		return E_OBJ;
	s_config = *config;
	s_state = (motion_task_state_t){0};
	s_hardware_enabled = hardware_enabled;
	s_state.status_dirty = hardware_enabled;
	task.task = (FP)motion_task;
	task.tskatr = TA_HLNG | TA_RNG3;
	task.itskpri = MOTION_TASK_PRIORITY;
	task.stksz = TASK_STACK_SIZE;
	id = tk_cre_tsk(&task);
	if (id < E_OK) {
		s_hardware_enabled = false;
		return id;
	}
	s_started = true;
	er = tk_sta_tsk(id, 0);
	if (er != E_OK) {
		ER cleanup = tk_del_tsk(id);
		if (cleanup != E_OK) tm_printf((UB*)"motion task cleanup failed: %d\n", (int)cleanup);
		else {
			s_started = false;
			s_hardware_enabled = false;
		}
		return er < E_OK ? er : E_SYS;
	}
	return E_OK;
}

ER motion_task_start(const motion_task_config_t *config)
{
	return motion_task_start_internal(config, false);
}

ER motion_task_start_hardware(const motion_task_config_t *config)
{
	return motion_task_start_internal(config, true);
}

#if APP_TEST
#include "../test_report.h"

static motion_command_message_t test_command(uint32_t sequence, uint32_t timestamp,
					      uint8_t mode, int16_t velocity,
					      uint16_t duration)
{
	motion_command_message_t command = {0};
	command.header.type = VEHICLE_MESSAGE_MOTION_COMMAND;
	command.header.version = VEHICLE_MESSAGE_VERSION;
	command.header.sequence = sequence;
	command.header.timestamp_ms = timestamp;
	command.mode = mode;
	command.linear_velocity = velocity;
	command.duration_ms = duration;
	return command;
}

static wheel_odometry_message_t test_odometry(uint32_t sequence, uint32_t timestamp,
					      int32_t count)
{
	wheel_odometry_message_t message = {0};
	message.header.type = VEHICLE_MESSAGE_WHEEL_ODOMETRY;
	message.header.version = VEHICLE_MESSAGE_VERSION;
	message.header.sequence = sequence;
	message.header.timestamp_ms = timestamp;
	message.left_count = count;
	message.right_count = count;
	message.valid = 1U;
	return message;
}

static BOOL send_command(const motion_command_message_t *command)
{
	return tk_snd_mbf(g_mbf_motion_commands, command, (INT)sizeof(*command), TMO_POL) == E_OK;
}

static BOOL send_odometry(const wheel_odometry_message_t *message)
{
	return tk_snd_mbf(g_mbf_motion_odometry, message, (INT)sizeof(*message), TMO_POL) == E_OK;
}

static BOOL drain_test_buffers(void)
{
	union {
		motion_command_message_t command;
		wheel_odometry_message_t odometry;
	} message;
	INT result;

	do {
		result = tk_rcv_mbf(g_mbf_motion_commands, &message, TMO_POL);
	} while (result > 0);
	if (result != E_TMOUT) return FALSE;
	return discard_odometry() == 0U;
}

static const motion_task_config_t test_config = {
	{{{2500U, 20.0f}, {2500U, 20.0f}, 150U, 250U},
	 {2.0f, 0.0f, 0.0f, 10.0f, 20U, 60U}},
	10U, 100U, 500U, 20U
};

static BOOL simulation_flow_test(void)
{
	motion_task_state_t state = {0};
	motion_command_message_t command;
	wheel_odometry_message_t odometry;
	BOOL ok;

	state.last_clock_ms = 100U;
	odometry = test_odometry(20U, 99U, 0);
	command = test_command(1U, 100U, VEHICLE_MOTION_VELOCITY, 10, 300U);
	ok = send_odometry(&odometry) && send_command(&command);
	ok = run_cycle(&state, &test_config, 100U) == 0U && state.active &&
	     !state.control.have_output && ok;
	odometry = test_odometry(21U, 101U, 0);
	ok = send_odometry(&odometry) && run_cycle(&state, &test_config, 100U) == 0U &&
	     state.have_pending && ok;
	ok = run_cycle(&state, &test_config, 101U) == 0U && !state.have_pending &&
	     state.control.feedback.have_baseline && ok;
	odometry = test_odometry(22U, 201U, 125);
	ok = send_odometry(&odometry) && run_cycle(&state, &test_config, 201U) == 0U &&
	     state.control.have_output && state.control.output.left_pwm_percent == 20U && ok;
	command = test_command(2U, 202U, VEHICLE_MOTION_VELOCITY, 10, 300U);
	ok = send_command(&command) && run_cycle(&state, &test_config, 202U) == 0U &&
	     state.issued_ms == 202U && state.control.have_output && ok;
	command = test_command(3U, 502U, VEHICLE_MOTION_VELOCITY, 10, 300U);
	ok = send_command(&command) && run_cycle(&state, &test_config, 502U) == 0U &&
	     !state.active && ok;
	command = test_command(4U, 503U, VEHICLE_MOTION_VELOCITY, -10, 300U);
	ok = send_command(&command) && run_cycle(&state, &test_config, 503U) == 0U &&
	     state.active && state.control.direction == -1 && ok;
	command = test_command(5U, 504U, VEHICLE_MOTION_STOP, 0, 0U);
	ok = send_command(&command) && ok;
	command = test_command(6U, 504U, VEHICLE_MOTION_VELOCITY, 10, 300U);
	ok = send_command(&command) && run_cycle(&state, &test_config, 504U) == 0U &&
	     !state.active && ok;
	command = test_command(7U, 504U, VEHICLE_MOTION_VELOCITY, 10, 300U);
	ok = send_command(&command) && run_cycle(&state, &test_config, 505U) == 0U &&
	     !state.active && ok;
	command = test_command(8U, 505U, VEHICLE_MOTION_VELOCITY, 10, 300U);
	ok = send_command(&command) && run_cycle(&state, &test_config, 505U) == 0U &&
	     state.active && ok;
	stop_motion(&state);
	return drain_test_buffers() && ok;
}

static BOOL simulation_fault_test(void)
{
	motion_task_state_t state = {0};
	motion_command_message_t command;
	wheel_odometry_message_t odometry;
	BOOL ok;

	state.last_clock_ms = 100U;
	command = test_command(1U, 100U, VEHICLE_MOTION_ARM, 0, 0U);
	ok = send_command(&command) &&
	     run_cycle(&state, &test_config, 100U) ==
	     VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_MOTION_COMMAND;
	command = test_command(1U, 100U, VEHICLE_MOTION_VELOCITY, 10, 100U);
	command.angular_velocity = 1;
	ok = send_command(&command) &&
	     run_cycle(&state, &test_config, 100U) == VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_MOTION_COMMAND && ok;
	command = test_command(1U, 99U, VEHICLE_MOTION_VELOCITY, 10, 1U);
	ok = send_command(&command) &&
	     run_cycle(&state, &test_config, 100U) == VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_MOTION_COMMAND && ok;
	command = test_command(1U, 101U, VEHICLE_MOTION_VELOCITY, 10, 100U);
	ok = send_command(&command) &&
	     run_cycle(&state, &test_config, 100U) == VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_MOTION_COMMAND && ok;
	command = test_command(1U, 100U, VEHICLE_MOTION_VELOCITY, 21, 100U);
	ok = send_command(&command) &&
	     run_cycle(&state, &test_config, 100U) == VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_MOTION_COMMAND && ok;
	command = test_command(1U, 100U, VEHICLE_MOTION_VELOCITY, 10, 300U);
	ok = send_command(&command) && run_cycle(&state, &test_config, 100U) == 0U && ok;
	command = test_command(3U, 101U, VEHICLE_MOTION_VELOCITY, 10, 300U);
	ok = send_command(&command) &&
	     run_cycle(&state, &test_config, 101U) == VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_MOTION_COMMAND && ok;
	command = test_command(9U, 0U, VEHICLE_MOTION_EMERGENCY_STOP, 0, 0U);
	ok = send_command(&command) &&
	     run_cycle(&state, &test_config, 101U) == VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_MOTION_EMERGENCY &&
	     !state.active && ok;
	state = (motion_task_state_t){0};
	state.last_clock_ms = 100U;
	command = test_command(1U, 100U, VEHICLE_MOTION_VELOCITY, 10, 300U);
	ok = send_command(&command) && run_cycle(&state, &test_config, 100U) == 0U && ok;
	odometry = test_odometry(1U, 100U, 0);
	ok = send_odometry(&odometry) && run_cycle(&state, &test_config, 100U) == 0U && ok;
	odometry = test_odometry(3U, 101U, 0);
	ok = send_odometry(&odometry) &&
	     run_cycle(&state, &test_config, 101U) == VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_MOTION_ODOMETRY && ok;
	stop_motion(&state);
	return drain_test_buffers() && ok;
}

static BOOL simulation_deadline_test(void)
{
	motion_task_state_t state = {0};
	motion_command_message_t command;
	wheel_odometry_message_t odometry;
	BOOL ok;

	state.last_clock_ms = UINT32_MAX - 10U;
	command = test_command(UINT32_MAX, UINT32_MAX - 10U,
			       VEHICLE_MOTION_VELOCITY, 10, 100U);
	ok = send_command(&command) &&
	     run_cycle(&state, &test_config, UINT32_MAX - 10U) == 0U && state.active;
	odometry = test_odometry(UINT32_MAX, UINT32_MAX - 10U, 0);
	ok = send_odometry(&odometry) &&
	     run_cycle(&state, &test_config, UINT32_MAX - 10U) == 0U && ok;
	odometry = test_odometry(0U, 39U, 62);
	ok = send_odometry(&odometry) && run_cycle(&state, &test_config, 39U) == 0U &&
	     state.control.have_output && ok;
	ok = run_cycle(&state, &test_config, 88U) == 0U && state.active && ok;
	ok = run_cycle(&state, &test_config, 89U) == 0U && !state.active &&
	     state.control.output.left_pwm_percent == 0U && ok;
	command = test_command(0U, 0U, VEHICLE_MOTION_VELOCITY, 10, 500U);
	ok = send_command(&command) &&
	     run_cycle(&state, &test_config, 101U) == VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_MOTION_COMMAND && ok;
	return drain_test_buffers() && ok;
}

static BOOL simulation_no_status_test(void)
{
	motion_task_state_t state = {0};
	motion_command_message_t command;
	motion_status_message_t status;
	BOOL ok;

	state.last_clock_ms = 100U;
	ok = run_cycle(&state, &test_config, 100U) == 0U &&
	     tk_rcv_mbf(g_mbf_motion_status, &status, TMO_POL) == E_TMOUT;
	command = test_command(1U, 100U, VEHICLE_MOTION_VELOCITY, 10, 300U);
	ok = send_command(&command) && run_cycle(&state, &test_config, 100U) == 0U &&
	     state.active && tk_rcv_mbf(g_mbf_motion_status, &status, TMO_POL) == E_TMOUT && ok;
	command = test_command(2U, 101U, VEHICLE_MOTION_STOP, 0, 0U);
	ok = send_command(&command) && run_cycle(&state, &test_config, 101U) == 0U &&
	     !state.active && tk_rcv_mbf(g_mbf_motion_status, &status, TMO_POL) == E_TMOUT && ok;
	stop_motion(&state);
	return drain_test_buffers() && ok;
}

static BOOL actuator_mapping_test(void)
{
	return motor_command(40U, 1, MOTION_LEFT_POLARITY) == 40 &&
	       motor_command(40U, 1, MOTION_RIGHT_POLARITY) == -40 &&
	       motor_command(40U, -1, MOTION_LEFT_POLARITY) == -40 &&
	       motor_command(40U, -1, MOTION_RIGHT_POLARITY) == 40;
}

static BOOL status_format_test(void)
{
	motion_task_state_t state = {0};
	motion_status_message_t status;

	state.active = true;
	state.motor_1_pwm = 40;
	state.motor_2_pwm = -40;
	make_motion_status_message(&state, 3U, 125U, &status);
	if (status.header.type != VEHICLE_MESSAGE_MOTION_STATUS ||
	    status.header.version != VEHICLE_MESSAGE_VERSION ||
	    status.header.sequence != 3U || status.header.timestamp_ms != 125U ||
	    status.active != 1U || status.faulted != 0U || status.fault_code != 0U ||
	    status.motor_1_pwm != 40 || status.motor_2_pwm != -40)
		return false;
	state.faulted = true;
	state.fault_code = VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_MOTION_ACTUATOR;
	make_motion_status_message(&state, 4U, 225U, &status);
	return status.faulted == 1U &&
	       status.fault_code == VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_MOTION_ACTUATOR &&
	       status.motor_1_pwm == 40 && status.motor_2_pwm == -40;
}

BOOL motion_task_test(void)
{
	BOOL flow_ok;
	BOOL fault_ok;
	BOOL deadline_ok;
	BOOL status_ok;
	BOOL mapping_ok;
	BOOL status_format_ok;
	TEST_INIT();

	flow_ok = simulation_flow_test();
	fault_ok = simulation_fault_test();
	deadline_ok = simulation_deadline_test();
	status_ok = simulation_no_status_test();
	mapping_ok = actuator_mapping_test();
	status_format_ok = status_format_test();
	TEST_CHECK("MOT-U07", flow_ok, "simulation command lifetime, feedback and stop precedence");
	TEST_CHECK("MOT-U08", fault_ok, "invalid commands, emergency and odometry faults");
	TEST_CHECK("MOT-U09", deadline_ok, "timestamp expiry and clock wrap");
	TEST_CHECK("MOT-U10", status_ok, "simulation does not publish motion status");
	TEST_CHECK("MOT-U11", mapping_ok, "forward and reverse commands preserve source motor polarity");
	TEST_CHECK("MOT-U12", status_format_ok, "hardware status reports commanded PWM and latched faults");
	TEST_SUMMARY("motion task simulation");
	return flow_ok && fault_ok && deadline_ok && status_ok && mapping_ok &&
	       status_format_ok;
}

#ifndef MOTION_TASK_H
#define MOTION_TASK_H

#include <tk/tkernel.h>
#include "motion_control.h"

typedef struct {
	motion_control_config_t control;
	RELTIM period_ms;
	uint32_t command_max_age_ms;
	uint16_t max_duration_ms;
	uint16_t max_linear_speed_cm_s;
} motion_task_config_t;

/* Simulation entry point: never accesses motors or publishes motion status. */
ER motion_task_start(const motion_task_config_t *config);

/* Hardware entry point: explicitly initializes the motor driver in its
 * braked/emergency-latched state. The controller must send a fresh ARM command
 * after its safety checks, followed by a fresh VELOCITY command. This is not
 * called by application startup. */
ER motion_task_start_hardware(const motion_task_config_t *config);

/* Both modes consume the dedicated motion and odometry buffers and neither
 * publishes motion status. The hardware mode uses the original motor polarity
 * mapping and requires independent freshness supervision; no physical stop is
 * guaranteed if this task stalls.
 *
 * VELOCITY accepts straight motion in signed cm/s; angular velocity must be
 * zero. Its nonzero duration is measured from the message timestamp. Hardware
 * mode requires ARM while stopped before VELOCITY; STOP does not re-arm.
 * STOP takes precedence over movement in the same queue batch; EMERGENCY_STOP
 * is latched until restart. A changed speed or direction requires STOP first.
 * The controller must issue consecutive sequence numbers, supervise stale
 * data independently, and handle faults. */

#if APP_TEST
BOOL motion_task_test(void);
#endif

#endif
=== C:\Users\Yong Jun\school\INF2004-Embedded_Systems\project\INF2004_Project\app_program\motion\motor_driver.c ===
#include "../app.h"
#include "motor_driver.h"

#include <tk/syslib.h>
#include <sys/sysdef.h>

#define MOTOR_1_A_PIN 8U
#define MOTOR_1_B_PIN 9U
#define MOTOR_2_A_PIN 10U
#define MOTOR_2_B_PIN 11U
#define MOTOR_PWM_FREQUENCY_HZ 20000U
#define MOTOR_REVERSE_BRAKE_US 50000U
#define MOTOR_PWM_DIVIDER 1U
#define MOTOR_PWM_DIV_REG(slice) \
	(PWM_BASE + PWM_CHx_DIV + ((slice) * 0x14U))
#define MOTOR_PWM_CC_REG(slice) \
	(PWM_BASE + PWM_CHx_CC + ((slice) * 0x14U))
#define MOTOR_PWM_SLICE(pin) (((pin) >> 1) & 0x07U)
#define MOTOR_CLOCK_HZ (TMCLK_MHz * 1000000U)

typedef struct {
	int8_t last_direction;
	bool braking;
	uint32_t brake_started_us;
} motor_state_t;

static const UINT s_motor_pins[4] = {
	MOTOR_1_A_PIN, MOTOR_1_B_PIN, MOTOR_2_A_PIN, MOTOR_2_B_PIN
};
static motor_state_t s_motor_states[2];
static ID s_owner_tid;
static uint16_t s_pwm_period_ticks;
static bool s_initialized;
static bool s_emergency_stopped = true;

static uint32_t timer_us(void)
{
	return in_w(TIMER_TIMELR);
}

static ER check_owner(void)
{
	if (s_owner_tid <= 0) return E_OBJ;
	return tk_get_tid() == s_owner_tid ? E_OK : E_CTX;
}

static bool valid_motor(motor_id_t motor)
{
	return motor == MOTOR_1 || motor == MOTOR_2;
}

static void write_pair(motor_id_t motor, uint16_t duty_a, uint16_t duty_b)
{
	UINT pin_a = motor == MOTOR_1 ? MOTOR_1_A_PIN : MOTOR_2_A_PIN;
	UINT slice = MOTOR_PWM_SLICE(pin_a);
	out_w(MOTOR_PWM_CC_REG(slice),
	      (UW)duty_a | ((UW)duty_b << 16));
}

static void brake(motor_id_t motor)
{
	motor_state_t *state = &s_motor_states[(UINT)motor];

	write_pair(motor, 0U, 0U);
	if (!state->braking) {
		state->braking = true;
		state->brake_started_us = timer_us();
	}
}

static ER safe_gpio_low(void)
{
	UINT i;
	ER result = E_OK;

	for (i = 0U; i < 4U; i++) {
		ER er = gpio_set_pin(s_motor_pins[i], GPIO_MODE_OUT);
		if (er < E_OK && result == E_OK) result = er;
		er = gpio_set_val(s_motor_pins[i], 0U);
		if (er < E_OK && result == E_OK) result = er;
	}
	return result;
}

static ER stop_pwm_slices(void)
{
	ER first = E_OK;
	ER er;

	er = pwm_set_enabled(MOTOR_1_A_PIN, FALSE);
	if (er < E_OK) first = er;
	er = pwm_set_enabled(MOTOR_2_A_PIN, FALSE);
	if (er < E_OK && first == E_OK) first = er;
	return first;
}

static ER initialization_failure(ER cause)
{
	ER cleanup;

	write_pair(MOTOR_1, 0U, 0U);
	write_pair(MOTOR_2, 0U, 0U);
	cleanup = stop_pwm_slices();
	if (cleanup < E_OK)
		tm_printf((UB*)"motor PWM cleanup failed: %d\n", (int)cleanup);
	cleanup = safe_gpio_low();
	if (cleanup < E_OK)
		tm_printf((UB*)"motor GPIO cleanup failed: %d\n", (int)cleanup);
	s_initialized = false;
	s_emergency_stopped = true;
	return cause;
}

ER motor_driver_init(void)
{
	ID tid = tk_get_tid();
	uint32_t period_ticks;
	UINT i;
	ER er;

	if (tid <= 0) return E_CTX;
	if (s_owner_tid > 0 && s_owner_tid != tid) return E_OBJ;
	if (s_initialized) return E_OBJ;
	s_owner_tid = tid;
	s_emergency_stopped = true;
	s_pwm_period_ticks = 0U;

	er = safe_gpio_low();
	if (er < E_OK) return initialization_failure(er);
	er = stop_pwm_slices();
	if (er < E_OK) return initialization_failure(er);

	period_ticks = (MOTOR_CLOCK_HZ + MOTOR_PWM_FREQUENCY_HZ - 1U) /
		       MOTOR_PWM_FREQUENCY_HZ;
	if (period_ticks < 2U || period_ticks > UINT16_MAX)
		return initialization_failure(E_SYS);
	s_pwm_period_ticks = (uint16_t)period_ticks;

	out_w(MOTOR_PWM_DIV_REG(MOTOR_PWM_SLICE(MOTOR_1_A_PIN)),
	      MOTOR_PWM_DIVIDER << 4);
	out_w(MOTOR_PWM_DIV_REG(MOTOR_PWM_SLICE(MOTOR_2_A_PIN)),
	      MOTOR_PWM_DIVIDER << 4);
	er = pwm_set_wrap(MOTOR_1_A_PIN, (UW)(s_pwm_period_ticks - 1U));
	if (er < E_OK) return initialization_failure(er);
	er = pwm_set_wrap(MOTOR_2_A_PIN, (UW)(s_pwm_period_ticks - 1U));
	if (er < E_OK) return initialization_failure(er);
	write_pair(MOTOR_1, 0U, 0U);
	write_pair(MOTOR_2, 0U, 0U);

	for (i = 0U; i < 4U; i++) {
		er = pwm_set_pin(s_motor_pins[i]);
		if (er < E_OK) return initialization_failure(er);
	}
	er = pwm_set_enabled(MOTOR_1_A_PIN, TRUE);
	if (er < E_OK) return initialization_failure(er);
	er = pwm_set_enabled(MOTOR_2_A_PIN, TRUE);
	if (er < E_OK) return initialization_failure(er);

	for (i = 0U; i < 2U; i++) {
		s_motor_states[i].last_direction = 0;
		s_motor_states[i].braking = true;
		s_motor_states[i].brake_started_us = timer_us();
	}
	s_initialized = true;
	return E_OK;
}

ER motor_set_speed(motor_id_t motor, int16_t speed_percent)
{
	motor_state_t *state;
	int8_t direction;
	uint16_t magnitude;
	uint16_t duty;
	ER er = check_owner();

	if (er < E_OK) return er;
	if (!s_initialized || s_emergency_stopped) return E_OBJ;
	if (!valid_motor(motor)) return E_PAR;
	if (speed_percent > 100) speed_percent = 100;
	else if (speed_percent < -100) speed_percent = -100;
	if (speed_percent == 0) {
		brake(motor);
		return E_OK;
	}

	state = &s_motor_states[(UINT)motor];
	direction = speed_percent > 0 ? 1 : -1;
	if (state->last_direction != 0 &&
	    direction != state->last_direction) {
		if (!state->braking) {
			brake(motor);
			return E_BUSY;
		}
		if (timer_us() - state->brake_started_us <
		    MOTOR_REVERSE_BRAKE_US)
			return E_BUSY;
	}

	magnitude = (uint16_t)(speed_percent < 0 ?
			      -speed_percent : speed_percent);
	duty = (uint16_t)(((uint32_t)magnitude * s_pwm_period_ticks) / 100U);
	if (direction > 0) write_pair(motor, duty, 0U);
	else write_pair(motor, 0U, duty);
	state->last_direction = direction;
	state->braking = false;
	return E_OK;
}

ER motor_brake_all(void)
{
	ER er = check_owner();

	if (er < E_OK) return er;
	if (!s_initialized) return E_OBJ;
	brake(MOTOR_1);
	brake(MOTOR_2);
	return E_OK;
}

ER motor_emergency_stop(void)
{
	ER er = check_owner();

	if (er < E_OK) return er;
	s_emergency_stopped = true;
	if (!s_initialized) return E_OBJ;
	brake(MOTOR_1);
	brake(MOTOR_2);
	return E_OK;
}

bool motor_is_emergency_stopped(void)
{
	return s_emergency_stopped;
}

ER motor_clear_emergency_stop(void)
{
	ER er = check_owner();

	if (er < E_OK) return er;
	if (!s_initialized) return E_OBJ;
	brake(MOTOR_1);
	brake(MOTOR_2);
	s_emergency_stopped = false;
	return E_OK;
}

typedef enum {
	MOTOR_1 = 0,
	MOTOR_2 = 1
} motor_id_t;

/* Call only from the motion task. Initialization leaves both motors braked
 * and emergency-stop latched; all driver calls require the initializing task. */

 * command after the controller authorizes re-arming. */

#include "pid_controller.h"

#include <math.h>
#include <stddef.h>

bool pid_init(pid_controller_t *pid,
              float kp,
              float ki,
              float kd,
              float output_min,
              float output_max)
{
    if (pid == NULL || !isfinite(kp) || !isfinite(ki) || !isfinite(kd) ||
        !isfinite(output_min) || !isfinite(output_max))
    {
        return false;
    }

    pid->kp = kp;
    pid->ki = ki;
    pid->kd = kd;

    if (output_min <= output_max)
    {
        pid->output_min = output_min;
        pid->output_max = output_max;
    }
    else
    {
        pid->output_min = output_max;
        pid->output_max = output_min;
    }

    return pid_reset(pid);
}

bool pid_reset(pid_controller_t *pid)
{
    if (pid == NULL)
    {
        return false;
    }

    pid->integral = 0.0f;
    pid->previous_error = 0.0f;
    pid->first_update = true;
    return true;
}

bool pid_update(pid_controller_t *pid,
                 float setpoint,
                 float measurement,
                 float dt_seconds,
                 float *result)
{
    if ((pid == NULL) ||
        (result == NULL) ||
        (!isfinite(pid->kp)) || (!isfinite(pid->ki)) || (!isfinite(pid->kd)) ||
        (!isfinite(pid->integral)) || (!isfinite(pid->previous_error)) ||
        (!isfinite(pid->output_min)) || (!isfinite(pid->output_max)) ||
        (pid->output_min > pid->output_max) ||
        (!isfinite(setpoint)) ||
        (!isfinite(measurement)) ||
        (!isfinite(dt_seconds)) ||
        (dt_seconds <= 0.0f))
    {
        return false;
    }

    float error = setpoint - measurement;

    float proportional = pid->kp * error;

    float derivative = 0.0f;

    if (!pid->first_update)
    {
        derivative =
            pid->kd *
            (error - pid->previous_error) /
            dt_seconds;
    }

    float candidate_integral =
        pid->integral + (error * dt_seconds);

    float candidate_output =
        proportional +
        (pid->ki * candidate_integral) +
        derivative;

    if (!isfinite(error) || !isfinite(proportional) ||
        !isfinite(derivative) || !isfinite(candidate_integral) ||
        !isfinite(candidate_output))
    {
        return false;
    }

    /*
     * Conditional integration:
     * Do not accumulate more integral error when
     * the output is saturated in the same direction.
     */
    bool allow_integration =
        ((candidate_output <= pid->output_max) &&
         (candidate_output >= pid->output_min)) ||
        ((candidate_output > pid->output_max) &&
         (error < 0.0f)) ||
        ((candidate_output < pid->output_min) &&
         (error > 0.0f));

    float output =
        proportional +
        (pid->ki * (allow_integration ? candidate_integral : pid->integral)) +
        derivative;

    if (!isfinite(output))
    {
        return false;
    }

    if (output > pid->output_max)
    {
        output = pid->output_max;
    }
    else if (output < pid->output_min)
    {
        output = pid->output_min;
    }

    if (allow_integration)
    {
        pid->integral = candidate_integral;
    }
    pid->previous_error = error;
    pid->first_update = false;

    *result = output;
    return true;
}
=== C:\Users\Yong Jun\school\INF2004-Embedded_Systems\project\INF2004_Project\app_program\motion\pid_controller.h ===

#ifndef PID_CONTROLLER_H
#define PID_CONTROLLER_H

#include <stdbool.h>

typedef struct
{
    float kp;
    float ki;
    float kd;

    float integral;
    float previous_error;

    float output_min;
    float output_max;

    bool first_update;
} pid_controller_t;

/* Initialise a PID controller with finite gains and output limits. */
bool pid_init(pid_controller_t *pid,
              float kp,
              float ki,
              float kd,
              float output_min,
              float output_max);

/* False means invalid input or arithmetic; state and output are unchanged. */
bool pid_update(pid_controller_t *pid,
                 float setpoint,
                 float measurement,
                 float dt_seconds,
                 float *output);

/* Reset accumulated state when stopping or restarting. */
bool pid_reset(pid_controller_t *pid);

#endif
=== C:\Users\Yong Jun\school\INF2004-Embedded_Systems\project\INF2004_Project\app_program\motion\wheel_speed_control.c ===

#include "wheel_speed_control.h"

#include <math.h>
#include <stddef.h>

/*
 * Buddy 2 - Dual Wheel PID Speed Controller
 *
 * Produces unsigned PWM magnitudes.
 * Motor directions and emergency stopping remain
 * the responsibility of the motion-control task.
 */

#define MAX_CONTROL_INTERVAL_SECONDS 0.25f

static bool valid_config(
    const wheel_speed_config_t *config)
{
    if (config == NULL)
    {
        return false;
    }

    if (!isfinite(config->kp) ||
        !isfinite(config->ki) ||
        !isfinite(config->kd) ||
        !isfinite(config->target_speed_cm_s))
    {
        return false;
    }

    if ((config->kp < 0.0f) ||
        (config->ki < 0.0f) ||
        (config->kd < 0.0f) ||
        (config->target_speed_cm_s <= 0.0f))
    {
        return false;
    }

    if ((config->base_pwm_percent == 0U) ||
        (config->max_pwm_percent == 0U) ||
        (config->max_pwm_percent > 100U) ||
        (config->base_pwm_percent >
         config->max_pwm_percent))
    {
        return false;
    }

    return true;
}

static uint8_t calculate_pwm(
    float base_pwm,
    float correction,
    uint8_t maximum)
{
    float command = base_pwm + correction;

    if (command < 0.0f)
    {
        command = 0.0f;
    }

    if (command > (float)maximum)
    {
        command = (float)maximum;
    }

    return (uint8_t)(command + 0.5f);
}

bool wheel_speed_control_init(
    wheel_speed_control_t *controller,
    const wheel_speed_config_t *config)
{
    if (controller == NULL)
    {
        return false;
    }

    controller->initialized = false;

    if (!valid_config(config))
    {
        return false;
    }

    controller->config = *config;

    /*
     * Limit PID correction so base + correction
     * always remains within the configured
     * motor PWM range.
     */
    float minimum_correction =
        -(float)config->base_pwm_percent;

    float maximum_correction =
        (float)config->max_pwm_percent -
        (float)config->base_pwm_percent;

    if (!pid_init(
        &controller->left_pid,
        config->kp,
        config->ki,
        config->kd,
        minimum_correction,
        maximum_correction))
    {
        return false;
    }

    if (!pid_init(
        &controller->right_pid,
        config->kp,
        config->ki,
        config->kd,
        minimum_correction,
        maximum_correction))
    {
        return false;
    }

    controller->initialized = true;

    return true;
}

bool wheel_speed_control_update(
    wheel_speed_control_t *controller,
    float left_speed_cm_s,
    float right_speed_cm_s,
    float dt_seconds,
    wheel_speed_output_t *output)
{
    if (output == NULL)
    {
        if (controller != NULL && controller->initialized)
        {
            (void)wheel_speed_control_reset(controller);
        }
        return false;
    }

    /* Safe result whenever an update fails. */
    output->left_pwm_percent = 0U;
    output->right_pwm_percent = 0U;

    if ((controller == NULL) ||
        (!controller->initialized))
    {
        return false;
    }

    if (!isfinite(left_speed_cm_s) ||
        !isfinite(right_speed_cm_s) ||
        !isfinite(dt_seconds))
    {
        (void)wheel_speed_control_reset(controller);
        return false;
    }

    if ((left_speed_cm_s < 0.0f) ||
        (right_speed_cm_s < 0.0f) ||
        (dt_seconds <= 0.0f) ||
        (dt_seconds > MAX_CONTROL_INTERVAL_SECONDS))
    {
        (void)wheel_speed_control_reset(controller);
        return false;
    }

    float target =
        controller->config.target_speed_cm_s;

    float left_correction;
    float right_correction;

    if (!pid_update(
        &controller->left_pid,
        target,
        left_speed_cm_s,
        dt_seconds,
        &left_correction) ||
        !pid_update(
        &controller->right_pid,
        target,
        right_speed_cm_s,
        dt_seconds,
        &right_correction))
    {
        (void)wheel_speed_control_reset(controller);
        return false;
    }

    float base =
        (float)controller->config.base_pwm_percent;

    uint8_t maximum =
        controller->config.max_pwm_percent;

    output->left_pwm_percent = calculate_pwm(
        base, left_correction, maximum);

    output->right_pwm_percent = calculate_pwm(
        base, right_correction, maximum);

    return true;
}

bool wheel_speed_control_reset(
    wheel_speed_control_t *controller)
{
    if ((controller == NULL) ||
        (!controller->initialized))
    {
        return false;
    }

    return pid_reset(&controller->left_pid) &&
           pid_reset(&controller->right_pid);
}
=== C:\Users\Yong Jun\school\INF2004-Embedded_Systems\project\INF2004_Project\app_program\motion\wheel_speed_control.h ===

#ifndef WHEEL_SPEED_CONTROL_H
#define WHEEL_SPEED_CONTROL_H

#include <stdbool.h>
#include <stdint.h>

#include "pid_controller.h"

/*
 * Buddy 2 - Closed-Loop Wheel Speed Control
 *
 * Calculates PWM commands using two PID
 * controllers and measured wheel speeds.
 *
 * No direct motor hardware access.
 *
 * Target and measured speeds are expressed
 * as positive forward-equivalent magnitudes
 * in cm/s.
 *
 * Physical motor direction is handled by
 * the motion controller separately.
 */

typedef struct
{
    float kp;
    float ki;
    float kd;

    float target_speed_cm_s;

    uint8_t base_pwm_percent;
    uint8_t max_pwm_percent;
} wheel_speed_config_t;

typedef struct
{
    pid_controller_t left_pid;
    pid_controller_t right_pid;

    wheel_speed_config_t config;

    bool initialized;
} wheel_speed_control_t;

typedef struct
{
    uint8_t left_pwm_percent;
    uint8_t right_pwm_percent;
} wheel_speed_output_t;

/*
 * Initialise both wheel PID controllers.
 *
 * Rejects invalid or non-finite settings.
 */
bool wheel_speed_control_init(
    wheel_speed_control_t *controller,
    const wheel_speed_config_t *config);

/*
 * Compute corrected PWM values.
 *
 * Measured speeds must be valid
 * non-negative wheel-speed magnitudes.
 *
 * dt_seconds must be greater than zero.
 *
 * Returns false without issuing motor
 * commands if input validation fails. A failed
 * update zeros the output and resets both PIDs;
 * the caller must stop the motors.
 */
bool wheel_speed_control_update(
    wheel_speed_control_t *controller,
    float left_speed_cm_s,
    float right_speed_cm_s,
    float dt_seconds,
    wheel_speed_output_t *output);

/*
 * Reset both PID controllers.
 *
 * Call whenever movement stops,
 * changes direction or is restarted.
 */
bool wheel_speed_control_reset(
    wheel_speed_control_t *controller);

#endif

#include "../app.h"
#include "wheel_speed_test.h"
