#ifndef ENCODER_SOURCE_H
#define ENCODER_SOURCE_H

#include <tk/tkernel.h>
#include "encoder_pair.h"

/* Register once from the core-0 startup owner, before the shared dispatcher
 * starts. The wheels must remain stationary through dispatcher startup. */
ER encoder_source_register(void);

/* Call only from a core-0-pinned task after dispatcher startup. A false valid
 * flag is latched until restart; counts are then diagnostic only. */
ER encoder_source_snapshot(encoder_snapshot_t *snapshot);

=== C:\Users\Yong Jun\school\INF2004-Embedded_Systems\project\INF2004_Project\app_program\odometry\odometry_producer.c ===
#include "odometry_producer.h"

#include <limits.h>
#include <math.h>
#include <stddef.h>

static uint32_t signed_bits(int32_t count, int8_t sign)
{
	uint32_t bits = (uint32_t)count;
	return (sign > 0) ? bits : 0U - bits;
}

static int32_t bits_to_signed(uint32_t bits)
{
	return (bits <= INT32_MAX) ? (int32_t)bits :
	       INT32_MIN + (int32_t)(bits - 0x80000000U);
}

static bool add_delta(int64_t total, int32_t delta, int64_t *result)
{
	if ((delta > 0 && total > INT64_MAX - delta) ||
	    (delta < 0 && total < INT64_MIN - delta)) return false;
	*result = total + delta;
	return true;
}

bool odometry_producer_init(odometry_producer_t *producer,
                            const odometry_producer_config_t *config,
                            int32_t left_count, int32_t right_count,
                            uint32_t timestamp_ms)
{
	double distance;
	odometry_producer_t initial = {0};

	if (producer == NULL || config == NULL ||
	    (config->left_sign != 1 && config->left_sign != -1) ||
	    (config->right_sign != 1 && config->right_sign != -1) ||
	    !wheel_odometry_distance_precise(&config->left, 0, &distance) ||
	    !wheel_odometry_distance_precise(&config->right, 0, &distance)) return false;
	initial.config = *config;
	initial.last_left = signed_bits(left_count, config->left_sign);
	initial.last_right = signed_bits(right_count, config->right_sign);
	initial.last_timestamp_ms = timestamp_ms;
	initial.initialized = true;
	*producer = initial;
	return true;
}

bool odometry_producer_update(odometry_producer_t *producer,
                              int32_t left_count, int32_t right_count,
                              uint32_t timestamp_ms,
                              wheel_odometry_message_t *message)
{
	uint32_t elapsed;
	uint32_t left_bits, right_bits;
	int32_t left_delta, right_delta;
	int64_t left_total, right_total;
	double left_cm, right_cm;
	double distance_mm;
	wheel_odometry_message_t next = {0};

	if (producer == NULL || message == NULL || !producer->initialized)
		return false;
	elapsed = timestamp_ms - producer->last_timestamp_ms;
	if (elapsed == 0U || elapsed > INT32_MAX) return false;
	left_bits = signed_bits(left_count, producer->config.left_sign);
	right_bits = signed_bits(right_count, producer->config.right_sign);
	if (!wheel_odometry_count_delta(left_bits, producer->last_left, &left_delta) ||
	    !wheel_odometry_count_delta(right_bits, producer->last_right, &right_delta) ||
	    !add_delta(producer->left_total, left_delta, &left_total) ||
	    !add_delta(producer->right_total, right_delta, &right_total) ||
	    !wheel_odometry_distance_precise(&producer->config.left, left_total, &left_cm) ||
	    !wheel_odometry_distance_precise(&producer->config.right, right_total, &right_cm))
		return false;

	distance_mm = (left_cm + right_cm) * 5.0;
	if (!isfinite(distance_mm) || distance_mm > INT32_MAX ||
	    distance_mm < INT32_MIN) return false;

	next.header.type = VEHICLE_MESSAGE_WHEEL_ODOMETRY;
	next.header.version = VEHICLE_MESSAGE_VERSION;
	next.header.sequence = producer->sequence + 1U;
	next.header.timestamp_ms = timestamp_ms;
	next.left_count = bits_to_signed(left_bits);
	next.right_count = bits_to_signed(right_bits);
	next.distance_mm = (int32_t)distance_mm;
	next.valid = 1U;

	producer->last_left = left_bits;
	producer->last_right = right_bits;
	producer->left_total = left_total;
	producer->right_total = right_total;
	producer->last_timestamp_ms = timestamp_ms;
	producer->sequence = next.header.sequence;
	*message = next;
	return true;
}
=== C:\Users\Yong Jun\school\INF2004-Embedded_Systems\project\INF2004_Project\app_program\odometry\odometry_producer.h ===
#ifndef ODOMETRY_PRODUCER_H
#define ODOMETRY_PRODUCER_H

#include "wheel_odometry.h"
#include "../vehicle_messages.h"

typedef struct {
	wheel_odometry_config_t left;
	wheel_odometry_config_t right;
	int8_t left_sign;
	int8_t right_sign;
} odometry_producer_config_t;

typedef struct {
	odometry_producer_config_t config;
	uint32_t last_left;
	uint32_t last_right;
	int64_t left_total;
	int64_t right_total;
	uint32_t last_timestamp_ms;
	uint32_t sequence;
	bool initialized;
} odometry_producer_t;

/* Calibration must be supplied by the owning task; signs are +1 or -1.
 * The initial snapshot defines zero distance. Input counts are wrapping
 * encoder values; output counts are polarity-normalized absolute counts.
 * Call only from the single owning task. */
bool odometry_producer_init(odometry_producer_t *producer,
                            const odometry_producer_config_t *config,
                            int32_t left_count, int32_t right_count,
                            uint32_t timestamp_ms);

/* On failure neither state nor message changes. Samples must be ordered:
 * consecutive timestamps and per-wheel count changes must be less than
 * half their respective 32-bit wrap periods. The owner must report failures
 * and consumers must check timestamp freshness and sequence gaps. */
bool odometry_producer_update(odometry_producer_t *producer,
                              int32_t left_count, int32_t right_count,
                              uint32_t timestamp_ms,
                              wheel_odometry_message_t *message);

#endif

#include "../app.h"

static BOOL calculation_test(void)
{
	odometry_producer_t state;
	const odometry_producer_config_t config = {{2500U, 20.0f}, {2500U, 20.0f}, 1, -1};
	wheel_odometry_message_t message;

	return odometry_producer_init(&state, &config, 0, 0, 10U) &&
	       odometry_producer_update(&state, 2500, -2500, 110U, &message) &&
	       message.header.type == VEHICLE_MESSAGE_WHEEL_ODOMETRY &&
	       message.header.version == VEHICLE_MESSAGE_VERSION &&
	       message.header.sequence == 1U && message.header.timestamp_ms == 110U &&
	       message.left_count == 2500 && message.right_count == 2500 &&
	       message.distance_mm == 200 && message.valid == 1U &&
	       odometry_producer_update(&state, 1250, -1250, 210U, &message) &&
	       message.header.sequence == 2U && message.distance_mm == 100;
}

static BOOL boundary_test(void)
{
	odometry_producer_t state;
	odometry_producer_config_t config = {{2500U, 20.0f}, {2500U, 20.0f}, 1, -1};
	wheel_odometry_message_t message = {0};

	if (!odometry_producer_init(&state, &config, INT32_MAX - 1, INT32_MIN + 2,
	                            UINT32_MAX - 50U) ||
	    !odometry_producer_update(&state, INT32_MIN + 1, INT32_MAX, 49U, &message) ||
	    state.left_total != 3 || state.right_total != 3 ||
	    message.left_count != INT32_MIN + 1 || message.right_count != INT32_MIN + 1)
		return false;
	if (odometry_producer_update(&state, INT32_MIN + 1, INT32_MAX, 49U, &message) ||
	    odometry_producer_update(&state, INT32_MIN + 1, INT32_MAX, 48U, &message) ||
	    state.sequence != 1U || message.header.sequence != 1U)
		return false;
	if (odometry_producer_update(&state, 1, INT32_MAX, 50U, &message) ||
	    state.sequence != 1U || message.header.sequence != 1U)
		return false;
	config.right_sign = 0;
	if (odometry_producer_init(&state, &config, 0, 0, 0U)) return false;
	config.right_sign = -1;
	config.left.counts_per_revolution = 0U;
	if (odometry_producer_init(&state, &config, 0, 0, 0U)) return false;
	config.left.counts_per_revolution = 2500U;
	if (!odometry_producer_init(&state, &config, 0, 0, 0U)) return false;
	state.left_total = INT64_MAX;
	if (odometry_producer_update(&state, 1, 0, 1U, &message) ||
	    state.left_total != INT64_MAX || state.sequence != 0U)
		return false;
	state.left_total = INT64_MAX / 2;
	state.right_total = INT64_MAX / 2;
	if (odometry_producer_update(&state, 1, -1, 1U, &message) ||
	    state.left_total != INT64_MAX / 2 || state.sequence != 0U)
		return false;
	config.left.counts_per_revolution = 1U;
	config.right.counts_per_revolution = 1U;
	config.left.wheel_circumference_cm = 1.0f;
	config.right.wheel_circumference_cm = 1.0f;
	return odometry_producer_init(&state, &config, 0, 0, 0U) &&
	       odometry_producer_update(&state, 16777217, -16777217, 1U, &message) &&
	       message.distance_mm == 167772170;
}

static BOOL drain_buffer(ID buffer)
{
	wheel_odometry_message_t message;
	INT result;
	do {
		result = tk_rcv_mbf(buffer, &message, TMO_POL);
	} while (result > 0);
	return result == E_TMOUT;
}

static BOOL publish_test(void)
{
	wheel_odometry_message_t sent = {0}, line = {0}, imu = {0};
	odometry_producer_t producer;
	const odometry_producer_config_t config = {{2500U, 20.0f}, {2500U, 20.0f}, 1, -1};
	odometry_publish_result_t result;
	BOOL ok;
	unsigned i;

	if (!odometry_producer_init(&producer, &config, 0, 0, 0U) ||
	    !odometry_producer_update(&producer, 2500, -2500, 100U, &sent))
		return FALSE;
	result = odometry_publish(&sent);
	sent.left_count = 0;
	sent.distance_mm = 0;
	ok = result.line == E_OK && result.imu == E_OK;
	ok = tk_rcv_mbf(g_mbf_line_odometry, &line, TMO_POL) == (INT)sizeof(line) && ok;
	ok = tk_rcv_mbf(g_mbf_imu_odometry, &imu, TMO_POL) == (INT)sizeof(imu) && ok;
	ok = ok && line.header.sequence == 1U && imu.header.sequence == 1U &&
	     line.left_count == 2500 && imu.left_count == 2500 &&
	     line.distance_mm == 200 && imu.distance_mm == 200;

	for (i = 0U; i < VEHICLE_MB_ODOMETRY_CAPACITY; i++) {
		if (tk_snd_mbf(g_mbf_line_odometry, &sent, (INT)sizeof(sent), TMO_POL) != E_OK)
			ok = FALSE;
	}
	result = odometry_publish(&sent);
	ok = result.line == E_TMOUT && result.imu == E_OK && ok;
	ok = tk_rcv_mbf(g_mbf_imu_odometry, &imu, TMO_POL) == (INT)sizeof(imu) && ok;
	ok = drain_buffer(g_mbf_line_odometry) && ok;
	for (i = 0U; i < VEHICLE_MB_ODOMETRY_CAPACITY; i++) {
		if (tk_snd_mbf(g_mbf_imu_odometry, &sent, (INT)sizeof(sent), TMO_POL) != E_OK)
			ok = FALSE;
	}
	result = odometry_publish(&sent);
	ok = result.line == E_OK && result.imu == E_TMOUT && ok;
	ok = tk_rcv_mbf(g_mbf_line_odometry, &line, TMO_POL) == (INT)sizeof(line) && ok;
	sent.valid = 0U;
	result = odometry_publish(&sent);
	ok = result.line == E_PAR && result.imu == E_PAR && ok;
	result = odometry_publish(NULL);
	ok = result.line == E_PAR && result.imu == E_PAR && ok;
	ok = drain_buffer(g_mbf_line_odometry) && ok;
	ok = drain_buffer(g_mbf_imu_odometry) && ok;
	return ok;
}

static BOOL motion_publish_test(void)
{
	wheel_odometry_message_t sent = {0}, line, imu, motion;
	odometry_publish_result_t result;
	BOOL ok;
	unsigned i;

	sent.header.type = VEHICLE_MESSAGE_WHEEL_ODOMETRY;
	sent.header.version = VEHICLE_MESSAGE_VERSION;
	sent.header.sequence = 7U;
	sent.left_count = 123;
	sent.valid = 1U;
	result = odometry_publish(&sent);
	ok = result.line == E_OK && result.imu == E_OK && !result.motion_attempted;
	ok = tk_rcv_mbf(g_mbf_line_odometry, &line, TMO_POL) == (INT)sizeof(line) && ok;
	ok = tk_rcv_mbf(g_mbf_imu_odometry, &imu, TMO_POL) == (INT)sizeof(imu) && ok;
	ok = tk_rcv_mbf(g_mbf_motion_odometry, &motion, TMO_POL) == E_TMOUT && ok;
	result = odometry_publish_with_motion(&sent);
	sent.left_count = 456;
	ok = result.line == E_OK && result.imu == E_OK && result.motion_attempted &&
	     result.motion == E_OK && ok;
	ok = tk_rcv_mbf(g_mbf_line_odometry, &line, TMO_POL) == (INT)sizeof(line) && ok;
	ok = tk_rcv_mbf(g_mbf_imu_odometry, &imu, TMO_POL) == (INT)sizeof(imu) && ok;
	ok = tk_rcv_mbf(g_mbf_motion_odometry, &motion, TMO_POL) == (INT)sizeof(motion) &&
	     line.left_count == 123 && imu.left_count == 123 && motion.left_count == 123 && ok;
	for (i = 0U; i < VEHICLE_MB_ODOMETRY_CAPACITY; i++) {
		if (tk_snd_mbf(g_mbf_motion_odometry, &sent, (INT)sizeof(sent), TMO_POL) != E_OK)
			ok = FALSE;
	}
	result = odometry_publish_with_motion(&sent);
	ok = result.line == E_OK && result.imu == E_OK && result.motion_attempted &&
	     result.motion == E_TMOUT && ok;
	ok = tk_rcv_mbf(g_mbf_line_odometry, &line, TMO_POL) == (INT)sizeof(line) && ok;
	ok = tk_rcv_mbf(g_mbf_imu_odometry, &imu, TMO_POL) == (INT)sizeof(imu) && ok;
	ok = drain_buffer(g_mbf_motion_odometry) && ok;
	for (i = 0U; i < VEHICLE_MB_ODOMETRY_CAPACITY; i++) {
		if (tk_snd_mbf(g_mbf_line_odometry, &sent, (INT)sizeof(sent), TMO_POL) != E_OK)
			ok = FALSE;
	}
	result = odometry_publish_with_motion(&sent);
	ok = result.line == E_TMOUT && result.imu == E_OK &&
	     result.motion_attempted && result.motion == E_OK && ok;
	ok = tk_rcv_mbf(g_mbf_imu_odometry, &imu, TMO_POL) == (INT)sizeof(imu) && ok;
	ok = tk_rcv_mbf(g_mbf_motion_odometry, &motion, TMO_POL) == (INT)sizeof(motion) && ok;
	sent.valid = 0U;
	result = odometry_publish_with_motion(&sent);
	ok = result.line == E_PAR && result.imu == E_PAR &&
	     !result.motion_attempted && ok;
	ok = drain_buffer(g_mbf_line_odometry) && ok;
	ok = drain_buffer(g_mbf_imu_odometry) && ok;
	ok = drain_buffer(g_mbf_motion_odometry) && ok;
	return ok;
}

	BOOL boundary_ok;

	TEST_CHECK("INT-05", motion_ok, "optional motion odometry delivery");
	TEST_SUMMARY("odometry producer");
	return calculation_ok && boundary_ok && publish_ok && motion_ok;
}

#include "../app.h"
#include "../vehicle_buffers.h"
#include "odometry_publish.h"

static odometry_publish_result_t publish(const wheel_odometry_message_t *message,
					 bool include_motion)
{
	odometry_publish_result_t result = {E_PAR, E_PAR, E_PAR, false};

	if (message == NULL || message->header.type != VEHICLE_MESSAGE_WHEEL_ODOMETRY ||
	    message->header.version != VEHICLE_MESSAGE_VERSION || message->valid != 1U)
		return result;
	result.line = tk_snd_mbf(g_mbf_line_odometry, message,
	                         (INT)sizeof(*message), TMO_POL);
	result.imu = tk_snd_mbf(g_mbf_imu_odometry, message,
	                        (INT)sizeof(*message), TMO_POL);
	if (include_motion) {
		result.motion_attempted = true;
		result.motion = tk_snd_mbf(g_mbf_motion_odometry, message,
					   (INT)sizeof(*message), TMO_POL);
	}
	return result;
}

odometry_publish_result_t odometry_publish(const wheel_odometry_message_t *message)
{
	return publish(message, false);
}

odometry_publish_result_t odometry_publish_with_motion(const wheel_odometry_message_t *message)
{
	return publish(message, true);
}
=== C:\Users\Yong Jun\school\INF2004-Embedded_Systems\project\INF2004_Project\app_program\odometry\odometry_publish.h ===
#ifndef ODOMETRY_PUBLISH_H
#define ODOMETRY_PUBLISH_H

#include <tk/tkernel.h>
#include "../vehicle_messages.h"

typedef struct {
	ER line;
	ER imu;
	ER motion;
	bool motion_attempted;
} odometry_publish_result_t;

/* Line and IMU sends are attempted independently. Motion is not sent by this
 * function; motion_attempted is false and motion is not a delivery result.
 * The caller must handle
 * each result, including a full buffer; this function never discards messages. */
odometry_publish_result_t odometry_publish(const wheel_odometry_message_t *message);

/* Also attempts the dedicated motion buffer for a valid message. All sends
 * are non-blocking and independent, so partial delivery is possible. */
odometry_publish_result_t odometry_publish_with_motion(const wheel_odometry_message_t *message);

#endif
=== C:\Users\Yong Jun\school\INF2004-Embedded_Systems\project\INF2004_Project\app_program\odometry\odometry_task.c ===
#include "../app.h"
#include "../vehicle_buffers.h"
#include "encoder_source.h"
#include "odometry_publish.h"
#include "odometry_task.h"

#define ODOMETRY_TASK_PRIORITY 10
#define ODOMETRY_FAULT_RETRY_MS 1000U

static odometry_producer_config_t s_config;
static RELTIM s_period_ms;
static bool s_started;
static bool s_publish_motion;

static uint16_t process_snapshot(odometry_producer_t *producer,
				 const encoder_snapshot_t *snapshot,
				 uint32_t timestamp_ms,
				 bool publish_motion,
				 odometry_publish_result_t *delivery)
{
	wheel_odometry_message_t message;

	if (!snapshot->valid) return VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_ODOMETRY_SOURCE;
	if (!odometry_producer_update(producer, snapshot->left_count,
				      snapshot->right_count, timestamp_ms, &message))
		return VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_ODOMETRY_CALCULATION;
	*delivery = publish_motion ? odometry_publish_with_motion(&message) :
				    odometry_publish(&message);
	if (delivery->line != E_OK || delivery->imu != E_OK ||
	    (publish_motion && (!delivery->motion_attempted || delivery->motion != E_OK)))
		return VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_ODOMETRY_DELIVERY;
	return 0U;
}

static void report_fault(uint16_t code, uint32_t timestamp_ms)
{
	fault_message_t fault = {0};
	bool sent = false;

	fault.header.type = VEHICLE_MESSAGE_FAULT;
	fault.header.version = VEHICLE_MESSAGE_VERSION;
	fault.header.sequence = 1U;
	fault.header.timestamp_ms = timestamp_ms;
	fault.source = code == VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_ODOMETRY_DELIVERY ?
		       VEHICLE_FAULT_COMMUNICATION :
		       (code == VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_ODOMETRY_CLOCK ||
			code == VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_ODOMETRY_SCHEDULE ?
			VEHICLE_FAULT_SYSTEM : VEHICLE_FAULT_SENSOR);
	fault.code = code;
	fault.recoverable = 0U;
	tm_printf((UB*)"odometry stopped; fault code %u\n", (unsigned)code);

	for (;;) {
		ER er;
		if (!sent) {
			er = tk_snd_mbf(g_mbf_faults, &fault, (INT)sizeof(fault), TMO_POL);
			if (er == E_OK) {
				sent = true;
			} else {
				tm_printf((UB*)"odometry fault delivery failed: %d\n", (int)er);
			}
		}
		er = tk_dly_tsk(ODOMETRY_FAULT_RETRY_MS);
		if (er < E_OK) {
			tm_printf((UB*)"odometry fault retry delay failed: %d\n", (int)er);
			er = tk_slp_tsk(TMO_FEVR);
			tm_printf((UB*)"odometry fault sleep returned: %d\n", (int)er);
		}
	}
}

static void odometry_task(INT stacd, void *exinf)
{
	odometry_producer_t producer = {0};
	encoder_snapshot_t snapshot;
	odometry_publish_result_t delivery = {0};
	SYSTIM now;
	uint32_t last_timestamp = 0U;
	uint16_t fault;
	ER er;

	(void)stacd;
	(void)exinf;
	er = encoder_source_snapshot(&snapshot);
	if (er < E_OK || !snapshot.valid) {
		tm_printf((UB*)"odometry initial snapshot failed: %d\n", (int)er);
		report_fault(VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_ODOMETRY_SOURCE, last_timestamp);
	}
	er = tk_get_otm(&now);
	if (er < E_OK) {
		tm_printf((UB*)"odometry clock failed: %d\n", (int)er);
		report_fault(VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_ODOMETRY_CLOCK, last_timestamp);
	}
	last_timestamp = now.lo;
	if (!odometry_producer_init(&producer, &s_config, snapshot.left_count,
				    snapshot.right_count, last_timestamp))
		report_fault(VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_ODOMETRY_CALCULATION, last_timestamp);

	for (;;) {
		er = tk_dly_tsk(s_period_ms);
		if (er < E_OK) {
			tm_printf((UB*)"odometry delay failed: %d\n", (int)er);
			report_fault(VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_ODOMETRY_SCHEDULE, last_timestamp);
		}
		er = encoder_source_snapshot(&snapshot);
		if (er < E_OK) {
			tm_printf((UB*)"odometry snapshot failed: %d\n", (int)er);
			report_fault(VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_ODOMETRY_SOURCE, last_timestamp);
		}
		er = tk_get_otm(&now);
		if (er < E_OK) {
			tm_printf((UB*)"odometry clock failed: %d\n", (int)er);
			report_fault(VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_ODOMETRY_CLOCK, last_timestamp);
		}
		last_timestamp = now.lo;
		fault = process_snapshot(&producer, &snapshot, last_timestamp,
					 s_publish_motion, &delivery);
		if (fault != 0U) {
			if (fault == VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_ODOMETRY_DELIVERY)
				tm_printf((UB*)"odometry delivery failed: line=%d imu=%d motion_attempted=%u motion=%d\n",
					  (int)delivery.line, (int)delivery.imu,
					  (unsigned)delivery.motion_attempted, (int)delivery.motion);
			report_fault(fault, last_timestamp);
		}
	}
}

static ER start_odometry(const odometry_producer_config_t *config, RELTIM period_ms,
			 bool publish_motion)
{
	odometry_producer_t validation;
	encoder_snapshot_t snapshot;
	T_CTSK task = {0};
	ID id;
	ER er;

#if TK_SUPPORT_SMP
	if (tk_get_prc() != TP_PRC1) return E_CTX;
#endif
	if (s_started) return E_OBJ;
	if (period_ms == 0U || period_ms > INT32_MAX ||
	    !odometry_producer_init(&validation, config, 0, 0, 0U))
		return E_PAR;
	if (g_mbf_line_odometry <= 0 || g_mbf_imu_odometry <= 0 || g_mbf_faults <= 0 ||
	    (publish_motion && g_mbf_motion_odometry <= 0))
		return E_OBJ;
	er = encoder_source_snapshot(&snapshot);
	if (er < E_OK) return er;
	if (!snapshot.valid) return E_IO;

	s_config = *config;
	s_period_ms = period_ms;
	s_publish_motion = publish_motion;
	task.task = (FP)odometry_task;
	task.tskatr = TA_HLNG | TA_RNG3;
	task.itskpri = ODOMETRY_TASK_PRIORITY;
	task.stksz = TASK_STACK_SIZE;
#if TK_SUPPORT_SMP
	task.tskatr |= TA_ASSPRC;
	task.assprc = TP_PRC1;
#endif
	id = tk_cre_tsk(&task);
	if (id < E_OK) return id;
	s_started = true;
	er = tk_sta_tsk(id, 0);
	if (er < E_OK) {
		ER cleanup = tk_del_tsk(id);
		if (cleanup < E_OK) {
			tm_printf((UB*)"odometry task cleanup failed: %d\n", (int)cleanup);
		} else {
			s_started = false;
		}
		return er;
	}
	return E_OK;
}

ER odometry_task_start(const odometry_producer_config_t *config, RELTIM period_ms)
{
	return start_odometry(config, period_ms, false);
}

ER odometry_task_start_with_motion(const odometry_producer_config_t *config,
				   RELTIM period_ms)
{
	return start_odometry(config, period_ms, true);
}

#if APP_TEST
#include "../test_report.h"

static BOOL drain_odometry_buffer(ID buffer)
{
	wheel_odometry_message_t message;
	INT result;

	do {
		result = tk_rcv_mbf(buffer, &message, TMO_POL);
	} while (result > 0);
	return result == E_TMOUT;
}

BOOL odometry_task_test(void)
{
	const odometry_producer_config_t config = {{2500U, 20.0f}, {2500U, 20.0f}, 1, -1};
	odometry_producer_t producer = {0};
	encoder_snapshot_t snapshot = {2500, -2500, true};
	odometry_publish_result_t delivery = {0};
	wheel_odometry_message_t line = {0}, imu = {0};
	BOOL step_ok;
	BOOL fault_ok;
	BOOL motion_ok;
	unsigned i;
	TEST_INIT();

	step_ok = odometry_producer_init(&producer, &config, 0, 0, 0U);
	if (step_ok) {
		step_ok = process_snapshot(&producer, &snapshot, 100U, false, &delivery) == 0U;
		step_ok = tk_rcv_mbf(g_mbf_line_odometry, &line, TMO_POL) ==
			  (INT)sizeof(line) && step_ok;
		step_ok = tk_rcv_mbf(g_mbf_imu_odometry, &imu, TMO_POL) ==
			  (INT)sizeof(imu) && step_ok;
		step_ok = step_ok && line.distance_mm == 200 && imu.distance_mm == 200 &&
			  line.header.sequence == 1U && imu.header.sequence == 1U;
	}

	snapshot.valid = false;
	fault_ok = process_snapshot(&producer, &snapshot, 200U, false, &delivery) ==
		   VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_ODOMETRY_SOURCE && producer.sequence == 1U;
	fault_ok = tk_rcv_mbf(g_mbf_line_odometry, &line, TMO_POL) == E_TMOUT && fault_ok;
	fault_ok = tk_rcv_mbf(g_mbf_imu_odometry, &imu, TMO_POL) == E_TMOUT && fault_ok;
	snapshot.valid = true;
	fault_ok = process_snapshot(&producer, &snapshot, 100U, false, &delivery) ==
		   VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_ODOMETRY_CALCULATION && fault_ok;

	line.header.type = VEHICLE_MESSAGE_WHEEL_ODOMETRY;
	line.header.version = VEHICLE_MESSAGE_VERSION;
	line.valid = 1U;
	for (i = 0U; i < VEHICLE_MB_ODOMETRY_CAPACITY; i++) {
		if (tk_snd_mbf(g_mbf_line_odometry, &line, (INT)sizeof(line), TMO_POL) != E_OK)
			fault_ok = FALSE;
	}
	fault_ok = process_snapshot(&producer, &snapshot, 200U, false, &delivery) ==
		   VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_ODOMETRY_DELIVERY && fault_ok;
	fault_ok = delivery.line == E_TMOUT && delivery.imu == E_OK && fault_ok;
	fault_ok = tk_rcv_mbf(g_mbf_imu_odometry, &imu, TMO_POL) ==
		   (INT)sizeof(imu) && fault_ok;
	fault_ok = drain_odometry_buffer(g_mbf_line_odometry) && fault_ok;
	fault_ok = drain_odometry_buffer(g_mbf_imu_odometry) && fault_ok;

	motion_ok = odometry_producer_init(&producer, &config, 0, 0, 0U);
	for (i = 0U; i < VEHICLE_MB_ODOMETRY_CAPACITY; i++) {
		if (tk_snd_mbf(g_mbf_motion_odometry, &line, (INT)sizeof(line), TMO_POL) != E_OK)
			motion_ok = FALSE;
	}
	motion_ok = process_snapshot(&producer, &snapshot, 100U, true, &delivery) ==
		    VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_ODOMETRY_DELIVERY &&
		    delivery.line == E_OK && delivery.imu == E_OK &&
		    delivery.motion_attempted && delivery.motion == E_TMOUT && motion_ok;
	motion_ok = drain_odometry_buffer(g_mbf_line_odometry) && motion_ok;
	motion_ok = drain_odometry_buffer(g_mbf_imu_odometry) && motion_ok;
	motion_ok = drain_odometry_buffer(g_mbf_motion_odometry) && motion_ok;

	TEST_CHECK("ODO-U08", step_ok, "task step delivers simulated snapshot");
	TEST_CHECK("ODO-U09", fault_ok, "task step rejects invalid snapshots and delivery failures");
	TEST_CHECK("ODO-U10", motion_ok, "full motion buffer stops odometry");
	TEST_SUMMARY("odometry task");
	return step_ok && fault_ok && motion_ok;
}

#ifndef ODOMETRY_TASK_H
#define ODOMETRY_TASK_H

#include <tk/tkernel.h>
#include "odometry_producer.h"

/* Called once by the core-0 startup owner after buffers and the shared GPIO
 * dispatcher are ready. Calibration and signs must be physically verified.
 * The task is pinned to core 0 and is not started automatically. */
ER odometry_task_start(const odometry_producer_config_t *config, RELTIM period_ms);

/* Opt in only when the motion consumer is ready. A full motion buffer stops
 * odometry just like a delivery failure to the other consumers. */
ER odometry_task_start_with_motion(const odometry_producer_config_t *config,
				   RELTIM period_ms);

#include "../app.h"
#include "odometry_test.h"

#if APP_TEST
#include "../test_report.h"
#include "encoder.h"

static BOOL encoder_pair_test(void)
{
	encoder_pair_t pair;
	encoder_snapshot_t snapshot;
	uint32_t pins = 0U;

	encoder_pair_init(&pair, pins);
	pins = (1U << ENCODER_LEFT_B_PIN) | (1U << ENCODER_RIGHT_A_PIN);
	encoder_pair_update(&pair, 0x00000880U, pins);
	pins |= (1U << ENCODER_LEFT_A_PIN) | (1U << ENCODER_RIGHT_B_PIN);
	encoder_pair_update(&pair, 0x00008008U, pins);
	snapshot = encoder_pair_snapshot(&pair);
	return snapshot.valid && snapshot.left_count == 2 && snapshot.right_count == -2;
}

static BOOL encoder_pair_invalid_test(void)
{
	encoder_pair_t pair;
	encoder_snapshot_t snapshot;
	uint32_t pins;

	encoder_pair_init(&pair, 0U);
	pins = (1U << ENCODER_LEFT_A_PIN) | (1U << ENCODER_LEFT_B_PIN);
	encoder_pair_update(&pair, 0x00000088U, pins);
	snapshot = encoder_pair_snapshot(&pair);
	if (snapshot.valid) return FALSE;
	pins &= ~(1U << ENCODER_LEFT_A_PIN);
	encoder_pair_update(&pair, 0x00000004U, pins);
	if (encoder_pair_snapshot(&pair).valid) return FALSE;

	encoder_pair_init(&pair, 0U);
	encoder_pair_update(&pair, 0x00000004U, 1U << ENCODER_LEFT_A_PIN);
	if (encoder_pair_snapshot(&pair).valid) return FALSE;

	encoder_pair_init(&pair, 0U);
	encoder_pair_update(&pair, 0x00000008U,
			    (1U << ENCODER_LEFT_A_PIN) | (1U << ENCODER_LEFT_B_PIN));
	return !encoder_pair_snapshot(&pair).valid;
}

BOOL odometry_test(void)
{
	encoder_t encoder;
	wheel_odometry_config_t config = {2500U, 20.0f};
	wheel_odometry_result_t result;
	float distance = 0.0f;
	BOOL encoder_ok;
	BOOL distance_ok;
	BOOL invalid_ok;
	BOOL pair_ok;
	BOOL pair_invalid_ok;
	TEST_INIT();

	encoder_init(&encoder, 0U, 0U);
	encoder_update(&encoder, 0U, 1U);
	encoder_update(&encoder, 1U, 1U);
	encoder_update(&encoder, 1U, 0U);
	encoder_update(&encoder, 0U, 0U);
	encoder_ok = encoder_get_count(&encoder) == 4;
	encoder_update(&encoder, 1U, 0U);
	encoder_update(&encoder, 1U, 1U);
	encoder_update(&encoder, 0U, 1U);
	encoder_update(&encoder, 0U, 0U);
	encoder_ok = encoder_ok && encoder_get_count(&encoder) == 0;
	encoder_update(&encoder, 1U, 1U);
	encoder_ok = encoder_ok && encoder_get_count(&encoder) == 0;
	encoder_init(&encoder, 0U, 0U);
	encoder.count = INT32_MAX;
	encoder_update(&encoder, 0U, 1U);
	encoder_ok = encoder_ok && encoder_get_count(&encoder) == INT32_MIN;
	encoder_update(&encoder, 0U, 0U);
	encoder_ok = encoder_ok && encoder_get_count(&encoder) == INT32_MAX;

	distance_ok = wheel_odometry_calculate(&config, 2500, 1.0f, &result) &&
		      result.distance_cm > 19.999f && result.distance_cm < 20.001f &&
		      result.rpm > 59.999f && result.rpm < 60.001f &&
		      result.speed_cm_per_second > 19.999f &&
		      result.speed_cm_per_second < 20.001f;
	distance_ok = distance_ok &&
		      wheel_odometry_distance(&config, -2500, &distance) &&
		      distance > -20.001f && distance < -19.999f;

	config.counts_per_revolution = 0U;
	invalid_ok = !wheel_odometry_distance(&config, 2500, &distance);
	config.counts_per_revolution = 2500U;
	invalid_ok = invalid_ok &&
		     !wheel_odometry_calculate(&config, 2500, 0.0f, &result);
	pair_ok = encoder_pair_test();
	pair_invalid_ok = encoder_pair_invalid_test();

	TEST_CHECK("ODO-U01", encoder_ok, "quadrature forward, reverse and invalid transitions");
	TEST_CHECK("ODO-U02", distance_ok, "calibrated wheel distance and speed");
	TEST_CHECK("ODO-U03", invalid_ok, "invalid calibration and elapsed time rejected");
	TEST_CHECK("ODO-U06", pair_ok, "coherent independent encoder counts");
	TEST_CHECK("ODO-U07", pair_invalid_ok, "ambiguous encoder events latch invalid");
	TEST_SUMMARY("odometry");
	return encoder_ok && distance_ok && invalid_ok && pair_ok && pair_invalid_ok;
}

#include "wheel_odometry.h"

#include <float.h>
#include <math.h>
#include <stddef.h>

bool wheel_odometry_count_delta(uint32_t current, uint32_t previous, int32_t *delta)
{
	uint32_t difference = current - previous;
	if (delta == NULL || difference == 0x80000000U) return false;
	*delta = difference <= INT32_MAX ? (int32_t)difference :
		 INT32_MIN + (int32_t)(difference - 0x80000000U);
	return true;
}

static bool valid_config(const wheel_odometry_config_t *config)
{
	return config != NULL &&
	       config->counts_per_revolution > 0U &&
	       isfinite(config->wheel_circumference_cm) &&
	       config->wheel_circumference_cm > 0.0f;
}

bool wheel_odometry_distance_precise(const wheel_odometry_config_t *config,
				     int64_t counts, double *distance_cm)
{
	double distance;

	if (!valid_config(config) || distance_cm == NULL) {
		return false;
	}
	distance = ((double)counts * (double)config->wheel_circumference_cm) /
		   (double)config->counts_per_revolution;
	if (!isfinite(distance)) {
		return false;
	}
	*distance_cm = distance;
	return true;
}

bool wheel_odometry_distance(const wheel_odometry_config_t *config,
			     int64_t counts, float *distance_cm)
{
	double distance;

	if (distance_cm == NULL ||
	    !wheel_odometry_distance_precise(config, counts, &distance) ||
	    distance > (double)FLT_MAX || distance < -(double)FLT_MAX) {
		return false;
	}
	*distance_cm = (float)distance;
	return true;
}

bool wheel_odometry_calculate(const wheel_odometry_config_t *config,
			      int64_t delta_counts, float dt_seconds,
			      wheel_odometry_result_t *result)
{
	float distance;
	double revolutions;
	double rpm;
	double speed;

	if (result == NULL || !isfinite(dt_seconds) || dt_seconds <= 0.0f ||
	    !wheel_odometry_distance(config, delta_counts, &distance)) {
		return false;
	}
	revolutions = (double)delta_counts / (double)config->counts_per_revolution;
	rpm = revolutions * 60.0 / (double)dt_seconds;
	speed = (double)distance / (double)dt_seconds;
	if (!isfinite(rpm) || !isfinite(speed) ||
	    rpm > (double)FLT_MAX || rpm < -(double)FLT_MAX ||
	    speed > (double)FLT_MAX || speed < -(double)FLT_MAX) {
		return false;
	}
	result->distance_cm = distance;
	result->speed_cm_per_second = (float)speed;
	result->rpm = (float)rpm;
	return true;
}

#include <stdbool.h>

/* Counts include all quadrature transitions and the gearbox ratio. */
typedef struct {

	float speed_cm_per_second;

/*
 *	subsystem1.c
 *	Subsystem 1 -- LED heartbeat: creates its objects and starts its task.
 *
 *	APIs shown: tk_cre_cyc, tk_sta_cyc
 */

#include "subsystem1.h"

EXPORT ID	tid_led;	/* IF-05, see subsystem1.h */
EXPORT ID	cyc_led;

EXPORT ER subsystem1_init(void)
{
	T_CCYC	ccyc = {0};

	/* Cyclic handler: once started, the kernel calls led_cyclic_handler
	   every S1_TICK_MS.  Without TA_STA it is created stopped; it is
	   started in subsystem1_start(), once the task it wakes exists. */
	ccyc.cycatr = TA_HLNG;
	ccyc.cychdr = (FP)led_cyclic_handler;
	ccyc.cyctim = S1_TICK_MS;	/* period, ms */
	ccyc.cycphs = S1_TICK_MS;	/* delay before the first call, ms */
	cyc_led = tk_cre_cyc(&ccyc);
	return (cyc_led < E_OK) ? cyc_led : E_OK;
}

EXPORT ER subsystem1_start(void)
{
	tid_led = app_start_task(led_task, S1_PRI_LED, 0, "led_task");
	if (tid_led < E_OK) {
		return tid_led;
	}
	return tk_sta_cyc(cyc_led);	/* start the cyclic handler now */
}

/*
 *	subsystem1_test.c
 *	On-target tests for subsystem 1.  Built only with: make TEST=1
 *
 *	Each TEST_CHECK prints a PASS/FAIL line on the console.  Save the
 *	console output to testing/subsystem1/logs/ and record the result
 *	in testing/subsystem1/TEST_LOG.md.  Test IDs: S1-U01, S1-U02, ...
 */

#include "subsystem1.h"
#include "../test_report.h"

#if APP_TEST

EXPORT void subsystem1_test(void)
{

	T_RTSK	rtsk;
	ER	er;

	TEST_INIT();

	/* S1-U01: the cyclic handler exists and is running */
	er = tk_ref_cyc(cyc_led, &rcyc);
	TEST_CHECK("S1-U01", er == E_OK && (rcyc.cycstat & TCYC_STA),
		   "cyclic handler is running");

	/* S1-U02: between ticks the LED task is asleep, not busy-looping.
	   Sample it 10 times over ~1 s; it should almost always be waiting. */
	{
		INT	i, waiting = 0;
		for (i = 0; i < 10; i++) {
			tk_dly_tsk(97);	/* not a multiple of the tick */
			if (tk_ref_tsk(tid_led, &rtsk) == E_OK &&
			    (rtsk.tskstat & TTS_WAI)) {
				waiting++;
			}
		}
		TEST_CHECK("S1-U02", waiting >= 9, "LED task sleeps between ticks");
	}

	TEST_SUMMARY("subsystem1");
}

#endif /* APP_TEST */
=== C:\Users\Yong Jun\school\INF2004-Embedded_Systems\project\INF2004_Project\app_program\subsystem1\task_led.c ===
/*
 *	task_led.c
 *	Subsystem 1 -- the LED task and its cyclic handler.
 *
 *	APIs shown: tk_slp_tsk, tk_wup_tsk, tk_can_wup, cyclic handler,
 *	            gpio_set_pin / gpio_set_val
 *
 *	Instead of tk_dly_tsk(), this task sleeps until a cyclic handler
 *	wakes it.  The handler is called by the kernel's timer at a fixed
 *	rate, so the blink rate stays exact however long the task's own work
 *	takes.
 */

#include "subsystem1.h"

/*
 * Cyclic handler.  Runs in interrupt context, NOT as a task, so it must
 * be short and must never wait (no tk_slp_tsk, tk_wai_sem, tm_printf...).
 * Signalling a task is the normal thing to do here.
 */
EXPORT void led_cyclic_handler(void *exinf)
{
	(void)exinf;
	tk_wup_tsk(tid_led);	/* wake the LED task (the request queues
				   up if the task is not asleep yet) */
}

EXPORT void led_task(INT stacd, void *exinf)
{
	UINT	level = 0;

	(void)stacd; (void)exinf;

	gpio_set_pin(S1_LED_PIN, GPIO_MODE_OUT);	/* make the pin an output */

	while (1) {
		/* Sleep until led_cyclic_handler calls tk_wup_tsk() */
		tk_slp_tsk(TMO_FEVR);

		/* tk_can_wup: discard wake-ups that queued up while we were
		   not sleeping (e.g. while subsystem 4 had us suspended), so
		   the LED does not "catch up" with a burst of fast blinks. */
		tk_can_wup(TSK_SELF);

		level = !level;
		gpio_set_val(S1_LED_PIN, level);
	}
}
=== C:\Users\Yong Jun\school\INF2004-Embedded_Systems\project\INF2004_Project\app_program\subsystem2\subsystem2.c ===
/*
 *	subsystem2.c
 *	Subsystem 2 -- Producer: creates its objects and starts its task.
 *
 *	APIs shown: tk_cre_sem, tk_cre_mpf
 */

#include "subsystem2.h"

EXPORT ID	sem_slots;	/* IF-02, see subsystem2.h */
EXPORT ID	mpf_samples;

EXPORT ER subsystem2_init(void)
{
	T_CSEM	csem = {0};
	T_CMPF	cmpf = {0};

	/* Semaphore: a counter of free "slots".  The producer takes one
	   (tk_wai_sem) before each sample; the consumer gives one back
	   (tk_sig_sem).  It never exceeds the number of memory blocks, so
	   the producer can never run out of memory. */
	csem.sematr  = TA_TFIFO;	/* waiting tasks queue in FIFO order */
	csem.isemcnt = S2_POOL_COUNT;	/* initial count */
	csem.maxsem  = S2_POOL_COUNT;	/* maximum count */
	sem_slots = tk_cre_sem(&csem);
	if (sem_slots < E_OK) {
		return sem_slots;
	}

	/* Fixed-size memory pool: S2_POOL_COUNT blocks, each exactly one
	   SAMPLE_MSG.  Faster and more predictable than malloc(). */
	cmpf.mpfatr = TA_TFIFO | TA_RNG3;
	cmpf.mpfcnt = S2_POOL_COUNT;		/* number of blocks */
	cmpf.blfsz  = sizeof(SAMPLE_MSG);	/* size of one block */
	mpf_samples = tk_cre_mpf(&cmpf);
	return (mpf_samples < E_OK) ? mpf_samples : E_OK;
}

EXPORT ER subsystem2_start(void)
{
	/* Pinned to core 1 (TP_PRC2): samples cross to subsystem 3 on core 0 */
	ID tid = app_start_task(producer_task, S2_PRI_PRODUCER, TP_PRC2,
				"producer_task");
	return (tid < E_OK) ? tid : E_OK;
}
=== C:\Users\Yong Jun\school\INF2004-Embedded_Systems\project\INF2004_Project\app_program\subsystem2\subsystem2.h ===
/*
 *	subsystem2.h
 *	Subsystem 2 -- Producer (sample source).  Student ID: <student ID>.
 *
 *	Public interface of subsystem 2.  Other subsystems include it as
 *	"../subsystem2/subsystem2.h".
 */

#ifndef SUBSYSTEM2_H
#define SUBSYSTEM2_H

#include "../app.h"

#define S2_PRI_PRODUCER		14	/* priority band 14-15 (TEAM.md) */
#define S2_PERIOD_MS		300	/* one sample every 300 ms */

/* ---------------------------------------------------------------------
 *  IF-01 data format: one sample, sent to subsystem 3's mailbox.
 *  A mailbox message MUST start with a T_MSG header: the kernel links
 *  messages together through it, so the data itself is never copied.
 * --------------------------------------------------------------------- */
typedef struct {
	T_MSG	hdr;		/* used by the kernel, do not touch */
	UW	seq;		/* sample sequence number */
	UW	time_ms;	/* system time when the sample was taken */
	INT	value;		/* the "measurement", 0..100 */
	INT	prc;		/* processor (core) that produced it: 1 or 2 */
} SAMPLE_MSG;

#define S2_POOL_COUNT	4	/* at most 4 samples in flight */

/* IF-02: the receiver of a sample gives its memory block back to
   mpf_samples (tk_rel_mpf) and then a slot back to sem_slots
   (tk_sig_sem, count 1).  Nothing else may use these two objects. */
IMPORT ID	sem_slots;
IMPORT ID	mpf_samples;

/* Private to subsystem 2 */
IMPORT void	producer_task(INT stacd, void *exinf);
IMPORT INT	s2_make_value(UW seq);

#endif /* SUBSYSTEM2_H */
=== C:\Users\Yong Jun\school\INF2004-Embedded_Systems\project\INF2004_Project\app_program\subsystem2\subsystem2_test.c ===
/*
 *	subsystem2_test.c
 *	On-target tests for subsystem 2.  Built only with: make TEST=1
 *	Record results in testing/subsystem2/TEST_LOG.md.
 */

#include "subsystem2.h"
#include "../test_report.h"

#if APP_TEST

EXPORT void subsystem2_test(void)
{
	T_RSEM	rsem;
	T_RMPF	rmpf;
	UW	seq;
	INT	v, bad = 0;

	TEST_INIT();

	/* S2-U01: every sample value is inside the documented 0..100 range */
	for (seq = 0; seq < 40; seq++) {
		v = s2_make_value(seq);
		if (v < 0 || v > 100) bad++;
	}
	TEST_CHECK("S2-U01", bad == 0, "sample values stay within 0..100");

	/* S2-U02: flow control never over- or under-counts */
	TEST_CHECK("S2-U02",
		   tk_ref_sem(sem_slots, &rsem) == E_OK &&
		   rsem.semcnt >= 0 && rsem.semcnt <= S2_POOL_COUNT &&
		   tk_ref_mpf(mpf_samples, &rmpf) == E_OK &&
		   rmpf.frbcnt <= S2_POOL_COUNT,
		   "free slots and free blocks within pool size");

	TEST_SUMMARY("subsystem2");
}

#endif /* APP_TEST */
=== C:\Users\Yong Jun\school\INF2004-Embedded_Systems\project\INF2004_Project\app_program\subsystem2\task_producer.c ===
/*
 *	task_producer.c
 *	Subsystem 2 -- the producer task.
 *
 *	APIs shown: tk_wai_sem, tk_get_mpf, tk_snd_mbx, tk_get_tim,
 *	            tk_dly_tsk, tk_get_prc
 *
 *	Replace s2_make_value() with a real sensor read (ADC, I2C, ...) in
 *	your project.  Keep each peripheral owned by ONE task.
 */

#include "subsystem2.h"
#include "../subsystem3/subsystem3.h"	/* IF-01: mbx_samples */

/* A fake measurement: a triangle wave between 0 and 100 */
EXPORT INT s2_make_value(UW seq)
{
	INT	phase = (INT)(seq % 20);
	return (phase < 10 ? phase : 20 - phase) * 10;
}

EXPORT void producer_task(INT stacd, void *exinf)
{
	UW		seq = 0;
	SAMPLE_MSG	*msg;
	SYSTIM		now;
	ER		er;

	(void)stacd; (void)exinf;

	while (1) {
		/* 1. Take a free slot.  If the consumer is behind and all
		      slots are in use, this task blocks here until one is
		      returned (flow control). */
		er = tk_wai_sem(sem_slots, 1, TMO_FEVR);
		CHECK(er, "tk_wai_sem");

		/* 2. Get a memory block for the message.  The semaphore
		      guarantees one is free, so poll (TMO_POL = don't wait). */
		er = tk_get_mpf(mpf_samples, (void **)&msg, TMO_POL);
		if (er < E_OK) {
			CHECK(er, "tk_get_mpf");
			tk_sig_sem(sem_slots, 1);	/* give the slot back */
			tk_dly_tsk(S2_PERIOD_MS);
			continue;
		}

		/* 3. Fill it in.  tk_get_tim gives milliseconds since boot
		      as a 64-bit hi:lo pair; lo is enough for ~49 days. */
		tk_get_tim(&now);
		msg->seq     = seq++;
		msg->time_ms = now.lo;
		msg->value   = s2_make_value(msg->seq);
#if TK_SUPPORT_SMP
		msg->prc     = tk_get_prc();	/* which core am I on? */
#else
		msg->prc     = 1;
#endif

		/* 4. Send the POINTER to subsystem 3.  From here on the block
		      belongs to the receiver: do not touch msg again. */
		er = tk_snd_mbx(mbx_samples, (T_MSG *)msg);
		CHECK(er, "tk_snd_mbx");

		/* 5. Wait for the next period.  Other tasks run meanwhile. */
		tk_dly_tsk(S2_PERIOD_MS);
	}
}
=== C:\Users\Yong Jun\school\INF2004-Embedded_Systems\project\INF2004_Project\app_program\subsystem3\subsystem3.c ===
/*
 *	subsystem3.c
 *	Subsystem 3 -- Consumer: creates its objects and starts its task.
 *
 *	APIs shown: tk_cre_mbx, tk_cre_mtx
 */

#include "subsystem3.h"

EXPORT ID		mbx_samples;	/* IF-01 */
EXPORT ID		mtx_stats;	/* IF-04 */
EXPORT APP_STATS	app_stats;

EXPORT ER subsystem3_init(void)
{
	T_CMBX	cmbx = {0};
	T_CMTX	cmtx = {0};

	/* Mailbox: passes a POINTER to a message (zero copy).  The message
	   memory must stay valid until the receiver is done with it, which
	   is why subsystem 2 takes it from its memory pool. */
	cmbx.mbxatr = TA_TFIFO | TA_MFIFO;	/* FIFO waiters, FIFO messages */
	mbx_samples = tk_cre_mbx(&cmbx);
	if (mbx_samples < E_OK) {
		return mbx_samples;
	}

	/* Mutex: only one task at a time may touch app_stats.  TA_INHERIT
	   (priority inheritance) avoids priority inversion: a low-priority
	   holder is temporarily raised to the waiter's priority. */
	cmtx.mtxatr = TA_INHERIT;
	mtx_stats = tk_cre_mtx(&cmtx);
	return (mtx_stats < E_OK) ? mtx_stats : E_OK;
}

EXPORT ER subsystem3_start(void)
{
	/* Pinned to core 0 (TP_PRC1): opposite core to the producer */
	ID tid = app_start_task(consumer_task, S3_PRI_CONSUMER, TP_PRC1,
				"consumer_task");
	return (tid < E_OK) ? tid : E_OK;
}

/*
 *	subsystem3.h
 *	Subsystem 3 -- Consumer (statistics).  Student ID: <student ID>.
 *
 *	Public interface of subsystem 3.  Other subsystems include it as
 *	"../subsystem3/subsystem3.h".
 */

#ifndef SUBSYSTEM3_H
#define SUBSYSTEM3_H

#include "../app.h"

#define S3_PRI_CONSUMER		16	/* priority band 16-17 (TEAM.md) */
#define S3_BATCH_SIZE		5	/* report to subsystem 4 every N samples */

/* IF-01: send SAMPLE_MSG (subsystem2.h) here with tk_snd_mbx */
IMPORT ID	mbx_samples;

/* IF-04: statistics.  Read them ONLY while holding mtx_stats:
 *	tk_loc_mtx(mtx_stats, TMO_FEVR);  copy = app_stats;  tk_unl_mtx(mtx_stats);
 */
typedef struct {
	UW	received;	/* samples received so far */
	INT	min;
	INT	max;
	W	sum;
} APP_STATS;

IMPORT ID		mtx_stats;
IMPORT APP_STATS	app_stats;

/* Private to subsystem 3 */
IMPORT void	consumer_task(INT stacd, void *exinf);

#endif /* SUBSYSTEM3_H */

/*
 *	subsystem3_test.c
 *	On-target tests for subsystem 3.  Built only with: make TEST=1
 *	Record unit results in testing/subsystem3/TEST_LOG.md and the
 *	INT- result in testing/integration/TEST_LOG.md.
 */

#include "../test_report.h"

#if APP_TEST

LOCAL APP_STATS read_stats(void)
{
	APP_STATS	s;

	tk_loc_mtx(mtx_stats, TMO_FEVR);
	s = app_stats;
	tk_unl_mtx(mtx_stats);
	return s;
}

EXPORT void subsystem3_test(void)

	TEST_INIT();

	/* INT-01 (integration, IF-01): samples from subsystem 2 arrive.
	   At one sample per 300 ms, 2 s should bring at least 5. */
	before = read_stats();
	tk_dly_tsk(2000);
	after = read_stats();
	TEST_CHECK("INT-01", after.received >= before.received + 5,
		   "samples flow from subsystem 2 to subsystem 3");

	/* S3-U01: the statistics are self-consistent */
	TEST_CHECK("S3-U01",
		   after.received > 0 && after.min <= after.max &&
		   after.min >= 0 && after.max <= 100,
		   "min <= max, both within 0..100");

	TEST_SUMMARY("subsystem3");
}

=== C:\Users\Yong Jun\school\INF2004-Embedded_Systems\project\INF2004_Project\app_program\subsystem3\task_consumer.c ===
/*
 *	task_consumer.c
 *	Subsystem 3 -- the consumer task.
 *
 *	APIs shown: tk_rcv_mbx, tk_rel_mpf, tk_sig_sem, tk_loc_mtx,
 *	            tk_unl_mtx, tk_snd_mbf, tk_set_flg
 */

#include "subsystem3.h"
#include "../subsystem2/subsystem2.h"	/* IF-01/02: SAMPLE_MSG, pool, slots */
#include "../subsystem4/subsystem4.h"	/* IF-03: mbf_summary, flg_events */

EXPORT void consumer_task(INT stacd, void *exinf)
{
	SAMPLE_MSG	*msg;
	UB		text[S4_SUMMARY_MAX];	/* tm_sprintf adds a '\0' */
	INT		len;
	UW		count;
	ER		er;

	(void)stacd; (void)exinf;

	while (1) {
		/* 1. Wait for the next sample (TMO_FEVR = wait forever) */
		er = tk_rcv_mbx(mbx_samples, (T_MSG **)&msg, TMO_FEVR);
		if (er < E_OK) {
			CHECK(er, "tk_rcv_mbx");
			continue;
		}

		/* 2. Update the shared statistics.  Subsystem 4 reads them
		      too, so lock the mutex around every access (IF-04). */
		tk_loc_mtx(mtx_stats, TMO_FEVR);
		if (app_stats.received == 0 || msg->value < app_stats.min) {
			app_stats.min = msg->value;
		}
		if (app_stats.received == 0 || msg->value > app_stats.max) {
			app_stats.max = msg->value;
		}
		app_stats.sum += msg->value;
		count = ++app_stats.received;
		tk_unl_mtx(mtx_stats);

		/* 3. Every S3_BATCH_SIZE samples, send a text line to subsystem
		      4 through its message buffer (the text is copied), then
		      set FLG_BATCH to wake it up (IF-03). */
		if (count % S3_BATCH_SIZE == 0) {
			/* tm_sprintf: like sprintf, returns the length.
			   The longest line here is ~35 bytes < S4_SUMMARY_MAX. */
			len = tm_sprintf(text, (const UB*)"n=%u last=%d from core %d",
					 count, msg->value, msg->prc - 1);

			/* TMO_POL: if the buffer is full, drop the line rather
			   than block the data path. */
			er = tk_snd_mbf(mbf_summary, text, len, TMO_POL);
			if (er == E_OK) {
				tk_set_flg(flg_events, FLG_BATCH);
			}
		}

		/* 4. Done with the sample (IF-02): return its memory block to
		      the pool, then the slot so the producer may go on. */
		tk_rel_mpf(mpf_samples, msg);
		tk_sig_sem(sem_slots, 1);
	}
}
=== C:\Users\Yong Jun\school\INF2004-Embedded_Systems\project\INF2004_Project\app_program\subsystem4\subsystem4.c ===
/*
 *	subsystem4.c
 *	Subsystem 4 -- Monitor: creates its objects and starts its task.
 *
 *	APIs shown: tk_cre_flg, tk_cre_mbf, tk_cre_alm
 */

#include "subsystem4.h"

EXPORT ID	mbf_summary;	/* IF-03 */
EXPORT ID	flg_events;	/* IF-03 */
EXPORT ID	alm_monitor;

EXPORT ER subsystem4_init(void)
{
	T_CFLG	cflg = {0};
	T_CMBF	cmbf = {0};
	T_CALM	calm = {0};

	/* Event flag: a 32-bit pattern of bits.  Tasks wait for a bit (or a
	   combination of bits) to be set. */
	cflg.flgatr  = TA_TFIFO | TA_WMUL;	/* allow several waiters */
	cflg.iflgptn = 0;			/* all bits clear to begin with */
	flg_events = tk_cre_flg(&cflg);
	if (flg_events < E_OK) {
		return flg_events;
	}

	/* Message buffer: COPIES variable-length data into a kernel ring
	   buffer.  Here it carries short text lines from subsystem 3. */
	cmbf.mbfatr = TA_TFIFO;
	cmbf.bufsz  = 4 * S4_SUMMARY_MAX;	/* total buffer size, bytes */
	cmbf.maxmsz = S4_SUMMARY_MAX;		/* largest single message */
	mbf_summary = tk_cre_mbf(&cmbf);
	if (mbf_summary < E_OK) {
		return mbf_summary;
	}

	/* Alarm handler: called ONCE, a given time after tk_sta_alm().
	   The monitor task re-arms it each time round its loop. */
	calm.almatr = TA_HLNG;
	calm.almhdr = (FP)monitor_alarm_handler;
	alm_monitor = tk_cre_alm(&calm);
	return (alm_monitor < E_OK) ? alm_monitor : E_OK;
}

EXPORT ER subsystem4_start(void)
{
	/* No affinity: the scheduler may run it on either core */
	ID tid = app_start_task(monitor_task, S4_PRI_MONITOR, 0, "monitor_task");
	return (tid < E_OK) ? tid : E_OK;
}

/*
 *	subsystem4.h
 *	Subsystem 4 -- Monitor (reporting).  Student ID: <student ID>.
 *

 *	"../subsystem4/subsystem4.h".

#ifndef SUBSYSTEM4_H
#define SUBSYSTEM4_H

#include "../app.h"

#define S4_PRI_MONITOR		18	/* priority band 18-19 (TEAM.md) */
#define S4_ALARM_MS		2000	/* report anyway if nothing for 2 s */
#define S4_LED_TOGGLE_REPORTS	6	/* pause/resume the LED every N reports */

/* IF-03: send one text line (at most S4_SUMMARY_MAX bytes, no '\0'
   needed) to mbf_summary, then set FLG_BATCH in flg_events. */
#define S4_SUMMARY_MAX		48
#define FLG_BATCH		0x0002	/* set by subsystem 3 */
#define FLG_ALARM		0x0001	/* private: set by our alarm handler */

IMPORT ID	mbf_summary;
IMPORT ID	flg_events;

/* Private to subsystem 4 */
IMPORT ID	alm_monitor;
IMPORT UW	s4_lines_received;	/* for the tests */
IMPORT void	monitor_task(INT stacd, void *exinf);
IMPORT void	monitor_alarm_handler(void *exinf);

#endif /* SUBSYSTEM4_H */

=== C:\Users\Yong Jun\school\INF2004-Embedded_Systems\project\INF2004_Project\app_program\subsystem4\task_monitor.c ===
/*
 *	task_monitor.c
 *	Subsystem 4 -- the monitor task and its alarm handler.
 *
 *	APIs shown: tk_sta_alm, tk_stp_alm, alarm handler, tk_wai_flg,
 *	            tk_rcv_mbf, tk_loc_mtx/tk_unl_mtx, tk_ref_sem, tk_ref_mpf,
 *	            tk_ref_tsk, tk_sus_tsk, tk_rsm_tsk, tk_chg_pri,
 *	            tk_get_tid, tk_get_tim
 */

#include "subsystem4.h"
#include "../subsystem1/subsystem1.h"	/* IF-05: tid_led */
#include "../subsystem2/subsystem2.h"	/* sem_slots, mpf_samples (read only) */
#include "../subsystem3/subsystem3.h"	/* IF-04: app_stats, mtx_stats */

EXPORT UW	s4_lines_received;

/*
 * Alarm handler: one-shot, interrupt context (same rules as a cyclic
 * handler: keep it short, never wait).
 */
EXPORT void monitor_alarm_handler(void *exinf)
{
	(void)exinf;
	tk_set_flg(flg_events, FLG_ALARM);
}

LOCAL const char *state_name(UINT tskstat)
{
	switch (tskstat) {
	case TTS_RUN:	return "RUN";
	case TTS_RDY:	return "READY";
	case TTS_WAI:	return "WAIT";
	case TTS_SUS:	return "SUSPEND";
	case TTS_WAS:	return "WAIT+SUSPEND";
	case TTS_DMT:	return "DORMANT";
	default:	return "?";
	}
}

EXPORT void monitor_task(INT stacd, void *exinf)
{
	UINT		flgptn;
	UB		text[S4_SUMMARY_MAX + 1];
	INT		len;
	APP_STATS	snap;
	T_RSEM		rsem;
	T_RMPF		rmpf;
	T_RTSK		rtsk;
	SYSTIM		now;
	UW		reports = 0;
	BOOL		led_paused = FALSE;
	ER		er;

	(void)stacd; (void)exinf;

	tm_printf((UB*)"monitor: I am task %d\n", tk_get_tid());

	while (1) {
		/* 1. Arm the one-shot alarm, then wait for EITHER bit.
		      TWF_ORW = wake on any of the bits, TWF_CLR = clear them
		      on wake-up.  The extra timeout is just a safety net. */
		tk_sta_alm(alm_monitor, S4_ALARM_MS);
		er = tk_wai_flg(flg_events, FLG_ALARM | FLG_BATCH,
				TWF_ORW | TWF_CLR, &flgptn, S4_ALARM_MS * 2);
		tk_stp_alm(alm_monitor);	/* cancel it if a batch came first */

		if (er == E_TMOUT) {
			tm_printf((UB*)"monitor: nothing at all happened?\n");
			continue;
		}
		if (flgptn & FLG_ALARM) {
			tm_printf((UB*)"monitor: alarm -- no batch for %d ms\n",
				  S4_ALARM_MS);
		}

		/* 2. Drain the message buffer without waiting (TMO_POL).
		      tk_rcv_mbf returns the message length, or an error. */
		while ((len = tk_rcv_mbf(mbf_summary, text, TMO_POL)) > 0) {
			text[len] = '\0';
			s4_lines_received++;
			tm_printf((UB*)"monitor: batch  %s\n", text);
		}

		/* 3. Copy the stats under subsystem 3's mutex, print outside
		      it: hold a mutex for as short a time as possible. */
		tk_loc_mtx(mtx_stats, TMO_FEVR);
		snap = app_stats;
		tk_unl_mtx(mtx_stats);

		tk_get_tim(&now);
		tk_ref_sem(sem_slots, &rsem);
		tk_ref_mpf(mpf_samples, &rmpf);
		tm_printf((UB*)"monitor: t=%u ms  samples=%u  min=%d max=%d avg=%d"
			  "  free slots=%d  free blocks=%d\n",
			  now.lo, snap.received, snap.min, snap.max,
			  snap.received ? (INT)(snap.sum / (W)snap.received) : 0,
			  rsem.semcnt, (INT)rmpf.frbcnt);

		/* 4. Every few reports, pause or resume subsystem 1's LED task
		      (IF-05) and look at it with tk_ref_tsk. */
		if (++reports % S4_LED_TOGGLE_REPORTS == 0) {
			if (led_paused) {
				tk_rsm_tsk(tid_led);
				tk_chg_pri(tid_led, S1_PRI_LED);	/* back to normal */
			} else {
				tk_sus_tsk(tid_led);
				/* Changing the priority of a task: */
				tk_chg_pri(tid_led, S1_PRI_LED + 1);
			}
			led_paused = !led_paused;

			tk_ref_tsk(tid_led, &rtsk);
			tm_printf((UB*)"monitor: LED task %s, state=%s, pri=%d\n",
				  led_paused ? "paused" : "resumed",
				  state_name(rtsk.tskstat), rtsk.tskpri);
		}
	}
}

#include "wifi_command.h"

#include <stddef.h>

bool wifi_command_decode(const uint8_t *packet, uint32_t length,
			 wifi_command_t *command)
{
	wifi_command_t decoded;

	if (packet == NULL || command == NULL || length != WIFI_COMMAND_PACKET_SIZE)
		return false;
	decoded.type = packet[0] >> 5U;
	if (decoded.type < WIFI_COMMAND_START || decoded.type > WIFI_COMMAND_JUNCTION)
		return false;
	decoded.argument = packet[0] & 0x1fU;
	decoded.sequence = packet[1];
	*command = decoded;
	return true;
}
=== C:\Users\Yong Jun\school\INF2004-Embedded_Systems\project\INF2004_Project\app_program\wifi\wifi_command.h ===
#ifndef WIFI_COMMAND_H
#define WIFI_COMMAND_H

#include <stdbool.h>
#include <stdint.h>

#define WIFI_COMMAND_PACKET_SIZE 2U

typedef enum {
	WIFI_COMMAND_START = 1,
	WIFI_COMMAND_PAUSE,
	WIFI_COMMAND_RESUME,
	WIFI_COMMAND_STOP,
	WIFI_COMMAND_REQUEST_STATUS,
	WIFI_COMMAND_JUNCTION
} wifi_command_type_t;

typedef struct {
	uint8_t type;
	uint8_t argument;
	uint8_t sequence;
} wifi_command_t;

/* Decode the legacy two-byte dashboard packet. The sequence is eight bits and
 * wraps at 255. This format has no authentication or sender timestamp; a
 * successful decode does not establish freshness, authority, or permission to
 * move. Argument meaning must be validated by the vehicle controller.
 * The output is unchanged on failure. */
bool wifi_command_decode(const uint8_t *packet, uint32_t length,
			 wifi_command_t *command);

#endif
=== C:\Users\Yong Jun\school\INF2004-Embedded_Systems\project\INF2004_Project\app_program\wifi\wifi_mqtt_bridge.c ===
#include "../app.h"
#include "../vehicle_buffers.h"
#include "wifi_mqtt_bridge.h"
#include "wifi_mqtt_bridge_owner.h"
#include "wifi_telemetry.h"

#define BRIDGE_CAPACITY 4U
#define BRIDGE_WORDS (BRIDGE_CAPACITY * \
	(1U + ((sizeof(telemetry_message_t) + sizeof(INT) - 1U) / sizeof(INT))))

typedef struct {
	telemetry_message_t pending;
	uint32_t max_age_ms;
	uint32_t last_clock_ms;
	bool have_pending;
} bridge_state_t;

typedef struct {
	uint32_t sequence;
	uint32_t time;
	uint32_t flags;
} bridge_snapshot_t;

#if TM_MQTT
static INT s_storage[BRIDGE_WORDS];
#endif
static ID s_buffer;
static bridge_state_t s_bridge;
/* Published last by the single startup owner; only the network owner faults it. */
static int s_status;
static int s_stop_requested;
static uint32_t s_started_at;
/* One network-owner writer; the sequence prevents mixed cross-core snapshots. */
static bridge_snapshot_t s_snapshot;
static bool s_fault_sent;
static bool s_fault_delivery_reported;

static bool valid_packet(const telemetry_message_t *message)
{
	return message->header.type == VEHICLE_MESSAGE_TELEMETRY &&
	       message->header.version == VEHICLE_MESSAGE_VERSION &&
	       wifi_telemetry_packet_valid((const uint8_t *)message->payload,
					   message->payload_length);
}

static void snapshot_write(bridge_snapshot_t *snapshot, uint32_t now, uint32_t flags)
{
	uint32_t sequence = __atomic_load_n(&snapshot->sequence, __ATOMIC_SEQ_CST);
	sequence = sequence >= UINT32_MAX - 1U ? 2U : sequence + 2U;
	__atomic_store_n(&snapshot->sequence, sequence - 1U, __ATOMIC_SEQ_CST);
	__atomic_store_n(&snapshot->time, now, __ATOMIC_SEQ_CST);
	__atomic_store_n(&snapshot->flags, flags, __ATOMIC_SEQ_CST);
	__atomic_store_n(&snapshot->sequence, sequence, __ATOMIC_SEQ_CST);
}

static bool snapshot_read(const bridge_snapshot_t *snapshot, uint32_t started_at,
			  uint32_t *timestamp, uint32_t *flags)
{
	uint32_t before, after;
	unsigned i;

	for (i = 0; i < 3U; i++) {
		before = __atomic_load_n(&snapshot->sequence, __ATOMIC_SEQ_CST);
		if (before & 1U) continue;
		*timestamp = __atomic_load_n(&snapshot->time, __ATOMIC_SEQ_CST);
		*flags = __atomic_load_n(&snapshot->flags, __ATOMIC_SEQ_CST);
		after = __atomic_load_n(&snapshot->sequence, __ATOMIC_SEQ_CST);
		if (before != after) continue;
		if (before == 0U) {
			*timestamp = started_at;
			*flags = 0U;
		}
		return true;
	}
	return false;
}

static ER bridge_cycle(bridge_state_t *state, ID buffer, uint32_t now,
		       int connected, wifi_mqtt_bridge_publish_t publish)
{
	INT received;
	int result;

	if (!state->have_pending && connected) {
		received = tk_rcv_mbf(buffer, &state->pending, TMO_POL);
		if (received == E_TMOUT) return E_OK;
		if (received != (INT)sizeof(state->pending) || !valid_packet(&state->pending))
			return E_SYS;
		state->have_pending = true;
	}
	if (!state->have_pending) return E_OK;
	if (now - state->pending.header.timestamp_ms > state->max_age_ms)
		return E_SYS;
	if (!connected) return E_OK;
	result = publish((const uint8_t *)state->pending.payload);
	if (result == WIFI_MQTT_PUBLISH_BUSY) return E_OK;
	if (result != WIFI_MQTT_PUBLISH_OK) return E_SYS;
	state->have_pending = false;
	return E_OK;
}

ER wifi_mqtt_bridge_start(uint32_t max_age_ms)
{
#if TM_MQTT
	T_CMBF config = {0};
	SYSTIM now;
	ID buffer;
	ER result;

	if (__atomic_load_n(&s_status, __ATOMIC_ACQUIRE) != 0) return E_OBJ;
	if (max_age_ms == 0U || max_age_ms > INT32_MAX) return E_PAR;
	if (g_mbf_faults <= 0) return E_OBJ;
	result = tk_get_otm(&now);
	if (result != E_OK) return result < E_OK ? result : E_SYS;
	config.mbfatr = TA_TFIFO | TA_USERBUF;
	config.bufsz = (SZ)sizeof(s_storage);
	config.maxmsz = (INT)sizeof(telemetry_message_t);
	config.bufptr = s_storage;
	buffer = tk_cre_mbf(&config);
	if (buffer < E_OK) return buffer;
	s_buffer = buffer;
	s_bridge.max_age_ms = max_age_ms;
	s_bridge.last_clock_ms = now.lo;
	s_started_at = now.lo;
	__atomic_store_n(&s_status, 1, __ATOMIC_RELEASE);
	return E_OK;
#else
	(void)max_age_ms;
	return E_NOSPT;
#endif
}

ER wifi_mqtt_bridge_submit(const telemetry_message_t *message)
{
	if (__atomic_load_n(&s_status, __ATOMIC_ACQUIRE) != 1) return E_OBJ;
	if (__atomic_load_n(&s_stop_requested, __ATOMIC_ACQUIRE)) return E_OBJ;
	if (message == NULL || !valid_packet(message)) return E_PAR;
	return tk_snd_mbf(s_buffer, message, (INT)sizeof(*message), TMO_POL);
}

ER wifi_mqtt_bridge_link(uint32_t max_age_ms, bool *connected, bool *mqtt_ready)
{
	uint32_t timestamp, flags;
	SYSTIM now;
	ER result;

	if (max_age_ms == 0U || max_age_ms > INT32_MAX ||
	    connected == NULL || mqtt_ready == NULL) return E_PAR;
	if (__atomic_load_n(&s_status, __ATOMIC_ACQUIRE) != 1 ||
	    __atomic_load_n(&s_stop_requested, __ATOMIC_ACQUIRE)) return E_OBJ;
	if (!snapshot_read(&s_snapshot, s_started_at, &timestamp, &flags)) return E_TMOUT;
	result = tk_get_otm(&now);
	if (result != E_OK) return result < E_OK ? result : E_SYS;
	if (now.lo - timestamp > max_age_ms) return E_SYS;
	if (__atomic_load_n(&s_status, __ATOMIC_ACQUIRE) != 1 ||
	    __atomic_load_n(&s_stop_requested, __ATOMIC_ACQUIRE)) return E_OBJ;
	*connected = (flags & 1U) != 0U;
	*mqtt_ready = (flags & 2U) != 0U;
	return E_OK;
}

ER wifi_mqtt_bridge_stop(void)
{
	if (__atomic_load_n(&s_status, __ATOMIC_ACQUIRE) == 0) return E_OBJ;
	__atomic_store_n(&s_stop_requested, 1, __ATOMIC_RELEASE);
	return E_OK;
}

void wifi_mqtt_bridge_publish_failed(void)
{
	if (__atomic_load_n(&s_status, __ATOMIC_ACQUIRE) == 1) {
		__atomic_store_n(&s_status, -1, __ATOMIC_RELEASE);
		tm_printf((UB*)"MQTT telemetry bridge stopped: publish failure\n");
	}
}

void wifi_mqtt_bridge_poll(wifi_mqtt_bridge_publish_t publish, int link_up, int mqtt_ready)
{
	int status = __atomic_load_n(&s_status, __ATOMIC_ACQUIRE);
	SYSTIM now;
	ER result;
	fault_message_t fault = {0};

	if (status == 0) return;
	if (status == 1 && __atomic_load_n(&s_stop_requested, __ATOMIC_ACQUIRE)) return;
	if (status == 1) {
		result = tk_get_otm(&now);
		if (result != E_OK || publish == NULL ||
		    (mqtt_ready && !link_up) ||
		    now.lo - s_bridge.last_clock_ms > INT32_MAX ||
		    bridge_cycle(&s_bridge, s_buffer, now.lo, mqtt_ready, publish) != E_OK) {
			__atomic_store_n(&s_status, -1, __ATOMIC_RELEASE);
			tm_printf((UB*)"MQTT telemetry bridge stopped: clock, message or publish error\n");
			status = -1;
		} else {
			s_bridge.last_clock_ms = now.lo;
			snapshot_write(&s_snapshot, now.lo,
				       (link_up ? 1U : 0U) | (mqtt_ready ? 2U : 0U));
		}
	}
	if (status == -1 && !s_fault_sent) {
		fault.header.type = VEHICLE_MESSAGE_FAULT;
		fault.header.version = VEHICLE_MESSAGE_VERSION;
		fault.header.sequence = 1U;
		if (tk_get_otm(&now) != E_OK) return;
		fault.header.timestamp_ms = now.lo;
		fault.source = VEHICLE_FAULT_COMMUNICATION;
		fault.code = VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_WIFI_BACKEND;
		result = tk_snd_mbf(g_mbf_faults, &fault, (INT)sizeof(fault), TMO_POL);
		if (result == E_OK) s_fault_sent = true;
		else if (!s_fault_delivery_reported) {
			tm_printf((UB*)"MQTT bridge fault delivery failed: %d\n", (int)result);
			s_fault_delivery_reported = true;
		}
	}
}

#if APP_TEST
#include "../test_report.h"

static int s_test_publish_result;
static unsigned s_test_calls;
static uint8_t s_test_payload[WIFI_TELEMETRY_PACKET_SIZE];

static int test_publish(const uint8_t *payload)
{
	unsigned i;
	s_test_calls++;
	if (s_test_publish_result == WIFI_MQTT_PUBLISH_OK) {
		for (i = 0; i < WIFI_TELEMETRY_PACKET_SIZE; i++) s_test_payload[i] = payload[i];
	}
	return s_test_publish_result;
}

BOOL wifi_mqtt_bridge_test(void)
{
	static INT storage[BRIDGE_WORDS];
	T_CMBF config = {0};
	bridge_state_t state = {0};
	telemetry_message_t message = {0};
	wifi_telemetry_data_t data = {0};
	ID buffer;
	BOOL ok;
	BOOL snapshot_ok;
	bridge_snapshot_t snapshot = {0};
	uint32_t timestamp, flags;
	unsigned i;
	TEST_INIT();

	config.mbfatr = TA_TFIFO | TA_USERBUF;
	config.bufsz = (SZ)sizeof(storage);
	config.maxmsz = (INT)sizeof(message);
	config.bufptr = storage;
	buffer = tk_cre_mbf(&config);
	if (buffer < E_OK) {
		TEST_CHECK("WIFI-U04", FALSE, "MQTT outbound bridge queue");
		TEST_SUMMARY("MQTT bridge");
		return FALSE;
	}
	state.max_age_ms = 20U;
	data.battery_pct = 100U;
	s_test_calls = 0U;
	s_test_publish_result = WIFI_MQTT_PUBLISH_BUSY;
	ok = wifi_telemetry_make(&data, 0U, UINT32_MAX - 5U, &message) &&
	     valid_packet(&message) &&
	     tk_snd_mbf(buffer, &message, (INT)sizeof(message), TMO_POL) == E_OK;
	message.payload[3] = 0;
	ok = bridge_cycle(&state, buffer, UINT32_MAX - 4U, 0, test_publish) == E_OK &&
	     s_test_calls == 0U && ok;
	ok = bridge_cycle(&state, buffer, UINT32_MAX - 4U, 1, test_publish) == E_OK &&
	     state.have_pending && s_test_calls == 1U && ok;
	s_test_publish_result = WIFI_MQTT_PUBLISH_OK;
	ok = bridge_cycle(&state, buffer, 3U, 1, test_publish) == E_OK &&
	     !state.have_pending && s_test_calls == 2U && s_test_payload[3] == 100U && ok;
	message.header.timestamp_ms = 10U;
	for (i = 0; i < BRIDGE_CAPACITY; i++)
		ok = tk_snd_mbf(buffer, &message, (INT)sizeof(message), TMO_POL) == E_OK && ok;
	ok = tk_snd_mbf(buffer, &message, (INT)sizeof(message), TMO_POL) == E_TMOUT && ok;
	for (i = 0; i < BRIDGE_CAPACITY; i++)
		ok = bridge_cycle(&state, buffer, 10U, 1, test_publish) == E_OK && ok;
	message.payload[4] = 1;
	ok = !valid_packet(&message) && ok;
	message.payload[4] = 0;
	message.payload[0] = (char)0x20;
	ok = !valid_packet(&message) && ok;
	message.payload[0] = 0;
	message.payload_length = 4U;
	ok = !valid_packet(&message) && ok;
	message.payload_length = WIFI_TELEMETRY_PACKET_SIZE;
	ok = tk_snd_mbf(buffer, &message, (INT)sizeof(message), TMO_POL) == E_OK &&
	     bridge_cycle(&state, buffer, 31U, 1, test_publish) == E_SYS && ok;
	state.have_pending = false;
	message.header.timestamp_ms = 50U;
	s_test_publish_result = WIFI_MQTT_PUBLISH_BUSY;
	ok = tk_snd_mbf(buffer, &message, (INT)sizeof(message), TMO_POL) == E_OK &&
	     bridge_cycle(&state, buffer, 50U, 1, test_publish) == E_OK &&
	     bridge_cycle(&state, buffer, 71U, 0, test_publish) == E_SYS && ok;
	state.have_pending = false;
	message.header.timestamp_ms = 80U;
	ok = tk_snd_mbf(buffer, &message, (INT)sizeof(message), TMO_POL) == E_OK &&
	     bridge_cycle(&state, buffer, 79U, 1, test_publish) == E_SYS && ok;
	state.have_pending = false;
	s_test_publish_result = WIFI_MQTT_PUBLISH_ERROR;
	message.header.timestamp_ms = 40U;
	ok = tk_snd_mbf(buffer, &message, (INT)sizeof(message), TMO_POL) == E_OK &&
	     bridge_cycle(&state, buffer, 40U, 1, test_publish) == E_SYS &&
	     state.have_pending && ok;
	ok = tk_del_mbf(buffer) == E_OK && ok;
	snapshot_ok = snapshot_read(&snapshot, 19U, &timestamp, &flags) &&
		      timestamp == 19U && flags == 0U;
	snapshot_write(&snapshot, 21U, 1U);
	snapshot_ok = snapshot_read(&snapshot, 19U, &timestamp, &flags) &&
		      timestamp == 21U && flags == 1U && snapshot_ok;
	snapshot.sequence = UINT32_MAX - 1U;
	snapshot_write(&snapshot, 22U, 3U);
	snapshot_ok = snapshot_read(&snapshot, 19U, &timestamp, &flags) &&
		      timestamp == 22U && flags == 3U && snapshot_ok;
	snapshot.sequence = 1U;
	snapshot_ok = !snapshot_read(&snapshot, 19U, &timestamp, &flags) && snapshot_ok;
	TEST_CHECK("WIFI-U04", ok, "MQTT bridge copied queue, retry and failures");
	TEST_CHECK("WIFI-U05", snapshot_ok, "network owner link snapshot");
	TEST_SUMMARY("MQTT bridge");
	return ok && snapshot_ok;
}

#ifndef WIFI_MQTT_BRIDGE_H
#define WIFI_MQTT_BRIDGE_H

#include <tk/tkernel.h>
#include "../vehicle_messages.h"

/* Call once from one startup owner after vehicle_buffers_init() and startup
 * tests. NET=lwip MQTT=1 is required. The bridge remains enabled until reboot.
 * No task is created. max_age_ms uses the operating clock and must be <= INT32_MAX. */
ER wifi_mqtt_bridge_start(uint32_t max_age_ms);

/* Copies a telemetry message with a pre-packed legacy five-byte telemetry
 * payload to a separate outbound queue. event_type and priority are ignored.
 * E_OK means queued, not delivered; E_TMOUT means the queue is full.
 * Only the application WiFi service should submit. */
ER wifi_mqtt_bridge_submit(const telemetry_message_t *message);

/* Read an owner-published link snapshot, or return an error if the bridge has
 * faulted, stopped, or the owner has not polled within max_age_ms. */
ER wifi_mqtt_bridge_link(uint32_t max_age_ms, bool *connected, bool *mqtt_ready);

/* Request shutdown without waiting for the network owner. An already-running
 * publish may still complete; queued data is abandoned until reboot. */
ER wifi_mqtt_bridge_stop(void);

#if APP_TEST
BOOL wifi_mqtt_bridge_test(void);
#endif

#endif

#ifndef WIFI_MQTT_BRIDGE_OWNER_H
#define WIFI_MQTT_BRIDGE_OWNER_H

#include <stdint.h>

enum {
	WIFI_MQTT_PUBLISH_OK,
	WIFI_MQTT_PUBLISH_BUSY,
	WIFI_MQTT_PUBLISH_ERROR
};

typedef int (*wifi_mqtt_bridge_publish_t)(const uint8_t *payload);
void wifi_mqtt_bridge_poll(wifi_mqtt_bridge_publish_t publish, int link_up, int mqtt_ready);
void wifi_mqtt_bridge_publish_failed(void);
