=== C:\Users\Yong Jun\school\INF2004-Embedded_Systems\project\INF2004_Project\app_program\wifi\wifi_service.c ===
#include "../app.h"
#include "../vehicle_buffers.h"
#include "wifi_service.h"
#include "wifi_mqtt_bridge.h"

#define WIFI_SERVICE_PRIORITY 10
#define WIFI_FAULT_RETRY_MS 1000U

typedef struct {
	wifi_service_link_t link;
	telemetry_message_t pending;
	uint32_t last_clock_ms;
	uint32_t last_status_ms;
	uint32_t status_sequence;
	uint32_t telemetry_sequence;
	uint32_t telemetry_timestamp_ms;
	ER callback_error;
	bool have_status;
	bool have_pending;
	bool have_telemetry;
} wifi_service_state_t;

static wifi_service_config_t s_config;
static wifi_service_state_t s_state;
static bool s_started;

static bool valid_interval(uint32_t interval)
{
	return interval != 0U && interval <= INT32_MAX;
}

static uint16_t get_clock(wifi_service_state_t *state, uint32_t *now)
{
	SYSTIM time;
	ER er = tk_get_otm(&time);
	if (er != E_OK || time.lo - state->last_clock_ms > INT32_MAX) {
		state->callback_error = er;
		return VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_WIFI_CLOCK;
	}
	*now = time.lo;
	state->last_clock_ms = *now;
	return 0U;
}

static bool same_link(const wifi_service_link_t *a, const wifi_service_link_t *b)
{
	return a->connected == b->connected && a->mqtt_ready == b->mqtt_ready &&
	       a->retry_count == b->retry_count && a->error_code == b->error_code;
}

static uint16_t publish_status(wifi_service_state_t *state,
			       const wifi_service_config_t *config,
			       const wifi_service_link_t *link, uint32_t now)
{
	network_status_message_t status = {0};
	ER er;

	if (state->have_status && same_link(&state->link, link) &&
	    now - state->last_status_ms < config->status_period_ms)
		return 0U;
	status.header.type = VEHICLE_MESSAGE_NETWORK_STATUS;
	status.header.version = VEHICLE_MESSAGE_VERSION;
	status.header.sequence = state->status_sequence + 1U;
	status.header.timestamp_ms = now;
	status.connected = link->connected;
	status.mqtt_ready = link->mqtt_ready;
	status.retry_count = link->retry_count;
	status.error_code = link->error_code;
	er = tk_snd_mbf(g_mbf_network_status, &status, (INT)sizeof(status), TMO_POL);
	if (er != E_OK) {
		state->callback_error = er;
		return VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_WIFI_DELIVERY;
	}
	state->link = *link;
	state->status_sequence = status.header.sequence;
	state->last_status_ms = now;
	state->have_status = true;
	return 0U;
}

static uint16_t receive_telemetry(wifi_service_state_t *state,
				  const wifi_service_config_t *config, uint32_t now)
{
	telemetry_message_t message;
	INT received;

	if (state->have_pending) return 0U;
	received = tk_rcv_mbf(g_mbf_telemetry, &message, TMO_POL);
	if (received == E_TMOUT) return 0U;
	if (received != (INT)sizeof(message) ||
	    message.header.type != VEHICLE_MESSAGE_TELEMETRY ||
	    message.header.version != VEHICLE_MESSAGE_VERSION ||
	    message.payload_length > sizeof(message.payload) ||
	    now - message.header.timestamp_ms > config->telemetry_max_age_ms ||
	    (state->have_telemetry &&
	     (message.header.sequence - state->telemetry_sequence != 1U ||
	      message.header.timestamp_ms - state->telemetry_timestamp_ms > INT32_MAX)))
		return VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_WIFI_TELEMETRY;
	state->pending = message;
	state->have_pending = true;
	return 0U;
}

static uint16_t service_cycle(wifi_service_state_t *state,
			      const wifi_service_config_t *config)
{
	wifi_service_link_t link = {0};
	uint32_t now;
	uint16_t fault;
	ER er = config->poll(config->context, &link);

	if (er != E_OK) {
		state->callback_error = er;
		return VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_WIFI_BACKEND;
	}
	if (link.mqtt_ready && !link.connected)
		return VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_WIFI_BACKEND;
	fault = get_clock(state, &now);
	if (fault != 0U) return fault;
	fault = publish_status(state, config, &link, now);
	if (fault != 0U) return fault;
	if (state->have_pending &&
	    now - state->pending.header.timestamp_ms > config->telemetry_max_age_ms)
		return VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_WIFI_TELEMETRY;
	if (!link.mqtt_ready) return 0U;
	fault = receive_telemetry(state, config, now);
	if (fault != 0U || !state->have_pending) return fault;
	er = config->publish(config->context, &state->pending);
	if (er == E_TMOUT) return 0U;
	if (er != E_OK) {
		state->callback_error = er;
		return VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_WIFI_BACKEND;
	}
	state->telemetry_sequence = state->pending.header.sequence;
	state->telemetry_timestamp_ms = state->pending.header.timestamp_ms;
	state->have_telemetry = true;
	state->have_pending = false;
	return 0U;
}

static void report_fault(uint16_t code, uint32_t now)
{
	fault_message_t fault = {0};
	bool sent = false;
	ER stopped = s_config.stop(s_config.context);

	if (stopped != E_OK) tm_printf((UB*)"WiFi backend stop failed: %d\n", (int)stopped);
	fault.header.type = VEHICLE_MESSAGE_FAULT;
	fault.header.version = VEHICLE_MESSAGE_VERSION;
	fault.header.sequence = 1U;
	fault.header.timestamp_ms = now;
	fault.source = VEHICLE_FAULT_COMMUNICATION;
	fault.code = code;
	fault.recoverable = 0U;
	tm_printf((UB*)"WiFi service stopped; fault=%u error=%d\n",
		  (unsigned)code, (int)s_state.callback_error);
	for (;;) {
		ER er;
		if (!sent) {
			er = tk_snd_mbf(g_mbf_faults, &fault, (INT)sizeof(fault), TMO_POL);
			if (er == E_OK) sent = true;
			else tm_printf((UB*)"WiFi fault delivery failed: %d\n", (int)er);
		}
		er = tk_dly_tsk(WIFI_FAULT_RETRY_MS);
		if (er != E_OK) {
			tm_printf((UB*)"WiFi fault delay failed: %d\n", (int)er);
			er = tk_slp_tsk(TMO_FEVR);
			tm_printf((UB*)"WiFi fault sleep returned: %d\n", (int)er);
		}
	}
}

static void wifi_service_task(INT stacd, void *exinf)
{
	(void)stacd;
	(void)exinf;
	for (;;) {
		uint16_t fault = service_cycle(&s_state, &s_config);
		ER er;
		if (fault != 0U) report_fault(fault, s_state.last_clock_ms);
		er = tk_dly_tsk(s_config.period_ms);
		if (er != E_OK) {
			s_state.callback_error = er;
			report_fault(VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_WIFI_SCHEDULE, s_state.last_clock_ms);
		}
	}
}

ER wifi_service_start(const wifi_service_config_t *config)
{
	T_CTSK task = {0};
	SYSTIM now;
	ID id;
	ER er;

	if (s_started) return E_OBJ;
	if (config == NULL || !valid_interval(config->period_ms) ||
	    !valid_interval(config->status_period_ms) ||
	    !valid_interval(config->telemetry_max_age_ms) ||
	    config->period_ms > config->status_period_ms ||
	    config->period_ms > config->telemetry_max_age_ms ||
	    config->poll == NULL || config->publish == NULL || config->stop == NULL)
		return E_PAR;
	if (g_mbf_telemetry <= 0 || g_mbf_network_status <= 0 || g_mbf_faults <= 0)
		return E_OBJ;
	er = tk_get_otm(&now);
	if (er != E_OK) return er < E_OK ? er : E_SYS;
	s_config = *config;
	s_state = (wifi_service_state_t){0};
	s_state.last_clock_ms = now.lo;
	task.task = (FP)wifi_service_task;
	task.tskatr = TA_HLNG | TA_RNG3;
	task.itskpri = WIFI_SERVICE_PRIORITY;
	task.stksz = TASK_STACK_SIZE;
	id = tk_cre_tsk(&task);
	if (id < E_OK) return id;
	s_started = true;
	er = tk_sta_tsk(id, 0);
	if (er != E_OK) {
		ER cleanup = tk_del_tsk(id);
		if (cleanup != E_OK) tm_printf((UB*)"WiFi task cleanup failed: %d\n", (int)cleanup);
		else s_started = false;
		return er < E_OK ? er : E_SYS;
	}
	return E_OK;
}

static ER mqtt_backend_poll(void *context, wifi_service_link_t *link)
{
	const uint32_t *max_age_ms = context;
	return wifi_mqtt_bridge_link(*max_age_ms, &link->connected, &link->mqtt_ready);
}

static ER mqtt_backend_publish(void *context, const telemetry_message_t *message)
{
	(void)context;
	return wifi_mqtt_bridge_submit(message);
}

static ER mqtt_backend_stop(void *context)
{
	(void)context;
	return wifi_mqtt_bridge_stop();
}

ER wifi_service_start_mqtt(RELTIM period_ms, uint32_t status_period_ms,
			   uint32_t telemetry_max_age_ms, uint32_t owner_max_age_ms)
{
	static uint32_t s_owner_max_age_ms;
	wifi_service_config_t config;
	ER result;

	if (s_started) return E_OBJ;
	if (!valid_interval(period_ms) || !valid_interval(status_period_ms) ||
	    !valid_interval(telemetry_max_age_ms) || !valid_interval(owner_max_age_ms) ||
	    period_ms > status_period_ms || period_ms > telemetry_max_age_ms ||
	    period_ms > owner_max_age_ms) return E_PAR;
	if (g_mbf_telemetry <= 0 || g_mbf_network_status <= 0 || g_mbf_faults <= 0)
		return E_OBJ;
	result = wifi_mqtt_bridge_start(telemetry_max_age_ms);
	if (result != E_OK) return result;
	s_owner_max_age_ms = owner_max_age_ms;
	config = (wifi_service_config_t){period_ms, status_period_ms, telemetry_max_age_ms,
					 mqtt_backend_poll, mqtt_backend_publish,
					 mqtt_backend_stop, &s_owner_max_age_ms};
	result = wifi_service_start(&config);
	if (result != E_OK) {
		ER stopped = wifi_mqtt_bridge_stop();
		if (stopped != E_OK)
			tm_printf((UB*)"MQTT bridge stop request failed: %d\n", (int)stopped);
	}
	return result;
}

#if APP_TEST
#include "../test_report.h"
#include "wifi_command.h"
#include "wifi_telemetry.h"
#include "wifi_telemetry_producer.h"

typedef struct {
	wifi_service_link_t link;
	telemetry_message_t published;
	ER poll_result;
	ER publish_result;
	unsigned publish_calls;
} wifi_test_backend_t;

static ER test_poll(void *context, wifi_service_link_t *link)
{
	wifi_test_backend_t *backend = context;
	*link = backend->link;
	return backend->poll_result;
}

static ER test_publish(void *context, const telemetry_message_t *message)
{
	wifi_test_backend_t *backend = context;
	backend->publish_calls++;
	if (backend->publish_result == E_OK) backend->published = *message;
	return backend->publish_result;
}

static ER test_stop(void *context)
{
	(void)context;
	return E_OK;
}

static BOOL drain_test_buffers(void)
{
	union {
		telemetry_message_t telemetry;
		network_status_message_t status;
	} message;
	INT received;
	BOOL ok;
	do {
		received = tk_rcv_mbf(g_mbf_telemetry, &message, TMO_POL);
	} while (received > 0);
	ok = received == E_TMOUT;
	do {
		received = tk_rcv_mbf(g_mbf_network_status, &message, TMO_POL);
	} while (received > 0);
	return received == E_TMOUT && ok;
}

static BOOL wifi_flow_test(void)
{
	wifi_test_backend_t backend = {0};
	wifi_service_config_t config = {10U, 1000U, 10000U, test_poll, test_publish, test_stop,
					 &backend};
	wifi_service_state_t state = {0};
	telemetry_message_t message = {0};
	network_status_message_t status;
	SYSTIM now;
	BOOL ok;

	if (tk_get_otm(&now) != E_OK) return FALSE;
	state.last_clock_ms = now.lo;
	message.header.type = VEHICLE_MESSAGE_TELEMETRY;
	message.header.version = VEHICLE_MESSAGE_VERSION;
	message.header.sequence = UINT32_MAX;
	message.header.timestamp_ms = now.lo;
	message.payload_length = 2U;
	message.payload[0] = 'O';
	message.payload[1] = 'K';
	ok = tk_snd_mbf(g_mbf_telemetry, &message, (INT)sizeof(message), TMO_POL) == E_OK;
	ok = service_cycle(&state, &config) == 0U && backend.publish_calls == 0U &&
	     tk_rcv_mbf(g_mbf_network_status, &status, TMO_POL) == (INT)sizeof(status) &&
	     !status.connected && !status.mqtt_ready && status.header.sequence == 1U && ok;
	backend.link.connected = true;
	backend.link.mqtt_ready = true;
	backend.publish_result = E_TMOUT;
	ok = service_cycle(&state, &config) == 0U && state.have_pending &&
	     backend.publish_calls == 1U &&
	     tk_rcv_mbf(g_mbf_network_status, &status, TMO_POL) == (INT)sizeof(status) &&
	     status.connected && status.mqtt_ready && status.header.sequence == 2U && ok;
	backend.publish_result = E_OK;
	ok = service_cycle(&state, &config) == 0U && !state.have_pending &&
	     backend.publish_calls == 2U && backend.published.header.sequence == UINT32_MAX &&
	     backend.published.payload[0] == 'O' && backend.published.payload[1] == 'K' && ok;
	message.header.sequence = 0U;
	ok = tk_snd_mbf(g_mbf_telemetry, &message, (INT)sizeof(message), TMO_POL) == E_OK &&
	     service_cycle(&state, &config) == 0U && backend.publish_calls == 3U &&
	     backend.published.header.sequence == 0U && ok;
	state.last_status_ms = UINT32_MAX - 100U;
	ok = publish_status(&state, &config, &backend.link, 898U) == 0U &&
	     tk_rcv_mbf(g_mbf_network_status, &status, TMO_POL) == E_TMOUT && ok;
	ok = publish_status(&state, &config, &backend.link, 899U) == 0U &&
	     tk_rcv_mbf(g_mbf_network_status, &status, TMO_POL) == (INT)sizeof(status) &&
	     status.header.sequence == 3U && status.header.timestamp_ms == 899U && ok;
	return drain_test_buffers() && ok;
}

static BOOL wifi_fault_test(void)
{
	wifi_test_backend_t backend = {0};
	wifi_service_config_t config = {10U, 1000U, 10000U, test_poll, test_publish, test_stop,
					 &backend};
	wifi_service_state_t state = {0};
	telemetry_message_t message = {0};
	network_status_message_t status = {0};
	SYSTIM now;
	BOOL ok;
	unsigned i;

	if (tk_get_otm(&now) != E_OK) return FALSE;
	state.last_clock_ms = now.lo;
	backend.link.mqtt_ready = true;
	ok = service_cycle(&state, &config) == VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_WIFI_BACKEND;
	backend.link.connected = true;
	message.header.type = VEHICLE_MESSAGE_TELEMETRY;
	message.header.version = VEHICLE_MESSAGE_VERSION;
	message.header.sequence = 1U;
	message.header.timestamp_ms = now.lo;
	message.payload_length = VEHICLE_TELEMETRY_PAYLOAD_SIZE + 1U;
	ok = tk_snd_mbf(g_mbf_telemetry, &message, (INT)sizeof(message), TMO_POL) == E_OK &&
	     service_cycle(&state, &config) == VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_WIFI_TELEMETRY && ok;
	ok = drain_test_buffers() && ok;
	state = (wifi_service_state_t){0};
	state.last_clock_ms = now.lo;
	message.payload_length = 0U;
	message.header.timestamp_ms = now.lo - config.telemetry_max_age_ms - 1U;
	ok = tk_snd_mbf(g_mbf_telemetry, &message, (INT)sizeof(message), TMO_POL) == E_OK &&
	     service_cycle(&state, &config) == VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_WIFI_TELEMETRY && ok;
	ok = drain_test_buffers() && ok;
	state = (wifi_service_state_t){0};
	state.last_clock_ms = now.lo;
	for (i = 0U; i < VEHICLE_MB_NETWORK_CAPACITY; i++)
		ok = tk_snd_mbf(g_mbf_network_status, &status, (INT)sizeof(status), TMO_POL) ==
		     E_OK && ok;
	ok = service_cycle(&state, &config) == VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_WIFI_DELIVERY &&
	     state.callback_error == E_TMOUT && ok;
	ok = drain_test_buffers() && ok;
	state = (wifi_service_state_t){0};
	state.last_clock_ms = now.lo;
	message.header.timestamp_ms = now.lo;
	backend.publish_result = E_TMOUT;
	ok = tk_snd_mbf(g_mbf_telemetry, &message, (INT)sizeof(message), TMO_POL) == E_OK &&
	     service_cycle(&state, &config) == 0U && state.have_pending && ok;
	state.pending.header.timestamp_ms = now.lo - config.telemetry_max_age_ms - 1U;
	ok = service_cycle(&state, &config) == VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_WIFI_TELEMETRY &&
	     state.have_pending && ok;
	ok = drain_test_buffers() && ok;
	state = (wifi_service_state_t){0};
	state.last_clock_ms = now.lo;
	backend.publish_result = E_SYS;
	ok = tk_snd_mbf(g_mbf_telemetry, &message, (INT)sizeof(message), TMO_POL) == E_OK &&
	     service_cycle(&state, &config) == VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_WIFI_BACKEND &&
	     state.have_pending && state.callback_error == E_SYS && ok;
	ok = drain_test_buffers() && ok;
	state = (wifi_service_state_t){0};
	state.last_clock_ms = now.lo;
	state.have_telemetry = true;
	state.telemetry_sequence = 5U;
	state.telemetry_timestamp_ms = now.lo;
	message.header.sequence = 7U;
	ok = tk_snd_mbf(g_mbf_telemetry, &message, (INT)sizeof(message), TMO_POL) == E_OK &&
	     service_cycle(&state, &config) == VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_WIFI_TELEMETRY && ok;
	ok = drain_test_buffers() && ok;
	state = (wifi_service_state_t){0};
	state.last_clock_ms = now.lo;
	backend.poll_result = E_SYS;
	ok = service_cycle(&state, &config) == VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_WIFI_BACKEND &&
	     state.callback_error == E_SYS && ok;
	return drain_test_buffers() && ok;
}

static BOOL wifi_telemetry_test(void)
{
	wifi_telemetry_data_t data = {WIFI_TELEMETRY_VEHICLE_RUNNING,
		WIFI_TELEMETRY_LINE_FOUND, WIFI_TELEMETRY_OBSTACLE_DETECTED,
		WIFI_TELEMETRY_TERRAIN_FLAT,
		WIFI_TELEMETRY_FAULT_MOTOR_STALL | WIFI_TELEMETRY_FAULT_WIFI_LOSS, 100U};
	telemetry_message_t message;
	const uint8_t *packet = (const uint8_t *)message.payload;
	BOOL ok;
	unsigned i, j;

	ok = wifi_telemetry_make(&data, 128U, UINT32_MAX, &message) &&
	     message.header.type == VEHICLE_MESSAGE_TELEMETRY &&
	     message.header.version == VEHICLE_MESSAGE_VERSION &&
	     message.header.sequence == 128U && message.header.timestamp_ms == UINT32_MAX &&
	     message.payload_length == WIFI_TELEMETRY_PACKET_SIZE &&
	     packet[0] == 0x05U && packet[1] == 0x91U && packet[2] == 0x01U &&
	     packet[3] == 100U && packet[4] == 0U &&
	     wifi_telemetry_packet_valid(packet, message.payload_length);
	data.vehicle_state = WIFI_TELEMETRY_VEHICLE_COMPLETE;
	data.line_state = WIFI_TELEMETRY_LINE_LOST;
	data.obstacle_state = WIFI_TELEMETRY_OBSTACLE_DETECTED;
	data.terrain_state = WIFI_TELEMETRY_TERRAIN_HUMP;
	data.fault_flags = WIFI_TELEMETRY_FAULT_ALL;
	ok = wifi_telemetry_make(&data, 127U, 0U, &message) &&
	     packet[0] == 0x12U && packet[1] == 0xafU && packet[2] == 0xffU && ok;
	for (i = 0U; i < sizeof(message); i++) ((uint8_t *)&message)[i] = 0xa5U;
	for (i = 0U; i < 6U; i++) {
		wifi_telemetry_data_t invalid = data;
		switch (i) {
		case 0: invalid.vehicle_state = 5U; break;
		case 1: invalid.line_state = 3U; break;
		case 2: invalid.obstacle_state = 3U; break;
		case 3: invalid.terrain_state = 3U; break;
		case 4: invalid.fault_flags = 32U; break;
		default: invalid.battery_pct = 101U; break;
		}
		ok = !wifi_telemetry_make(&invalid, 0U, 0U, &message) && ok;
	}
	ok = !wifi_telemetry_make(NULL, 0U, 0U, &message) &&
	     !wifi_telemetry_make(&data, 0U, 0U, NULL) && ok;
	for (j = 0U; j < sizeof(message); j++)
		ok = ((const uint8_t *)&message)[j] == 0xa5U && ok;
	{
		uint8_t bytes[WIFI_TELEMETRY_PACKET_SIZE] = {0};
		ok = wifi_telemetry_packet_valid(bytes, sizeof(bytes)) &&
		     !wifi_telemetry_packet_valid(NULL, sizeof(bytes)) &&
		     !wifi_telemetry_packet_valid(bytes, sizeof(bytes) - 1U) && ok;
		for (i = 0U; i < 7U; i++) {
			bytes[0] = bytes[1] = bytes[3] = bytes[4] = 0U;
			switch (i) {
			case 0: bytes[0] = 0x20U; break;
			case 1: bytes[0] = 0x14U; break;
			case 2: bytes[0] = 0x03U; break;
			case 3: bytes[1] = 0xc0U; break;
			case 4: bytes[1] = 0x30U; break;
			case 5: bytes[3] = 101U; break;
			default: bytes[4] = 1U; break;
			}
			ok = !wifi_telemetry_packet_valid(bytes, sizeof(bytes)) && ok;
		}
	}
	return ok;
}

static BOOL wifi_producer_test(void)
{
	wifi_telemetry_producer_t producer = {0};
	wifi_telemetry_data_t data = {0};
	wifi_test_backend_t backend = {0};
	wifi_service_config_t config = {10U, 1000U, 10000U, test_poll, test_publish,
					 test_stop, &backend};
	wifi_service_state_t state = {0};
	telemetry_message_t message = {0};
	network_status_message_t status;
	SYSTIM now;
	BOOL ok;
	unsigned i;

	if (tk_get_otm(&now) != E_OK) return FALSE;
	ok = wifi_telemetry_producer_init(&producer, UINT32_MAX, 0U) == E_PAR &&
	     wifi_telemetry_producer_send(&producer, &data, now.lo) == E_OBJ &&
	     wifi_telemetry_producer_init(&producer, UINT32_MAX, 10000U) == E_OK &&
	     wifi_telemetry_producer_init(&producer, 1U, 10000U) == E_OBJ;
	data.battery_pct = 101U;
	ok = wifi_telemetry_producer_send(&producer, &data, now.lo) == E_PAR &&
	     producer.next_sequence == UINT32_MAX && ok;
	data.battery_pct = 80U;
	ok = wifi_telemetry_producer_send(&producer, &data, now.lo - 11000U) == E_PAR &&
	     wifi_telemetry_producer_send(&producer, &data, now.lo + 1000000U) == E_PAR && ok;
	for (i = 0U; i < VEHICLE_MB_TELEMETRY_CAPACITY; i++)
		ok = tk_snd_mbf(g_mbf_telemetry, &message, (INT)sizeof(message), TMO_POL) ==
		     E_OK && ok;
	ok = wifi_telemetry_producer_send(&producer, &data, now.lo) == E_TMOUT &&
	     producer.next_sequence == UINT32_MAX && !producer.have_sent && ok;
	ok = drain_test_buffers() && ok;
	backend.link.connected = true;
	backend.link.mqtt_ready = true;
	state.last_clock_ms = now.lo;
	ok = wifi_telemetry_producer_send(&producer, &data, now.lo) == E_OK &&
	     service_cycle(&state, &config) == 0U && backend.publish_calls == 1U &&
	     backend.published.header.sequence == UINT32_MAX &&
	     (uint8_t)backend.published.payload[2] == 0xfeU &&
	     (uint8_t)backend.published.payload[3] == 80U &&
	     tk_rcv_mbf(g_mbf_network_status, &status, TMO_POL) == (INT)sizeof(status) && ok;
	ok = wifi_telemetry_producer_send(&producer, &data, now.lo - 1U) == E_PAR &&
	     producer.next_sequence == 0U &&
	     wifi_telemetry_producer_send(&producer, &data, now.lo) == E_OK &&
	     service_cycle(&state, &config) == 0U && backend.publish_calls == 2U &&
	     backend.published.header.sequence == 0U &&
	     (uint8_t)backend.published.payload[2] == 0U && ok;
	return drain_test_buffers() && ok;
}

static BOOL wifi_command_test(void)
{
	wifi_command_t command = {99U, 88U, 77U};
	uint8_t packet[WIFI_COMMAND_PACKET_SIZE] = {0x80U, 44U};
	BOOL ok;
	unsigned type;

	ok = wifi_command_decode(packet, sizeof(packet), &command) &&
	     command.type == WIFI_COMMAND_STOP && command.argument == 0U &&
	     command.sequence == 44U;
	packet[0] = 0xc4U;
	packet[1] = 255U;
	ok = wifi_command_decode(packet, sizeof(packet), &command) &&
	     command.type == WIFI_COMMAND_JUNCTION && command.argument == 4U &&
	     command.sequence == 255U && ok;
	for (type = WIFI_COMMAND_START; type <= WIFI_COMMAND_JUNCTION; type++) {
		packet[0] = (uint8_t)((type << 5U) | 31U);
		packet[1] = 0U;
		ok = wifi_command_decode(packet, sizeof(packet), &command) &&
		     command.type == type && command.argument == 31U && command.sequence == 0U && ok;
	}
	packet[0] = 0xc4U;
	packet[1] = 255U;
	ok = wifi_command_decode(packet, sizeof(packet), &command) && ok;
	packet[0] = 0U;
	ok = !wifi_command_decode(packet, sizeof(packet), &command) &&
	     command.type == WIFI_COMMAND_JUNCTION && command.argument == 4U &&
	     command.sequence == 255U && ok;
	packet[0] = 0xe0U;
	ok = !wifi_command_decode(packet, sizeof(packet), &command) &&
	     !wifi_command_decode(packet, 1U, &command) &&
	     !wifi_command_decode(packet, 3U, &command) &&
	     !wifi_command_decode(NULL, sizeof(packet), &command) &&
	     !wifi_command_decode(packet, sizeof(packet), NULL) &&
	     command.type == WIFI_COMMAND_JUNCTION && command.argument == 4U &&
	     command.sequence == 255U && ok;
	return ok;
}

BOOL wifi_service_test(void)
{
	BOOL flow_ok;
	BOOL fault_ok;
	BOOL command_ok;
	BOOL telemetry_ok;
	BOOL producer_ok;
	TEST_INIT();

	flow_ok = wifi_flow_test();
	fault_ok = wifi_fault_test();
	command_ok = wifi_command_test();
	telemetry_ok = wifi_telemetry_test();
	producer_ok = wifi_producer_test();
	TEST_CHECK("WIFI-U01", flow_ok, "link status and telemetry backpressure");
	TEST_CHECK("WIFI-U02", fault_ok, "invalid telemetry and link or delivery failures");
	TEST_CHECK("WIFI-U03", command_ok, "legacy command decode and malformed packets");
	TEST_CHECK("WIFI-U06", telemetry_ok, "legacy telemetry encoding and validation");
	TEST_CHECK("WIFI-U07", producer_ok, "telemetry producer and service queue");
	TEST_SUMMARY("WiFi service");
	return flow_ok && fault_ok && command_ok && telemetry_ok && producer_ok;
}

#ifndef WIFI_SERVICE_H
#define WIFI_SERVICE_H

#include <tk/tkernel.h>
#include "../vehicle_messages.h"

typedef struct {
	bool connected;
	bool mqtt_ready;
	uint8_t retry_count;
	uint8_t error_code;
} wifi_service_link_t;

/* These callbacks run in the application task and must return
 * promptly. They must not call MQTT, lwIP, CYW43 or other network APIs: the
 * template's built-in cyw43_utk_task owns the network stack. poll reports link
 * state and any non-E_OK result stops the service. publish must copy any data
 * needed beyond the call;
 * E_OK means it accepted the whole message, E_TMOUT means nothing was accepted
 * and the service should retry. Other results are failures. stop requests that
 * the backend stop promptly. Context and callbacks must remain valid for the
 * task's entire lifetime. */
typedef ER (*wifi_service_poll_t)(void *context, wifi_service_link_t *link);
typedef ER (*wifi_service_publish_t)(void *context, const telemetry_message_t *message);
typedef ER (*wifi_service_stop_t)(void *context);

typedef struct {
	RELTIM period_ms;
	uint32_t status_period_ms;
	uint32_t telemetry_max_age_ms;
	wifi_service_poll_t poll;
	wifi_service_publish_t publish;
	wifi_service_stop_t stop;
	void *context;
} wifi_service_config_t;

/* Opt-in service. The controller must consume status and faults and handle
 * queue-full errors. Remote commands are not implemented. Callback stalls
 * require independent supervision. */
ER wifi_service_start(const wifi_service_config_t *config);

/* Starts the outbound MQTT bridge and service after buffers and tests. The
 * owner snapshot must be no older than owner_max_age_ms. Startup must be
 * serialized by one owner; a failure after bridge creation requires reboot.
 * The bridge accepts only pre-packed legacy five-byte telemetry messages. */
ER wifi_service_start_mqtt(RELTIM period_ms, uint32_t status_period_ms,
			   uint32_t telemetry_max_age_ms, uint32_t owner_max_age_ms);

#if APP_TEST
BOOL wifi_service_test(void);
#endif

#endif
=== C:\Users\Yong Jun\school\INF2004-Embedded_Systems\project\INF2004_Project\app_program\wifi\wifi_telemetry.c ===
#include "wifi_telemetry.h"

#include <stddef.h>

bool wifi_telemetry_make(const wifi_telemetry_data_t *data, uint32_t sequence,
			 uint32_t timestamp_ms, telemetry_message_t *message)
{
	telemetry_message_t result = {0};
	uint8_t *packet = (uint8_t *)result.payload;

	if (data == NULL || message == NULL ||
	    data->vehicle_state > WIFI_TELEMETRY_VEHICLE_COMPLETE ||
	    data->line_state > WIFI_TELEMETRY_LINE_LOST ||
	    data->obstacle_state > WIFI_TELEMETRY_OBSTACLE_DETECTED ||
	    data->terrain_state > WIFI_TELEMETRY_TERRAIN_HUMP ||
	    data->fault_flags > WIFI_TELEMETRY_FAULT_ALL || data->battery_pct > 100U)
		return false;

	result.header.type = VEHICLE_MESSAGE_TELEMETRY;
	result.header.version = VEHICLE_MESSAGE_VERSION;
	result.header.sequence = sequence;
	result.header.timestamp_ms = timestamp_ms;
	result.payload_length = WIFI_TELEMETRY_PACKET_SIZE;
	packet[0] = (uint8_t)((data->vehicle_state << 2U) | data->line_state);
	packet[1] = (uint8_t)((data->obstacle_state << 6U) |
			      (data->terrain_state << 4U) | (data->fault_flags & 0x0fU));
	packet[2] = (uint8_t)(((sequence & 0x7fU) << 1U) |
			      ((data->fault_flags >> 4U) & 1U));
	packet[3] = data->battery_pct;
	packet[4] = 0U;
	*message = result;
	return true;
}

bool wifi_telemetry_packet_valid(const uint8_t *packet, uint32_t length)
{
	return packet != NULL && length == WIFI_TELEMETRY_PACKET_SIZE &&
	       (packet[0] >> 5U) == 0U &&
	       ((packet[0] >> 2U) & 7U) <= WIFI_TELEMETRY_VEHICLE_COMPLETE &&
	       (packet[0] & 3U) <= WIFI_TELEMETRY_LINE_LOST &&
	       (packet[1] >> 6U) <= WIFI_TELEMETRY_OBSTACLE_DETECTED &&
	       ((packet[1] >> 4U) & 3U) <= WIFI_TELEMETRY_TERRAIN_HUMP &&
	       packet[3] <= 100U && packet[4] == 0U;
}

#ifndef WIFI_TELEMETRY_H
#define WIFI_TELEMETRY_H

#include "../vehicle_messages.h"

#define WIFI_TELEMETRY_PACKET_SIZE 5U

typedef enum {
	WIFI_TELEMETRY_VEHICLE_IDLE = 0,
	WIFI_TELEMETRY_VEHICLE_RUNNING,
	WIFI_TELEMETRY_VEHICLE_PAUSED,
	WIFI_TELEMETRY_VEHICLE_FAULT,
	WIFI_TELEMETRY_VEHICLE_COMPLETE
} wifi_telemetry_vehicle_state_t;

typedef enum {
	WIFI_TELEMETRY_LINE_UNKNOWN = 0,
	WIFI_TELEMETRY_LINE_FOUND,
	WIFI_TELEMETRY_LINE_LOST
} wifi_telemetry_line_state_t;

typedef enum {
	WIFI_TELEMETRY_OBSTACLE_UNKNOWN = 0,
	WIFI_TELEMETRY_OBSTACLE_CLEAR,
	WIFI_TELEMETRY_OBSTACLE_DETECTED
} wifi_telemetry_obstacle_state_t;

typedef enum {
	WIFI_TELEMETRY_TERRAIN_UNKNOWN = 0,
	WIFI_TELEMETRY_TERRAIN_FLAT,
	WIFI_TELEMETRY_TERRAIN_HUMP
} wifi_telemetry_terrain_state_t;

#define WIFI_TELEMETRY_FAULT_MOTOR_STALL 0x01U
#define WIFI_TELEMETRY_FAULT_ENCODER     0x02U
#define WIFI_TELEMETRY_FAULT_IMU         0x04U
#define WIFI_TELEMETRY_FAULT_NO_ECHO     0x08U
#define WIFI_TELEMETRY_FAULT_WIFI_LOSS   0x10U
#define WIFI_TELEMETRY_FAULT_ALL         0x1fU

typedef struct {
	uint8_t vehicle_state;
	uint8_t line_state;
	uint8_t obstacle_state;
	uint8_t terrain_state;
	uint8_t fault_flags;
	uint8_t battery_pct;
} wifi_telemetry_data_t;

/* The seven-bit wire sequence is derived from the low bits of sequence.
 * timestamp_ms must be the operating-clock timestamp of the input data.
 * No unknown-battery value exists in this legacy format. The caller must
 * supply verified data and check the result; output is unchanged on failure. */
bool wifi_telemetry_make(const wifi_telemetry_data_t *data, uint32_t sequence,
			 uint32_t timestamp_ms, telemetry_message_t *message);

bool wifi_telemetry_packet_valid(const uint8_t *packet, uint32_t length);

=== C:\Users\Yong Jun\school\INF2004-Embedded_Systems\project\INF2004_Project\app_program\wifi\wifi_telemetry_producer.c ===
#include "../app.h"
#include "../vehicle_buffers.h"
#include "wifi_telemetry_producer.h"

ER wifi_telemetry_producer_init(wifi_telemetry_producer_t *producer,
				uint32_t first_sequence, uint32_t max_age_ms)
{
	SYSTIM now;
	ER result;

	if (producer == NULL || max_age_ms == 0U || max_age_ms > INT32_MAX) return E_PAR;
	if (producer->initialized || g_mbf_telemetry <= 0) return E_OBJ;
	result = tk_get_otm(&now);
	if (result != E_OK) return result < E_OK ? result : E_SYS;
	*producer = (wifi_telemetry_producer_t){0};
	producer->next_sequence = first_sequence;
	producer->max_age_ms = max_age_ms;
	producer->last_clock_ms = now.lo;
	producer->initialized = true;
	return E_OK;
}

ER wifi_telemetry_producer_send(wifi_telemetry_producer_t *producer,
				const wifi_telemetry_data_t *data, uint32_t timestamp_ms)
{
	telemetry_message_t message;
	SYSTIM now;
	ER result;

	if (producer == NULL || data == NULL) return E_PAR;
	if (!producer->initialized || producer->clock_fault) return E_OBJ;
	result = tk_get_otm(&now);
	if (result != E_OK || now.lo - producer->last_clock_ms > INT32_MAX) {
		producer->clock_fault = true;
		return result < E_OK ? result : E_SYS;
	}
	producer->last_clock_ms = now.lo;
	if (now.lo - timestamp_ms > producer->max_age_ms ||
	    (producer->have_sent &&
	     timestamp_ms - producer->last_timestamp_ms > INT32_MAX) ||
	    !wifi_telemetry_make(data, producer->next_sequence, timestamp_ms, &message))
		return E_PAR;
	result = tk_snd_mbf(g_mbf_telemetry, &message, (INT)sizeof(message), TMO_POL);
	if (result == E_OK) {
		producer->last_timestamp_ms = timestamp_ms;
		producer->have_sent = true;
		producer->next_sequence++;
	}
	return result;
}

#ifndef WIFI_TELEMETRY_PRODUCER_H
#define WIFI_TELEMETRY_PRODUCER_H

#include <tk/tkernel.h>
#include "wifi_telemetry.h"

typedef struct {
	uint32_t next_sequence;
	uint32_t max_age_ms;
	uint32_t last_clock_ms;
	uint32_t last_timestamp_ms;
	bool initialized;
	bool have_sent;
	bool clock_fault;
} wifi_telemetry_producer_t;

/* Initialize a zeroed state after vehicle_buffers_init(). Exactly one task
 * owns this state and is the sole producer of g_mbf_telemetry. max_age_ms
 * must not exceed the service's telemetry age limit. Start the service first. */
ER wifi_telemetry_producer_init(wifi_telemetry_producer_t *producer,
				uint32_t first_sequence, uint32_t max_age_ms);

/* timestamp_ms is the operating-clock timestamp of the oldest input used.
 * The caller supplies verified values, including battery percentage. E_OK
 * means queued, not delivered; E_TMOUT means the caller may retry without
 * advancing sequence. Handle all other errors. A clock fault latches until
 * restart. This function does not start a task or call network APIs. */
ER wifi_telemetry_producer_send(wifi_telemetry_producer_t *producer,
				const wifi_telemetry_data_t *data, uint32_t timestamp_ms);

#endif

/*
 *	app.h
 *	Definitions shared by the whole team.  Changed only by team agreement.
 *
 *	Anything that belongs to ONE subsystem goes in that subsystem's own
 *	header instead (app_program/subsystemN/subsystemN.h).
 */

#ifndef APP_H
#define APP_H

#include <tk/tkernel.h>		/* micro T-Kernel API (tk_xxx_yyy) */
#include <tm/tmonitor.h>	/* T-Monitor API: tm_printf() console output */
#include <bsp/libbsp.h>		/* Board helpers: gpio_set_pin/val, pwm_... */
#include "vehicle_messages.h"

/* ---------------------------------------------------------------------
 *  Pins: free for projects are GP0..GP22 and GP26..GP28.  GP0/GP1 are
 *  taken by UART0 when built with "make CRASH_REPORT=1" (see README).
 *  Never use GP23/24/25/29: they are wired to the Pico W radio.
 *  Claim every pin in TEAM.md section 2 before using it.
 * --------------------------------------------------------------------- */

/* ---------------------------------------------------------------------
 *  Task priorities: 1 is the highest; the kernel's USB console (3) and
 *  WiFi (4) tasks sit above every application task.  Each subsystem
 *  has its own band (TEAM.md section 4), defined in its own header.
 * --------------------------------------------------------------------- */
#define TASK_STACK_SIZE		2048	/* bytes; tm_printf needs ~1 KB */

/* Create and start a task.  prc = TP_PRC1 (core 0), TP_PRC2 (core 1), or
   0 for "any core".  Defined in app_main.c. */
IMPORT ID app_start_task(FP entry, PRI pri, UW prc, const char *name);

/* ---------------------------------------------------------------------
 *  Every subsystem provides these.  usermain() calls ALL the _init()
 *  functions first and only then ALL the _start() functions, so every
 *  kernel object exists before any task can use it.
 *    subsystemN_init()   create this subsystem's kernel objects
 *    subsystemN_start()  create and start this subsystem's tasks
 *    subsystemN_test()   on-target tests, only in test builds (make TEST=1)
 * --------------------------------------------------------------------- */

IMPORT ER subsystem2_start(void);
IMPORT ER subsystem3_start(void);

/* Print a readable message if a kernel call failed (negative error code) */
#define CHECK(er, what)							\
	do {								\
		INT check_er_ = (INT)(er);	/* evaluate once */	\
		if (check_er_ < E_OK) {					\
			tm_printf((UB*)"ERROR %s: %d\n", (what), check_er_); \
		}							\
	} while (0)

/*
 *	app_main.c
 *	START HERE.  usermain() below is your program's entry point.
 *
 *	How micro T-Kernel starts your code
 *	-----------------------------------
 *	  reset -> kernel initialises itself -> creates the "initial task"
 *	        -> initial task calls usermain()
 *
 *	usermain() runs inside that initial task at priority 1 (the highest).
 *	On a single core nothing you create runs until usermain() blocks, but
 *	on the dual-core build the OTHER core may start a new task at once.
 *	So usermain() creates vehicle buffers first. When EXAMPLES=1,
 *	it then creates every example subsystem's objects and starts its tasks.
 *	and then puts the initial task to sleep forever.
 *	If usermain() RETURNS, the kernel shuts the whole system down.
 *
 *	The example system (enabled with EXAMPLES=1): one task per folder
 *	------------------------------------------------------------
 *	  subsystem1/  LED       blinks GP19, paced by a cyclic handler
 *	  subsystem2/  Producer  makes a "sample" every 300 ms     (core 1)
 *	  subsystem3/  Consumer  collects samples into statistics  (core 0)
 *	  subsystem4/  Monitor   prints reports, pauses/resumes the LED
 *	  subsystem5/  (empty template for the fifth student)
 *
 *	How they talk to each other (the interfaces, see TEAM.md section 5):
 *	  IF-01  S2 -> S3  mailbox mbx_samples      (owned by S3)
 *	  IF-02  S3 -> S2  semaphore sem_slots + memory pool mpf_samples
 *	                   (owned by S2: S3 hands slots and blocks back)
 *	  IF-03  S3 -> S4  message buffer mbf_summary + event flag flg_events
 *	                   (owned by S4)
 *	  IF-04  S4 -> S3  reads app_stats under mutex mtx_stats (owned by S3)
 *	  IF-05  S4 -> S1  suspends/resumes the LED task tid_led (owned by S1)
 *	The subsystem that RECEIVES or OWNS the data creates the object and
 *	publishes its ID in its header; the other side just uses it.
 *
 *	micro T-Kernel APIs used, and where to find them:
 *	  tasks       tk_cre_tsk tk_sta_tsk tk_slp_tsk tk_wup_tsk tk_can_wup
 *	              tk_dly_tsk tk_sus_tsk tk_rsm_tsk tk_chg_pri tk_ref_tsk
 *	              tk_get_tid tk_get_prc                         app_main.c, S1-S4
 *	  semaphore   tk_cre_sem tk_wai_sem tk_sig_sem tk_ref_sem       S2, S3, S4
 *	  mem. pool   tk_cre_mpf tk_get_mpf tk_rel_mpf tk_ref_mpf       S2, S3, S4
 *	  mailbox     tk_cre_mbx tk_snd_mbx tk_rcv_mbx                  S2, S3
 *	  mutex       tk_cre_mtx tk_loc_mtx tk_unl_mtx                  S3, S4
 *	  msg buffer  tk_cre_mbf tk_snd_mbf tk_rcv_mbf                  S3, S4
 *	  event flag  tk_cre_flg tk_set_flg tk_wai_flg                  S3, S4
 *	  cyclic      tk_cre_cyc tk_ref_cyc                             S1
 *	  alarm       tk_cre_alm tk_sta_alm tk_stp_alm                  S4
 *	  time        tk_get_tim                                        S2, S4
 *	  system      tk_ref_ver                                        app_main.c
 *
 *	Output appears on the USB serial port (115200 8N1 in any terminal).
 */

/*
 * Create and start one task (declared in app.h for every subsystem).
 *   prc = TP_PRC1 (core 0), TP_PRC2 (core 1), or 0 for "any core".
 */
EXPORT ID app_start_task(FP entry, PRI pri, UW prc, const char *name)
{
	T_CTSK	ctsk = {0};
	ID	tid;
	ER	er;

	ctsk.task    = entry;		/* function the task runs */
	ctsk.itskpri = pri;		/* initial priority (1 = highest) */
	ctsk.stksz   = TASK_STACK_SIZE;	/* kernel allocates the stack */
	ctsk.tskatr  = TA_HLNG		/* written in a high-level language (C) */
		     | TA_RNG3;		/* runs at user protection level */
#if TK_SUPPORT_SMP
	if (prc != 0) {
		ctsk.tskatr |= TA_ASSPRC;	/* honour the affinity below */
		ctsk.assprc  = prc;		/* which core(s) it may run on */
	}
#else
	(void)prc;			/* single-core build: no affinity */
#endif

	/* tk_cre_tsk: create the task.  It starts in the DORMANT state. */
	tid = tk_cre_tsk(&ctsk);
	if (tid < E_OK) {
		CHECK(tid, name);
		return tid;
	}

	/* tk_sta_tsk: DORMANT -> READY.  The int is passed as stacd. */
	er = tk_sta_tsk(tid, 0);
	CHECK(er, name);
	return tid;
}

EXPORT INT usermain(void)
{
	T_RVER	ver;
	ER	er;

	/* tk_ref_ver: ask the kernel who it is */
	tk_ref_ver(&ver);
	tm_printf((UB*)"\n=== micro T-Kernel on Raspberry Pi Pico W"
		  " (spec 0x%04x, product 0x%04x) ===\n", ver.spver, ver.prver);
#if TK_SUPPORT_SMP
	tm_printf((UB*)"dual-core (SMP) build, usermain on core %d\n",
		  tk_get_prc() - 1);	/* tk_get_prc() counts from 1 */
#else
	tm_printf((UB*)"single-core build\n");
#endif

	/* Phase 1: every subsystem creates its kernel objects */
	er = vehicle_buffers_init();
	if (er < E_OK) {
		tm_printf((UB*)"vehicle buffer initialization failed; tasks not started\n");
		for (;;) {
			tk_dly_tsk(1000);
		}
	}
#if APP_EXAMPLES
	CHECK(subsystem1_init(), "subsystem1_init");
	CHECK(subsystem2_init(), "subsystem2_init");
	CHECK(subsystem3_init(), "subsystem3_init");
	CHECK(subsystem4_init(), "subsystem4_init");
	CHECK(subsystem5_init(), "subsystem5_init");
#endif

#if APP_TEST
	if (!vehicle_buffers_test()) {
		for (;;) {
			tm_printf((UB*)"[TEST] INT-03 FAIL  vehicle buffers; tasks not started\n");
			tk_dly_tsk(1000);
		}
	}
	if (!odometry_test()) {
		for (;;) {
			tm_printf((UB*)"[TEST] ODO FAIL  odometry calculations; tasks not started\n");
			tk_dly_tsk(1000);
		}
	}
	if (!odometry_producer_test()) {
		for (;;) {
			tm_printf((UB*)"[TEST] ODO-PRODUCER FAIL  producer tests; tasks not started\n");
			tk_dly_tsk(1000);
		}
	}
	if (!odometry_task_test()) {
		for (;;) {
			tm_printf((UB*)"[TEST] ODO-TASK FAIL  task tests; tasks not started\n");
			tk_dly_tsk(1000);
		}
	}
	if (!line_observation_test()) {
		for (;;) {
			tm_printf((UB*)"[TEST] LINE FAIL  line classification; tasks not started\n");
			tk_dly_tsk(1000);
		}
	}
	if (!barcode_scanner_test()) {
		for (;;) {
			tm_printf((UB*)"[TEST] BAR FAIL  barcode decoder; tasks not started\n");
			tk_dly_tsk(1000);
		}
	}
	if (!line_barcode_task_test()) {
		for (;;) {
			tm_printf((UB*)"[TEST] LB-TASK FAIL  task tests; tasks not started\n");
			tk_dly_tsk(1000);
		}
	}
	if (!line_adc_reader_test()) {
		for (;;) {
			tm_printf((UB*)"[TEST] LINE-ADC FAIL  reader tests; tasks not started\n");
			tk_dly_tsk(1000);
		}
	}
	if (!imu_processor_test()) {
		for (;;) {
			tm_printf((UB*)"[TEST] IMU FAIL  processing; tasks not started\n");
			tk_dly_tsk(1000);
		}
	}
	if (!imu_task_test()) {
		for (;;) {
			tm_printf((UB*)"[TEST] IMU-TASK FAIL  task tests; tasks not started\n");
			tk_dly_tsk(1000);
		}
	}
	if (!obs_profile_test()) {
		for (;;) {
			tm_printf((UB*)"[TEST] OBS FAIL  profiling; tasks not started\n");
			tk_dly_tsk(1000);
		}
	}
	if (!obs_task_test()) {
		for (;;) {
			tm_printf((UB*)"[TEST] OBS-TASK FAIL  task tests; tasks not started\n");
			tk_dly_tsk(1000);
		}
	}
	if (!wheel_speed_test()) {
		for (;;) {
			tm_printf((UB*)"[TEST] MOT FAIL  wheel speed control; tasks not started\n");
			tk_dly_tsk(1000);
		}
	}
	if (!motion_task_test()) {
		for (;;) {
			tm_printf((UB*)"[TEST] MOT-TASK FAIL  simulation tests; tasks not started\n");
			tk_dly_tsk(1000);
		}
	}
	if (!car_gate_test()) {
		for (;;) {
			tm_printf((UB*)"[TEST] VC FAIL  controller gate; tasks not started\n");
			tk_dly_tsk(1000);
		}
	}
	if (!line_steering_test()) {
		for (;;) {
			tm_printf((UB*)"[TEST] VC FAIL  line steering; tasks not started\n");
			tk_dly_tsk(1000);
		}
	}
	if (!controller_monitor_test()) {
		for (;;) {
			tm_printf((UB*)"[TEST] VC-MONITOR FAIL  monitor tests; tasks not started\n");
			tk_dly_tsk(1000);
		}
	}
	if (!vehicle_controller_task_test()) {
		for (;;) {
			tm_printf((UB*)"[TEST] VC-TASK FAIL  vehicle controller tests; tasks not started\n");
			tk_dly_tsk(1000);
		}
	}
	if (!wifi_service_test()) {
		for (;;) {
			tm_printf((UB*)"[TEST] WIFI FAIL  service tests; tasks not started\n");
			tk_dly_tsk(1000);
		}
		if (!wifi_mqtt_bridge_test()) {
			for (;;) {
				tm_printf((UB*)"[TEST] WIFI-BRIDGE FAIL  bridge tests; tasks not started\n");
				tk_dly_tsk(1000);

	if (!gpio_irq_test()) {
		for (;;) {
			tm_printf((UB*)"[TEST] GPIO FAIL  interrupt routing; tasks not started\n");
			tk_dly_tsk(1000);
		}
	}
#endif

	/* Phase 2: start the opt-in template example tasks. */
#if APP_EXAMPLES
	CHECK(subsystem1_start(), "subsystem1_start");
	CHECK(subsystem2_start(), "subsystem2_start");
	CHECK(subsystem3_start(), "subsystem3_start");
	CHECK(subsystem4_start(), "subsystem4_start");
	CHECK(subsystem5_start(), "subsystem5_start");

	tm_printf((UB*)"template example subsystems started\n\n");
#else
#if APP_ODOMETRY_TEST
	er = encoder_bringup_start();
	if (er < E_OK) {
		for (;;) {
			tm_printf((UB*)"encoder task start failed: %d\n", (int)er);
			tk_dly_tsk(1000);
		}
	}
	tm_printf((UB*)"encoder bring-up task started\n\n");
#else
	tm_printf((UB*)"template examples disabled; no vehicle tasks started yet\n\n");
#endif
#endif

#if APP_TEST
	/* With examples enabled, run every example subsystem's tests in turn,
	   here in the initial task, while the system is running.  Tests may
	   call tk_dly_tsk() to let the other tasks work for a while. */
	tm_printf((UB*)"[TEST] === test run start ===\n");
#if APP_EXAMPLES
	subsystem1_test();
	subsystem2_test();
	subsystem3_test();
	subsystem4_test();
	subsystem5_test();
#endif
	tm_printf((UB*)"[TEST] INT-03 PASS  vehicle buffer startup result (repeated)\n");
	tm_printf((UB*)"[TEST] ODO PASS  odometry startup tests (repeated)\n");
	tm_printf((UB*)"[TEST] ODO-PRODUCER PASS  producer tests (repeated)\n");
	tm_printf((UB*)"[TEST] ODO-TASK PASS  task tests (repeated)\n");
	tm_printf((UB*)"[TEST] LINE PASS  line classification tests (repeated)\n");
	tm_printf((UB*)"[TEST] BAR PASS  barcode decoder tests (repeated)\n");
	tm_printf((UB*)"[TEST] LB-TASK PASS  task tests (repeated)\n");
	tm_printf((UB*)"[TEST] LINE-ADC PASS  reader tests (repeated)\n");
	tm_printf((UB*)"[TEST] IMU PASS  IMU processing tests (repeated)\n");
	tm_printf((UB*)"[TEST] OBS PASS  obstacle profile tests (repeated)\n");
	tm_printf((UB*)"[TEST] MOT PASS  wheel speed tests (repeated)\n");
	tm_printf((UB*)"[TEST] VC PASS  controller gate tests (repeated)\n");
	tm_printf((UB*)"[TEST] GPIO PASS  interrupt routing tests (repeated)\n");
	tm_printf((UB*)"[TEST] === test run end ===\n");
#endif

#if APP_TEST && !APP_EXAMPLES
	/* Keep the startup test result visible after USB serial connects. */
	for (;;) {
		tk_dly_tsk(5000);
		tm_printf((UB*)"[TEST] INT-03 PASS  vehicle buffer startup result (repeated)\n");
		tm_printf((UB*)"[TEST] ODO PASS  odometry startup tests (repeated)\n");
		tm_printf((UB*)"[TEST] ODO-PRODUCER PASS  producer tests (repeated)\n");
		tm_printf((UB*)"[TEST] ODO-TASK PASS  task tests (repeated)\n");
		tm_printf((UB*)"[TEST] LINE PASS  line classification tests (repeated)\n");
		tm_printf((UB*)"[TEST] BAR PASS  barcode decoder tests (repeated)\n");
		tm_printf((UB*)"[TEST] LB-TASK PASS  task tests (repeated)\n");
		tm_printf((UB*)"[TEST] LINE-ADC PASS  reader tests (repeated)\n");
		tm_printf((UB*)"[TEST] IMU PASS  IMU processing tests (repeated)\n");
		tm_printf((UB*)"[TEST] OBS PASS  obstacle profile tests (repeated)\n");
		tm_printf((UB*)"[TEST] MOT PASS  wheel speed tests (repeated)\n");
		tm_printf((UB*)"[TEST] VC PASS  controller gate tests (repeated)\n");
		tm_printf((UB*)"[TEST] GPIO PASS  interrupt routing tests (repeated)\n");
	}
#else
	/* Sleep forever.  Returning from usermain() would shut down. */
	tk_slp_tsk(TMO_FEVR);
	return 0;
#endif
}

/*
 *	test_report.h
 *	Minimal on-target test helpers.
 *
 *	Every check prints ONE line in a fixed format, so a saved console log
 *	can be searched for results ("grep '\[TEST\]' log.txt"):
 *
 *	    [TEST] S1-U01 PASS  ADC reads in range
 *	    [TEST] S1-U02 FAIL  queue rejects overflow   (task_x.c:57)
 *	    [TEST] subsystem1 SUMMARY 1 passed, 1 failed
 *
 *	Usage, inside a test function:
 *	    TEST_INIT();
 *	    TEST_CHECK("S1-U01", value >= 0 && value <= 4095, "ADC reads in range");
 *	    TEST_SUMMARY("subsystem1");
 *
 *	Test IDs must match the ones in testing/<subsystem>/TEST_LOG.md.
 */

#ifndef TEST_REPORT_H
#define TEST_REPORT_H

#include <tm/tmonitor.h>

#define TEST_INIT()	INT test_pass_ = 0, test_fail_ = 0

#define TEST_CHECK(id, cond, desc)					\
	do {								\
		if (cond) {						\
			test_pass_++;					\
			tm_printf((UB*)"[TEST] %s PASS  %s\n", (id), (desc)); \
		} else {						\
			test_fail_++;					\
			tm_printf((UB*)"[TEST] %s FAIL  %s   (%s:%d)\n",	\
				  (id), (desc), __FILE__, __LINE__);	\
		}							\
	} while (0)

#define TEST_SUMMARY(name)						\
	tm_printf((UB*)"[TEST] %s SUMMARY %d passed, %d failed\n",	\
		  (name), test_pass_, test_fail_)

#endif /* TEST_REPORT_H */

#include "app.h"
#include "vehicle_buffers.h"

#if APP_TEST
#include "test_report.h"
#endif

/*
 * The RP2040 kernel uses an INT-sized header and rounds each message up to
 * sizeof(INT) bytes in its ring buffer (kernel/tkernel/messagebuf.h).
 */
#define BUFFER_WORDS(type, capacity) \
	((capacity) * (1U + ((sizeof(type) + sizeof(INT) - 1U) / sizeof(INT))))
#define DEFINE_BUFFER(name, type, capacity) \
	ID g_mbf_##name; \
	static INT s_##name##_storage[BUFFER_WORDS(type, capacity)]

DEFINE_BUFFER(line_observations, line_observation_message_t, VEHICLE_MB_LINE_CAPACITY);
DEFINE_BUFFER(barcode_events, barcode_event_message_t, VEHICLE_MB_BARCODE_CAPACITY);
DEFINE_BUFFER(imu_estimates, imu_estimate_message_t, VEHICLE_MB_IMU_CAPACITY);
DEFINE_BUFFER(obstacle_events, obstacle_event_message_t, VEHICLE_MB_OBSTACLE_CAPACITY);
DEFINE_BUFFER(obstacle_commands, obstacle_command_message_t, VEHICLE_MB_OBSTACLE_COMMAND_CAPACITY);
DEFINE_BUFFER(line_odometry, wheel_odometry_message_t, VEHICLE_MB_ODOMETRY_CAPACITY);
DEFINE_BUFFER(imu_odometry, wheel_odometry_message_t, VEHICLE_MB_ODOMETRY_CAPACITY);
DEFINE_BUFFER(motion_odometry, wheel_odometry_message_t, VEHICLE_MB_ODOMETRY_CAPACITY);
DEFINE_BUFFER(motion_commands, motion_command_message_t, VEHICLE_MB_MOTION_CAPACITY);
DEFINE_BUFFER(motion_status, motion_status_message_t, VEHICLE_MB_STATUS_CAPACITY);
DEFINE_BUFFER(remote_commands, remote_command_message_t, VEHICLE_MB_REMOTE_CAPACITY);
DEFINE_BUFFER(network_status, network_status_message_t, VEHICLE_MB_NETWORK_CAPACITY);
DEFINE_BUFFER(telemetry, telemetry_message_t, VEHICLE_MB_TELEMETRY_CAPACITY);
DEFINE_BUFFER(faults, fault_message_t, VEHICLE_MB_FAULT_CAPACITY);

typedef struct {
	ID *id;
	INT *storage;
	SZ storage_size;
	INT message_size;
	UINT capacity;
	const char *name;
} vehicle_buffer_definition_t;

#define BUFFER_ENTRY(name, type, capacity) \
	{ &g_mbf_##name, s_##name##_storage, (SZ)sizeof(s_##name##_storage), \
	  (INT)sizeof(type), (capacity), #name }

static const vehicle_buffer_definition_t s_buffers[] = {
	BUFFER_ENTRY(line_observations, line_observation_message_t, VEHICLE_MB_LINE_CAPACITY),
	BUFFER_ENTRY(barcode_events, barcode_event_message_t, VEHICLE_MB_BARCODE_CAPACITY),
	BUFFER_ENTRY(imu_estimates, imu_estimate_message_t, VEHICLE_MB_IMU_CAPACITY),
	BUFFER_ENTRY(obstacle_events, obstacle_event_message_t, VEHICLE_MB_OBSTACLE_CAPACITY),
	BUFFER_ENTRY(obstacle_commands, obstacle_command_message_t, VEHICLE_MB_OBSTACLE_COMMAND_CAPACITY),
	BUFFER_ENTRY(line_odometry, wheel_odometry_message_t, VEHICLE_MB_ODOMETRY_CAPACITY),
	BUFFER_ENTRY(imu_odometry, wheel_odometry_message_t, VEHICLE_MB_ODOMETRY_CAPACITY),
	BUFFER_ENTRY(motion_odometry, wheel_odometry_message_t, VEHICLE_MB_ODOMETRY_CAPACITY),
	BUFFER_ENTRY(motion_commands, motion_command_message_t, VEHICLE_MB_MOTION_CAPACITY),
	BUFFER_ENTRY(motion_status, motion_status_message_t, VEHICLE_MB_STATUS_CAPACITY),
	BUFFER_ENTRY(remote_commands, remote_command_message_t, VEHICLE_MB_REMOTE_CAPACITY),
	BUFFER_ENTRY(network_status, network_status_message_t, VEHICLE_MB_NETWORK_CAPACITY),
	BUFFER_ENTRY(telemetry, telemetry_message_t, VEHICLE_MB_TELEMETRY_CAPACITY),
	BUFFER_ENTRY(faults, fault_message_t, VEHICLE_MB_FAULT_CAPACITY)
};

ER vehicle_buffers_init(void)
{
	UINT i;

	for (i = 0; i < sizeof(s_buffers) / sizeof(s_buffers[0]); ++i) {
		T_CMBF config = {0};
		ID id;

		config.mbfatr = TA_TFIFO | TA_USERBUF;
		config.bufsz = s_buffers[i].storage_size;
		config.maxmsz = s_buffers[i].message_size;
		config.bufptr = s_buffers[i].storage;
		id = tk_cre_mbf(&config);
		if (id < E_OK) {
			ER error = id;
			tm_printf((UB*)"ERROR creating vehicle buffer %s: %d\n",
				  s_buffers[i].name, (int)error);
			while (i > 0) {
				ER cleanup;
				--i;
				cleanup = tk_del_mbf(*s_buffers[i].id);
				if (cleanup < E_OK) {
					tm_printf((UB*)"ERROR deleting vehicle buffer %s: %d\n",
						  s_buffers[i].name, (int)cleanup);
				} else {
					*s_buffers[i].id = 0;
				}
			}
			return error;
		}
		*s_buffers[i].id = id;
	}
	return E_OK;
}

#if APP_TEST
BOOL vehicle_buffers_test(void)
{
	union {
		line_observation_message_t line;
		barcode_event_message_t barcode;
		imu_estimate_message_t imu;
		obstacle_event_message_t obstacle;
		obstacle_command_message_t obstacle_command;
		wheel_odometry_message_t odometry;
		motion_command_message_t motion_command;
		motion_status_message_t motion_status;
		remote_command_message_t remote;
		network_status_message_t network;
		telemetry_message_t telemetry;
		fault_message_t fault;
	} sent, received;
	UINT i;
	BOOL ok = TRUE;
	TEST_INIT();

	for (i = 0; i < sizeof(s_buffers) / sizeof(s_buffers[0]); ++i) {
		UINT j;
		const vehicle_buffer_definition_t *buffer = &s_buffers[i];
		BOOL buffer_ok = TRUE;

		for (j = 0; j < buffer->capacity; ++j) {
			UINT k;
			for (k = 0; k < (UINT)buffer->message_size; ++k) {
				((UB *)&sent)[k] = (UB)(j + 1U);
			}
			if (tk_snd_mbf(*buffer->id, &sent, buffer->message_size,
				       TMO_POL) != E_OK) {
				buffer_ok = FALSE;
				break;
			}
		}
		if (j == buffer->capacity &&
		    tk_snd_mbf(*buffer->id, &sent, buffer->message_size,
			       TMO_POL) != E_TMOUT) {
			buffer_ok = FALSE;
		}
		for (j = 0; j < buffer->capacity; ++j) {
			UINT k;
			if (tk_rcv_mbf(*buffer->id, &received, TMO_POL) !=
			    buffer->message_size) {
				buffer_ok = FALSE;
				break;
			}
			for (k = 0; k < (UINT)buffer->message_size; ++k) {
				if (((UB *)&received)[k] != (UB)(j + 1U)) {
					buffer_ok = FALSE;
					break;
				}
			}
		}
		if (!buffer_ok) {
			tm_printf((UB*)"vehicle buffer test failed: %s\n", buffer->name);
			ok = FALSE;
		}
	}
	TEST_CHECK("INT-03", ok, "vehicle buffers copy messages and hold configured capacity");
	TEST_SUMMARY("vehicle buffers");
	return ok;
}
#endif

#ifndef VEHICLE_MESSAGES_H
#define VEHICLE_MESSAGES_H

#include <stdbool.h>
#include <stdint.h>

/*
 * Inter-task messages are copied into ÂµT-Kernel message buffers. Messages
 * therefore contain values only; they must not contain pointers to task-owned
 * or stack storage.
 */

#define VEHICLE_MESSAGE_VERSION            1U
#define VEHICLE_BARCODE_PAYLOAD_SIZE       32U
#define VEHICLE_TELEMETRY_PAYLOAD_SIZE     128U
#define VEHICLE_REMOTE_ARGUMENT_SIZE       32U
#define VEHICLE_MOTION_STATUS_PERIOD_MS  100U
/* A clear validity bit means the corresponding fields are not measurements. */
#define VEHICLE_IMU_VALID_ROLL_PITCH       0x0001U
#define VEHICLE_IMU_VALID_YAW              0x0002U
#define VEHICLE_IMU_VALID_GYRO             0x0004U

typedef enum {
    VEHICLE_MESSAGE_LINE_OBSERVATION = 1,
    VEHICLE_MESSAGE_BARCODE_EVENT,
    VEHICLE_MESSAGE_IMU_ESTIMATE,
    VEHICLE_MESSAGE_OBSTACLE_EVENT,
    VEHICLE_MESSAGE_OBSTACLE_COMMAND,
    VEHICLE_MESSAGE_WHEEL_ODOMETRY,
    VEHICLE_MESSAGE_MOTION_COMMAND,
    VEHICLE_MESSAGE_MOTION_STATUS,
    VEHICLE_MESSAGE_REMOTE_COMMAND,
    VEHICLE_MESSAGE_NETWORK_STATUS,
    VEHICLE_MESSAGE_TELEMETRY,
    VEHICLE_MESSAGE_FAULT
} vehicle_message_type_t;

typedef enum {
    VEHICLE_BUFFER_LINE_OBSERVATIONS = 1,
    VEHICLE_BUFFER_BARCODE_EVENTS,
    VEHICLE_BUFFER_IMU_ESTIMATES,
    VEHICLE_BUFFER_OBSTACLE_EVENTS,
    VEHICLE_BUFFER_OBSTACLE_COMMANDS,
    VEHICLE_BUFFER_LINE_ODOMETRY,
    VEHICLE_BUFFER_IMU_ODOMETRY,
    VEHICLE_BUFFER_MOTION_COMMANDS,
    VEHICLE_BUFFER_MOTION_STATUS,
    VEHICLE_BUFFER_REMOTE_COMMANDS,
    VEHICLE_BUFFER_NETWORK_STATUS,
    VEHICLE_BUFFER_TELEMETRY,
    VEHICLE_BUFFER_FAULTS,
    VEHICLE_BUFFER_MOTION_ODOMETRY
} vehicle_buffer_id_t;

typedef enum {
    VEHICLE_BUFFER_KEEP_LATEST = 1,
    VEHICLE_BUFFER_KEEP_ORDERED,
    VEHICLE_BUFFER_PRIORITY
} vehicle_buffer_policy_t;

typedef enum {
    VEHICLE_MOTION_STOP = 0,
    VEHICLE_MOTION_ARM,
    VEHICLE_MOTION_VELOCITY,
    VEHICLE_MOTION_TURN,
    VEHICLE_MOTION_EMERGENCY_STOP
} vehicle_motion_mode_t;

typedef enum {
    VEHICLE_FAULT_NONE = 0,
    VEHICLE_FAULT_SENSOR,
    VEHICLE_FAULT_MOTION,
    VEHICLE_FAULT_COMMUNICATION,
    VEHICLE_FAULT_SYSTEM
} vehicle_fault_source_t;

typedef enum {
    VEHICLE_OBSTACLE_SCAN_REQUEST = 1,
    VEHICLE_OBSTACLE_BYPASS_COMPLETE,
    VEHICLE_OBSTACLE_ABORT,
    VEHICLE_OBSTACLE_RESET
} vehicle_obstacle_command_t;

typedef enum {
    VEHICLE_OBSTACLE_AHEAD_READING = 1,
    VEHICLE_OBSTACLE_SCAN_CLEAR,
    VEHICLE_OBSTACLE_SCAN_FOUND
} vehicle_obstacle_event_class_t;

typedef enum {
    VEHICLE_OBSTACLE_ADVICE_NONE = 0,
    VEHICLE_OBSTACLE_ADVICE_LEFT,
    VEHICLE_OBSTACLE_ADVICE_RIGHT,
    VEHICLE_OBSTACLE_ADVICE_REVERSE
} vehicle_obstacle_advice_t;

#define VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_IMU_I2C 1U
#define VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_ODOMETRY_SOURCE 2U
#define VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_ODOMETRY_CALCULATION 3U
#define VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_ODOMETRY_DELIVERY 4U
#define VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_ODOMETRY_CLOCK 5U
#define VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_ODOMETRY_SCHEDULE 6U
#define VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_LINE_SENSOR 7U
#define VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_LINE_ODOMETRY 8U
#define VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_LINE_BARCODE 9U
#define VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_LINE_DELIVERY 10U
#define VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_LINE_CLOCK 11U
#define VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_LINE_SCHEDULE 12U
#define VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_IMU_SENSOR 13U
#define VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_IMU_ODOMETRY 14U
#define VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_IMU_PROCESSING 15U
#define VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_IMU_DELIVERY 16U
#define VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_IMU_CLOCK 17U
#define VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_IMU_SCHEDULE 18U
#define VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_OBSTACLE_SENSOR 19U
#define VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_OBSTACLE_COMMAND 20U
#define VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_OBSTACLE_DELIVERY 21U
#define VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_OBSTACLE_CLOCK 22U
#define VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_OBSTACLE_SCHEDULE 23U
#define VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_OBSTACLE_SCAN_TIMEOUT 24U
#define VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_MOTION_COMMAND 25U
#define VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_MOTION_ODOMETRY 26U
#define VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_MOTION_CONTROL 27U
#define VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_MOTION_CLOCK 28U
#define VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_MOTION_SCHEDULE 29U
#define VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_MOTION_EMERGENCY 30U
#define VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_MOTION_ACTUATOR 36U
#define VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_MOTION_STATUS 37U
#define VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_WIFI_BACKEND 31U
#define VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_WIFI_DELIVERY 32U
#define VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_WIFI_TELEMETRY 33U
#define VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_WIFI_CLOCK 34U
#define VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_WIFI_SCHEDULE 35U

typedef struct {
    uint16_t type;
    uint16_t version;
    uint32_t sequence;
    uint32_t timestamp_ms;
} vehicle_message_header_t;

typedef struct {
    vehicle_message_header_t header;
    int16_t lateral_error;
    uint16_t confidence;
    uint8_t line_detected;
    uint8_t intersection_detected;
} line_observation_message_t;

typedef struct {
    vehicle_message_header_t header;
    char payload[VEHICLE_BARCODE_PAYLOAD_SIZE];
    uint8_t length;
    uint8_t valid;
} barcode_event_message_t;

typedef struct {
    vehicle_message_header_t header;
    int32_t roll_mdeg;
    int32_t pitch_mdeg;
    int32_t yaw_mdeg;
    int16_t gyro_x;
    int16_t gyro_y;
    int16_t gyro_z;
    uint16_t validity;
    uint16_t terrain_telemetry;
    uint8_t shock_detected;
    uint8_t tilt_detected;
} imu_estimate_message_t;

typedef struct {
    vehicle_message_header_t header;
    /* Minimum reported range, or 0x0fff when all measurements report none. */
    uint16_t distance_mm;
    uint8_t obstacle_class;
    uint8_t emergency;
    uint16_t target_distance_mm;
    uint16_t width_mm;
    uint16_t left_mm;
    uint16_t right_mm;
    uint16_t lateral_mm;
    uint16_t advance_mm;
    uint8_t advisory_action;
} obstacle_event_message_t;

typedef struct {
    vehicle_message_header_t header;
    uint8_t command;
} obstacle_command_message_t;

typedef struct {
    vehicle_message_header_t header;
    int32_t left_count;
    int32_t right_count;
    int32_t distance_mm;
    uint8_t valid;
} wheel_odometry_message_t;

typedef struct {
    vehicle_message_header_t header;
    int16_t linear_velocity;
    int16_t angular_velocity;
    uint16_t duration_ms;
    uint8_t mode;
    uint8_t stop_reason;
} motion_command_message_t;

typedef struct {
    vehicle_message_header_t header;
    /* Signed commanded motor PWM, not measured motion or proof of a stop. */
    int16_t motor_1_pwm;
    int16_t motor_2_pwm;
    /* active is accepted command state; ARM acceptance is not reported. */
    uint8_t active;
    uint8_t faulted;
    uint16_t fault_code;
} motion_status_message_t;

typedef struct {
    vehicle_message_header_t header;
    uint8_t command;
    int32_t value;
    char argument[VEHICLE_REMOTE_ARGUMENT_SIZE];
} remote_command_message_t;

typedef struct {
    vehicle_message_header_t header;
    uint8_t connected;
    uint8_t mqtt_ready;
    uint8_t retry_count;
    uint8_t error_code;
} network_status_message_t;

typedef struct {
    vehicle_message_header_t header;
    uint8_t event_type;
    uint8_t priority;
    uint16_t payload_length;
    char payload[VEHICLE_TELEMETRY_PAYLOAD_SIZE];
} telemetry_message_t;

typedef struct {
    vehicle_message_header_t header;
    uint8_t source;
    uint8_t recoverable;
    uint16_t code;
} fault_message_t;

/*
 * These limits describe the initial queue contract. They are not ÂµT-Kernel
 * object IDs; the task startup module will create the actual message buffers.
 */
#define VEHICLE_MB_LINE_CAPACITY       4U
#define VEHICLE_MB_BARCODE_CAPACITY    8U

#define VEHICLE_MB_ODOMETRY_CAPACITY   2U
#define VEHICLE_MB_MOTION_CAPACITY     2U
#define VEHICLE_MB_STATUS_CAPACITY     2U
#define VEHICLE_MB_REMOTE_CAPACITY     4U
#define VEHICLE_MB_NETWORK_CAPACITY    2U
#define VEHICLE_MB_TELEMETRY_CAPACITY  8U
#define VEHICLE_MB_FAULT_CAPACITY      4U

#endif

################################################################################
# micro T-Kernel 3.0 BSP  makefile
################################################################################

# app_program/*.c plus one level of subfolders (one per subsystem)
TEMP_SRCS = $(wildcard ../app_program/*.c) $(wildcard ../app_program/*/*.c)
TEMP_OBJS = $(TEMP_SRCS:.c=.o)
TEMP_DEPS = $(TEMP_SRCS:.c=.d)

OBJS += $(subst ../, ./mtkernel_3/, $(TEMP_OBJS))
C_DEPS += $(subst ../, ./mtkernel_3/, $(TEMP_DEPS))

mtkernel_3/app_program/%.o: ../app_program/%.c
	@echo 'Building file: $<'
	@mkdir -p $(dir $@)
	$(GCC) $(CFLAGS) -D$(TARGET) $(INCPATH) -MF"$(@:%.o=%.d)" -MT"$(@)" -c -o "$@" "$<"
	@echo 'Finished building: $<'
	@echo ' '

=== C:\Users\Yong Jun\school\INF2004-Embedded_Systems\project\INF2004_Project\build_make\pico_rp2040.mk ===
################################################################################
# micro T-Kernel 3.0 BSP makefile
#     Target Board: Raspberry Pi Pico
################################################################################

################################################################################
# Execution model
#
#   SMP=1  (default)  dual core.  TK_MAX_CORE is 2.
#   SMP=0             single core.  TK_MAX_CORE is 1, the per-core arrays
#                     collapse and the current-core index folds to a constant
#                     zero, so the generated code is equivalent to the
#                     pre-SMP port.
#
# Passed to the assembler as well: the dispatcher indexes the per-core arrays
# by SIO CPUID and needs the same profile.
################################################################################

SMP ?= 1

ifneq ($(SMP),0)
ifneq ($(SMP),1)
$(error Unknown SMP '$(SMP)'; use 0 or 1)
endif
endif

# Console default is needed here because the image name includes it.
#
# Defaults to usb_cdc so the console appears on the Pico's own USB cable.
# usb_cdc still mirrors every line to UART0 (GP0 TX / GP1 RX) for early boot
# and panic messages, so GP0/GP1 stay reserved either way.  CONSOLE=uart drops
# the USB console and its TinyUSB dependency.
CONSOLE ?= usb_cdc

# Pico W radio.  Defaults to cyw43: the radio is powered up and owned by a
# core-0 polling service task, using the Pico SDK's cyw43 driver.  WIFI=none
# leaves the radio powered down.
WIFI ?= cyw43
ifneq ($(filter $(WIFI),none cyw43),$(WIFI))
$(error Unknown WIFI '$(WIFI)'; use none or cyw43)
endif

ifeq ($(WIFI),cyw43)
WIFI_SUFFIX := _wifi
# SMP=0 is supported: the radio service task simply drops its TP_PRC1 affinity
# request when TK_SUPPORT_SMP is off (see cyw43_utk_kernel.c), the same way the
# USB console task already did. Everything touching the radio and lwIP still
# runs on that one task, which is what docs/RELEASE.md safety rule 6 actually
# requires -- single-core satisfies it trivially.
endif

# Optional lwIP network stack on top of WIFI=cyw43. NO_SYS=1 (raw API, no
# tcpip thread): everything that calls into lwIP -- cyw43_poll(),
# sys_check_timeouts(), and any application tcp_*/udp_*/dns_* call -- runs
# on the same processor-1-owned task that already services the radio, per
# docs/RELEASE.md safety rule 6. See lib/liblwip/sysdepend/pico_rp2040/.
NET ?= none
ifneq ($(filter $(NET),none lwip),$(NET))
$(error Unknown NET '$(NET)'; use none or lwip)
endif
ifeq ($(NET),lwip)
ifneq ($(WIFI),cyw43)
$(error NET=lwip requires WIFI=cyw43)
endif
NET_SUFFIX := _lwip
endif

# Optional MQTT client on top of NET=lwip. Connects and publishes from the
# same processor-1-owned task as everything else above -- see
# lib/liblwip/sysdepend/pico_rp2040/mqtt_utk.c. Broker address and topic
# come from config/wifi_secrets.h (gitignored; copy from
# config/wifi_secrets.example.h), same as the WiFi credentials.
MQTT ?= 0
ifneq ($(filter $(MQTT),0 1),$(MQTT))
$(error Unknown MQTT '$(MQTT)'; use 0 or 1)
endif
ifeq ($(MQTT),1)
ifneq ($(NET),lwip)
$(error MQTT=1 requires NET=lwip)
endif
MQTT_SUFFIX := _mqtt
endif

# TEST=1 builds the subsystem self-tests into the image (APP_TEST=1 in C).
# The image gets a _test suffix so it cannot be mistaken for the normal one.
TEST ?= 0
ifneq ($(filter $(TEST),0 1),$(TEST))
$(error Unknown TEST '$(TEST)'; use 0 or 1)
endif
ifeq ($(TEST),1)
TEST_SUFFIX := _test
endif

# Template examples are opt-in: subsystem 1 drives the robot's encoder GP19.
EXAMPLES ?= 0
ifneq ($(filter $(EXAMPLES),0 1),$(EXAMPLES))
$(error Unknown EXAMPLES '$(EXAMPLES)'; use 0 or 1)
endif
ifeq ($(EXAMPLES),1)
EXAMPLES_SUFFIX := _examples
endif

# Live encoder bring-up is separate from the unfinished vehicle application.
ODOMETRY_TEST ?= 0
ifneq ($(filter $(ODOMETRY_TEST),0 1),$(ODOMETRY_TEST))
$(error Unknown ODOMETRY_TEST '$(ODOMETRY_TEST)'; use 0 or 1)
endif
ifeq ($(ODOMETRY_TEST),1)
ifneq ($(TEST),1)
$(error ODOMETRY_TEST=1 requires TEST=1)
endif
ifneq ($(EXAMPLES),0)
$(error ODOMETRY_TEST=1 cannot run with EXAMPLES=1: both use GP19)
endif
ODOMETRY_SUFFIX := _odometry
endif

# CRASH_REPORT=1 also copies every console line to UART0 on GP0 (TX) /
# GP1 (RX), 115200 8N1.  UART output is sent immediately, so it still shows
# the last messages and the kernel's crash report after a crash, when the
# USB console can no longer send anything.  It needs a USB-serial adapter on
# GP0/GP1 and takes those two pins.
# CRASH_REPORT=0 (default): console on USB only; the boot code leaves GP0/GP1
# alone, so a subsystem may claim them in TEAM.md.
# CONSOLE=uart always uses UART0 as the console, so it forces CRASH_REPORT=1.
CRASH_REPORT ?= 0
ifneq ($(filter $(CRASH_REPORT),0 1),$(CRASH_REPORT))
$(error Unknown CRASH_REPORT '$(CRASH_REPORT)'; use 0 or 1)
endif
ifeq ($(CONSOLE),uart)
override CRASH_REPORT := 1
endif
ifeq ($(CONSOLE)$(CRASH_REPORT),usb_cdc1)
CRASH_SUFFIX := _crash
endif

#
# Images are named after the profile they were built with.  Every image is
# flashed by hand from a downloads folder, so identically-named artifacts are
# a live hazard: an SMP=1 build was once flashed in place of an SMP=0 one and
# only the banner in the log revealed it.
#
EXE_FILE := mtk3pico_smp$(SMP)_$(CONSOLE)$(WIFI_SUFFIX)$(NET_SUFFIX)$(MQTT_SUFFIX)$(CRASH_SUFFIX)$(EXAMPLES_SUFFIX)$(ODOMETRY_SUFFIX)$(TEST_SUFFIX)

GCC := arm-none-eabi-gcc
AS := arm-none-eabi-gcc
LINK := arm-none-eabi-gcc
LDLIBS += -lm
SIZE := arm-none-eabi-size
E2U := tools/elf2uf2

# Fail early, with a readable message, when the cross compiler is not on PATH.
ifneq ($(MAKECMDGOALS),clean)
ifeq ($(shell command -v $(GCC) 2>/dev/null),)
$(error $(GCC) not found. Add your Arm GNU Toolchain 'bin' folder to PATH, e.g. export PATH="/path/to/arm-gnu-toolchain/bin:$$PATH")
endif
endif

# The USB console and the radio take sources from the Pico C SDK.  Its location
# must come from the environment (export PICO_SDK_PATH=...) or the command line.
ifneq ($(MAKECMDGOALS),clean)
ifneq ($(CONSOLE)$(WIFI),uartnone)
ifeq ($(strip $(PICO_SDK_PATH)),)
$(error PICO_SDK_PATH is not set. export PICO_SDK_PATH=/path/to/pico-sdk, or build with CONSOLE=uart WIFI=none)
endif
ifeq ($(wildcard $(PICO_SDK_PATH)/pico_sdk_init.cmake),)
$(error PICO_SDK_PATH='$(PICO_SDK_PATH)' does not look like a Pico C SDK folder)
endif
endif
endif

# The ELF-to-UF2 converter is a small host program built from tools/elf2uf2
# with the host C++ compiler the first time it is needed.
$(E2U): ../tools/elf2uf2/main.cpp
	@echo 'Building host tool: $@'
	c++ -O2 -std=c++14 -I../tools/elf2uf2 -o $@ $<

$(EXE_FILE).elf: | $(E2U)

CFLAGS := -mcpu=cortex-m0plus -mthumb -ffreestanding\
    -std=gnu11 \
    -O2 -g3 \
    -MMD -MP \
    -mfloat-abi=soft \

ASFLAGS := -mcpu=cortex-m0plus -mthumb -ffreestanding\
    -x assembler-with-cpp \
    -O2 -g3 \
    -MMD -MP \

LFLAGS := -mcpu=cortex-m0plus -mthumb -ffreestanding \
    -nostartfiles \
    -O2 -g3 \
    -mfloat-abi=soft \

LNKFILE := "../etc/linker/pico_rp2040/tkernel_map.ld"

# Applied here, after CFLAGS/ASFLAGS are assigned with ':=', which would
# otherwise discard anything appended above.
ifeq ($(SMP),1)
CFLAGS  += -DCNF_SMP=1
ASFLAGS += -DCNF_SMP=1
endif
CFLAGS  += -DAPP_TEST=$(TEST)
CFLAGS  += -DAPP_EXAMPLES=$(EXAMPLES)
CFLAGS  += -DAPP_ODOMETRY_TEST=$(ODOMETRY_TEST)
CFLAGS  += -DTM_CONSOLE_UART=$(CRASH_REPORT)
ifeq ($(CRASH_REPORT),0)
CFLAGS  += -DBOARD_UART0_FREE=1
endif

################################################################################
# Console selection
#
#   CONSOLE=usb_cdc             USB CDC-ACM console, with the UART0 mirror
#                               retained for early boot and panics.
#   CONSOLE=uart                UART0 only, no TinyUSB dependency.
#
# usb_cdc pulls seven TinyUSB sources and a set of pico-sdk headers from
# PICO_SDK_PATH.  Only headers and those sources are used; the port does not
# link the pico-sdk runtime, and lib/libtm/sysdepend/pico_rp2040/usb/
# usb_sdk_compat.c supplies the few SDK hooks TinyUSB calls, mapped onto
# micro T-Kernel's tk_def_int().
################################################################################

################################################################################
# Stale-object guard
#
# Every profile compiles into the same object tree, so switching profiles
# without cleaning silently relinks the previous profile's objects: SMP=0 and
# SMP=1 built back to back produced byte-identical images. Record the profile
# and discard the objects whenever it changes. Only *.o and *.d are removed:
# build_make/mtkernel_3/ also holds the tracked subdir.mk files, so removing
# the directory wholesale deletes source. Images are named after their
# profile, so both can coexist. This runs at parse time, so it completes
# before any recipe executes.
################################################################################

PROFILE_ID := smp$(SMP)-$(CONSOLE)-wifi$(WIFI)-net$(NET)-mqtt$(MQTT)-crash$(CRASH_REPORT)-examples$(EXAMPLES)-odometry$(ODOMETRY_TEST)-test$(TEST)
PROFILE_FILE := .build_profile

ifneq ($(strip $(shell cat $(PROFILE_FILE) 2>/dev/null)),$(PROFILE_ID))
$(info Build profile is now $(PROFILE_ID); discarding objects built for another profile.)
$(shell find mtkernel_3 -name '*.o' -o -name '*.d' | xargs -r rm -f; \
	  rm -f $(EXE_FILE).elf $(EXE_FILE).map $(EXE_FILE).uf2; \
	  echo $(PROFILE_ID) > $(PROFILE_FILE))
endif


ifeq ($(CONSOLE),usb_cdc)

TINYUSB_PATH := $(PICO_SDK_PATH)/lib/tinyusb

ifeq ($(wildcard $(TINYUSB_PATH)/src/tusb.c),)
$(error TinyUSB not found at $(TINYUSB_PATH). Set PICO_SDK_PATH, or build with CONSOLE=uart)
endif

USBDIR := ../lib/libtm/sysdepend/pico_rp2040/usb

USB_SRCS := usb_console.c usb_descriptors.c usb_sdk_compat.c usb_tinyusb_glue.c
USB_OBJS := $(addprefix ./mtkernel_3/lib/libtm/sysdepend/pico_rp2040/usb/,$(USB_SRCS:.c=.o))

TINYUSB_SRCS := tusb.c \
                common/tusb_fifo.c \
                device/usbd.c \
                device/usbd_control.c \
                class/cdc/cdc_device.c \
                portable/raspberrypi/rp2040/rp2040_usb.c \
                portable/raspberrypi/rp2040/dcd_rp2040.c
TINYUSB_OBJS := $(addprefix ./mtkernel_3/tinyusb/,$(TINYUSB_SRCS:.c=.o))

USB_INCLUDES := -I"$(USBDIR)" \
                -I"$(TINYUSB_PATH)/src" \
                -I"$(PICO_SDK_PATH)/src/common/pico_base_headers/include" \
                -I"$(PICO_SDK_PATH)/src/common/pico_binary_info/include" \
                -I"$(PICO_SDK_PATH)/src/rp2040/pico_platform/include" \
                -I"$(PICO_SDK_PATH)/src/rp2_common/pico_platform_compiler/include" \
                -I"$(PICO_SDK_PATH)/src/rp2_common/pico_platform_sections/include" \
                -I"$(PICO_SDK_PATH)/src/rp2_common/pico_platform_panic/include" \
                -I"$(PICO_SDK_PATH)/src/rp2_common/pico_platform_common/include" \
                -I"$(PICO_SDK_PATH)/src/rp2_common/hardware_base/include" \
                -I"$(PICO_SDK_PATH)/src/rp2_common/hardware_irq/include" \
                -I"$(PICO_SDK_PATH)/src/rp2_common/hardware_resets/include" \
                -I"$(PICO_SDK_PATH)/src/rp2_common/hardware_sync/include" \
                -I"$(PICO_SDK_PATH)/src/rp2_common/hardware_sync_spin_lock/include" \
                -I"$(PICO_SDK_PATH)/src/rp2_common/hardware_timer/include" \
                -I"$(PICO_SDK_PATH)/src/rp2040/hardware_regs/include" \
                -I"$(PICO_SDK_PATH)/src/rp2040/hardware_structs/include"

CFLAGS += -DTM_CONSOLE_USB_CDC=1 -DNDEBUG $(USB_INCLUDES)

OBJS += $(USB_OBJS) $(TINYUSB_OBJS)
C_DEPS += $(USB_OBJS:.o=.d) $(TINYUSB_OBJS:.o=.d)

mtkernel_3/lib/libtm/sysdepend/pico_rp2040/usb/%.o: $(USBDIR)/%.c
	@echo 'Building file: $<'
	@mkdir -p "$(@D)"
	$(GCC) $(CFLAGS) -D$(TARGET) $(INCPATH) -MF"$(@:%.o=%.d)" -MT"$(@)" -c -o "$@" "$<"
	@echo 'Finished building: $<'
	@echo ' '

mtkernel_3/tinyusb/%.o: $(TINYUSB_PATH)/src/%.c
	@echo 'Building TinyUSB file: $<'
	@mkdir -p "$(@D)"
	$(GCC) $(CFLAGS) -D$(TARGET) $(INCPATH) -MF"$(@:%.o=%.d)" -MT"$(@)" -c -o "$@" "$<"
	@echo 'Finished building: $<'
	@echo ' '

else ifneq ($(CONSOLE),uart)
$(error Unknown CONSOLE '$(CONSOLE)'; use usb_cdc or uart)
endif

################################################################################
# CYW43439 radio (polling architecture). NET=lwip below adds lwIP on top,
# still within the same polling model -- see docs/RELEASE.md and
# lib/liblwip/sysdepend/pico_rp2040/.
################################################################################

ifeq ($(WIFI),cyw43)

CYW43_DRIVER_PATH := $(PICO_SDK_PATH)/lib/cyw43-driver
CYW43_PICO_PATH := $(PICO_SDK_PATH)/src/rp2_common/pico_cyw43_driver
WIFIDIR := ../lib/libwifi/sysdepend/pico_rp2040

ifeq ($(wildcard $(CYW43_DRIVER_PATH)/src/cyw43_ll.c),)
$(error CYW43 driver not found below $(PICO_SDK_PATH); initialize the pico-sdk submodules)
endif

WIFI_LOCAL_SRCS := cyw43_utk.c cyw43_utk_kernel.c cyw43_utk_compat.c
WIFI_LOCAL_OBJS := $(addprefix ./mtkernel_3/lib/libwifi/sysdepend/pico_rp2040/,$(WIFI_LOCAL_SRCS:.c=.o))

CYW43_OBJS := ./mtkernel_3/cyw43/cyw43_ll.o \
              ./mtkernel_3/cyw43/cyw43_ctrl.o \
              ./mtkernel_3/cyw43/cyw43_stats.o \
              ./mtkernel_3/cyw43/cyw43_bus_pio_spi.o \
              ./mtkernel_3/cyw43/pio.o \
              ./mtkernel_3/cyw43/dma.o \
              ./mtkernel_3/cyw43/gpio.o

WIFI_INCLUDES := -I"$(WIFIDIR)" \
                 -I"../lib/libtm/sysdepend/pico_rp2040/usb" \
                 -I"$(CYW43_DRIVER_PATH)/src" \
                 -I"$(CYW43_DRIVER_PATH)/firmware" \
                 -I"$(CYW43_PICO_PATH)/include" \
                 -I"$(PICO_SDK_PATH)/src/boards/include" \
                 -I"$(PICO_SDK_PATH)/src/common/pico_base_headers/include" \
                 -I"$(PICO_SDK_PATH)/src/common/hardware_claim/include" \
                 -I"$(PICO_SDK_PATH)/src/common/pico_binary_info/include" \
                 -I"$(PICO_SDK_PATH)/src/common/pico_util/include" \
                 -I"$(PICO_SDK_PATH)/src/rp2040/pico_platform/include" \
                 -I"$(PICO_SDK_PATH)/src/rp2040/hardware_regs/include" \
                 -I"$(PICO_SDK_PATH)/src/rp2040/hardware_structs/include" \
                 -I"$(PICO_SDK_PATH)/src/rp2_common/pico_platform_common/include" \
                 -I"$(PICO_SDK_PATH)/src/rp2_common/pico_platform_compiler/include" \
                 -I"$(PICO_SDK_PATH)/src/rp2_common/pico_platform_sections/include" \
                 -I"$(PICO_SDK_PATH)/src/rp2_common/pico_platform_panic/include" \
                 -I"$(PICO_SDK_PATH)/src/rp2_common/hardware_base/include" \
                 -I"$(PICO_SDK_PATH)/src/rp2_common/hardware_pio/include" \
                 -I"$(PICO_SDK_PATH)/src/rp2_common/hardware_gpio/include" \
                 -I"$(PICO_SDK_PATH)/src/rp2_common/hardware_dma/include" \
                 -I"$(PICO_SDK_PATH)/src/rp2_common/hardware_irq/include" \
                 -I"$(PICO_SDK_PATH)/src/rp2_common/hardware_sync/include" \
                 -I"$(PICO_SDK_PATH)/src/rp2_common/hardware_sync_spin_lock/include" \
                 -I"$(PICO_SDK_PATH)/src/rp2_common/hardware_clocks/include" \
                 -I"$(PICO_SDK_PATH)/src/rp2_common/hardware_resets/include" \
                 -I"$(PICO_SDK_PATH)/src/rp2_common/hardware_timer/include"

CFLAGS += -DTM_WIFI_CYW43=1 -DCYW43_LWIP=$(if $(filter lwip,$(NET)),1,0) -DCYW43_ENABLE_BLUETOOTH=0 \
          -DCYW43_USE_OTP_MAC=1 -DPICO_CYW43_LOGGING_ENABLED=0 \
          -DPICO_RP2040=1 -DPICO_32BIT=1 -DPICO_ON_DEVICE=1 -DPICO_BUILD=1 \
          -DPICO_NO_HARDWARE=0 -DRASPBERRYPI_PICO_W=1 \
          -DCYW43_DEFAULT_PIN_WL_REG_ON=23u \
          -DCYW43_DEFAULT_PIN_WL_DATA_OUT=24u \
          -DCYW43_DEFAULT_PIN_WL_DATA_IN=24u \
          -DCYW43_DEFAULT_PIN_WL_HOST_WAKE=24u \
          -DCYW43_DEFAULT_PIN_WL_CLOCK=29u \
          -DCYW43_DEFAULT_PIN_WL_CS=25u \
          -DNDEBUG -ffunction-sections -fdata-sections $(WIFI_INCLUDES)
LFLAGS += -Wl,--gc-sections

OBJS += $(WIFI_LOCAL_OBJS) $(CYW43_OBJS)
C_DEPS += $(WIFI_LOCAL_OBJS:.o=.d) $(CYW43_OBJS:.o=.d)

mtkernel_3/lib/libwifi/sysdepend/pico_rp2040/%.o: $(WIFIDIR)/%.c
	@echo 'Building Wi-Fi port file: $<'
	@mkdir -p "$(@D)"
	$(GCC) $(CFLAGS) -D$(TARGET) $(INCPATH) -MF"$(@:%.o=%.d)" -MT"$(@)" -c -o "$@" "$<"

mtkernel_3/cyw43/cyw43_ll.o: $(CYW43_DRIVER_PATH)/src/cyw43_ll.c
	@mkdir -p "$(@D)"
	$(GCC) $(CFLAGS) -D$(TARGET) $(INCPATH) -MF"$(@:%.o=%.d)" -MT"$(@)" -c -o "$@" "$<"

mtkernel_3/cyw43/cyw43_ctrl.o: $(CYW43_DRIVER_PATH)/src/cyw43_ctrl.c
	@mkdir -p "$(@D)"
	$(GCC) $(CFLAGS) -D$(TARGET) $(INCPATH) -MF"$(@:%.o=%.d)" -MT"$(@)" -c -o "$@" "$<"

mtkernel_3/cyw43/cyw43_stats.o: $(CYW43_DRIVER_PATH)/src/cyw43_stats.c
	@mkdir -p "$(@D)"
	$(GCC) $(CFLAGS) -D$(TARGET) $(INCPATH) -MF"$(@:%.o=%.d)" -MT"$(@)" -c -o "$@" "$<"

mtkernel_3/cyw43/cyw43_bus_pio_spi.o: $(CYW43_PICO_PATH)/cyw43_bus_pio_spi.c
	@mkdir -p "$(@D)"

mtkernel_3/cyw43/pio.o: $(PICO_SDK_PATH)/src/rp2_common/hardware_pio/pio.c
	@mkdir -p "$(@D)"
	$(GCC) $(CFLAGS) -D$(TARGET) $(INCPATH) -MF"$(@:%.o=%.d)" -MT"$(@)" -c -o "$@" "$<"
